# 1. 致學生（可作為自學用途）

本語法參考書是專為學習基礎英語語法的讀者而編寫。書內共有115個單元，而每一單元均針對不同的語法項目。

本書開首部分的目錄已列明各單元的主題。讀者可先選擇一些合乎所需的單元，而無需依次學習書內的所有單元。例如你對現在完成式（I have been, he has done等等）這方面有困難，便可先學習單元15- 20。

除了可以利用目錄找到各單元的主題外，也可以從（書末的）索引找出所需的單元。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7c8172ae9d58985cf4c8c1498142ca8e0c531c4e4a734a85e18cdbc0cdd3aa6d.jpg)

若你不確定需要研習哪個單元，可使用第273頁至第283頁的學習錦囊，幫助讀者決定需要研習的單元。

每一單元均以對貢形式編排：左頁是精簡淺白的解說部分，並加插中英對照的例句；右頁則載有相關練習，讓讀者即時鞏固所學。主要内容特色如下：

旨在提醒讀者一些語法要説或常犯錯誤。

單元內的標題皆以精體標示，方便讀者掌握內容主旨。

在中文編寫練習指引，方便讀者理解題旨。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ebc4a7dbdcc49fde56057fa6874e2480a4ee9c215c7242efd9d5bd549399c353.jpg)

先細閱左頁的解說部分，然後完成右頁的練習。利用書末提供的答案核對成績。（答案在第284- 308頁）有需要時可重溫左頁的解說部分。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3d1d382396a64150a27511f0f35d4d205ff2fc0366b7781645dbde2e9041d245.jpg)

書末尚有7個附錄（第245- 253頁），講解主動語態、被動語態、不規則動詞、縮約式、拼寫和片語動詞。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d6faba704d5f21b9715b500e14c32e24388a7c1e5b174e42fa30184aa139bdfd.jpg)

書末提供的附加練習（第254- 272頁）新增了題目，而有關練習的主題則詳列於第254頁。附加練習答案在第309至311頁。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a517cef29dd3b34ccc41e8b66614e038f1957abbde6506d066ac8936367b4f67.jpg)

書末新附一張自學光碟，為每個單元提供大量額外的練習題。光碟內還有逾600道測試題，可作為自我測試之用。此外，光碟更有內置詞典，可即時提供詞語的解釋。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7a2d8af577eb4c1d617c449d0b44d9e1296084946eb58880022721ffb3a30873.jpg)

# 2. 致教師

# 本書特色

本書特色- 本書為語法參考書,內容集中講授英語語法的不同項目。- 本書是專為學習基礎英語的讀者而編寫,內容涵蓋在基礎程度上普遍教授的語法項目。- 本書是一本附有練習的參考書,按照語法分類項目來編排。- 本書可作為課堂或自學之用。

# 结構编排

結構編排本書共有115個單元,每一單元針對講解一個語法項目。單元內容是根據語法的類別而編寫,例如時態、疑問句和冠詞等等。各單元並非依照內容的深淺程度而編排,因此讀者應按自己的需要和進度來選擇合適的單元進行學習,無需由開首順序地學習。書內的目錄列明了各單元的主題,書末更設有分別以字母及筆畫排列的索引,方便讀者找尋所需單元。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c46a138c7c060c08d9c6c21273fe822f6b77f24c4eb5c5e8627bce2458701158.jpg)

# 每一單元均以對貢形式编排

每一單元均以對貢形式編排——左頁是語法的解說部分,右頁則為相應的練習。此外,書末還特設7個附錄(第245- 253頁),講解主動語態、被動語態、不規則動詞、縮約式、拼寫和片語動詞,教師可利用這些資源教授學生一些重要的語法項目,以加深他們的印象。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9144e3fb711bae789352407677b4bc003c3dc276a39c3409d14272f6c03e14e3.jpg)

書未還提供附加練習（第254- 272頁），內容綜合了不同單元（尤其是有關動詞形式的單元）的語法重點。此部分共包含35個練習,而練習的主題內容則詳列於第254頁內。

此外,書末新增的學習錦囊有助讀者決定先研習哪一個單元——參看第273頁。而書末的單元練習答案（第284- 308頁）及附加練習答案（第309- 311頁）則可供學生自行核對成績。

書未附有詳盡的中、英雙語索引。英文索引（第314- 318頁）以英文字母順序編排,有助讀者快捷地找出所需的語法項目及相關單元。而中文索引（第319- 320頁）則以語法項目的筆畫編排,方便讀者以語法項目的中譯名稱搜尋所需資料。

# 涵蓋程度

本書是為學習基英語語法的讀者而寫，對象為對英語語法有初步識的人士，也可供剛達到英語中級程度，而語法能力稍，或對個别基英語語法项目感到困難的學生使用。

由於本書是以學習基英語語法的人士為對象，所以例句和解的用語一般较为白、精簡。例句和練習中所用的词是根據讀者的程度而精挑細選的。

# 使用明

本書既適合英語自學者使用（見第vii真「致學生（可作為自學用途）」），又可作為課堂上的補充習之用。無是在哪種情况下，本書都是必不可缺的基法参考書籍。

作為課堂上的教材時，本書可用作即時強化、複習重溫或改善語法運用能力。教師可於課堂上使用或個别需要额外助及習的學生使用。

在某些情况下，左真的解部分可於課堂上使用，但须留意它們主要是為自學及参考用途而寫，故在大多數情况下，教師應按其為適當的方式教授語法重點，而書内的習可作為學生的家課。這樣，左真的解部分便可作為學生課後自行参考之用。

部分教師或會為本書適合作為複習及改善語法之用。在這樣的情况下，教師可引導個别學生或學習小選合適的單元作自修及習之用。

# 自學光碟

自學光碟為每個單元提供大量额外習题。光碟還含逾600條測試题，供使用者寫測驗試卷，作為自我測試之用。此外，光碟更有内置典，方便查問語的解。

# Essential Grammar in Use《英语語法精解》第三版

第三版與第二版的分别是：

第三版與第二版的分別是：重新設計，采用全新的彩色插圖。新增了一個單元（單元35），並進行重新編排，令大部分單元的編號和上一版次不同。對不少說明文字、例句和練習題進行了修訂。附加練習（第254- 272頁）新增了題目。新增書末的學習錦囊，以幫助讀者決定研習哪一個單元。新附一張自學光碟，提供更多練習及內置典。

# 否定句

<table><tr><td>I</td><td>am</td><td>(I&#x27;m)</td></tr><tr><td>he</td><td>is</td><td>(he&#x27;s)</td></tr><tr><td>she</td><td>it</td><td>(she&#x27;s)</td></tr><tr><td>we</td><td>you</td><td>(we&#x27;re)</td></tr><tr><td>they</td><td>are</td><td>(you&#x27;re)</td></tr><tr><td></td><td></td><td>(they&#x27;re)</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>I</td><td>am not</td><td>(I&#x27;m not)</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>he</td><td>is not</td><td>(he&#x27;s not or she&#x27;s not or it&#x27;s not)</td><td>he isn&#x27;t)</td></tr><tr><td>she</td><td>it</td><td>(it&#x27;s not or it is not)</td><td>she isn&#x27;t)</td></tr><tr><td>we</td><td>are not</td><td>(we&#x27;re not or you&#x27;re not or they&#x27;re not)</td><td>we aren&#x27;t)</td></tr><tr><td>you</td><td>they</td><td>(you&#x27;re not or they aren&#x27;t)</td><td>you aren&#x27;t)</td></tr></table>

# 缩约式

I'm cold. Can you close the window, please? 我觉得冷, 麻烦你可否把窗上?

I'm 32 years old. My sister is 29. 我 32 威, 我的妹妹 29 威。

Steve is ill. He's in bed. 史蒂夫病了。他在林上。

My brother is afraid of dogs. 我的弟弟怕狗。

It's ten o'clock. You're late again. 现在已是十时, 你又遇到了。

Ann and I are good friends. 安跟我是好朋友。

Your keys are on the table. 你的钥匙在桌上。

I'm tired, but I'm not hungry. 我雎然疲倦, 但並不覺得餓。

Tom isn't interested in politics. He's interested in music. 湯姆對政治不感興趣。他對音樂有興趣。

Jane isn't a teacher. She's a student. 珍不是教師, 她是學生。

Those people aren't English. They're Australian. 那些人並不是英國人, 是澳洲人。

It's sunny today, but it isn't warm. 今天陽光普照, 但天氣並不和暖。

that's = that is there's = there is here's = here is

Thank you. That's very kind of you. 謝謝你, 你的人品非常好。

Look! There's Chris. 看! 克里斯在那裏。

Here's your key. 'Thank you.'

Here's your key. 'Thank you.'

「這是你的匙。」「謝謝你。」

# am/is/are 疑問句 (questions)

肯定句

疑問句

<table><tr><td>I</td><td>am</td></tr><tr><td>he</td><td>is</td></tr><tr><td>she</td><td>is</td></tr><tr><td>it</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>we</td><td>are</td></tr><tr><td>you</td><td>are</td></tr><tr><td>they</td><td></td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>am</td><td>I?</td></tr><tr><td>is</td><td>he?</td></tr><tr><td>is</td><td>she?</td></tr><tr><td>they?</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>are</td><td>we?</td></tr><tr><td>you?</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>they?</td><td></td></tr></table>

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e3e2f16d3e0a27d60c440dfbf1e80eb7b7ebd7b9aac5f4c56a726fd8d9e1acda.jpg)

'Am I late?' 'No, you're on time.' 「我是否遇到了？」「不，你准时到。」

'Is your mother at home?' 'No, she's out.' 「你的媽媽是否在家？」「不，她外出了。」

'Are your parents at home?' 'No, they're out.' 「你的父母在家嗎？」「不，他們外出了。」

'Is it cold in your room?' 'Yes, a little.' 「你的房間冷嗎？」「是的，有點冷。」

'Your shoes are nice. Are they new?' 你的鞋子很好看，是不是新買的？

應該說：

Is she at home? / Is your mother at home? (不是 Is at home your mother?)

Are they new? / Are your shoes new? (不是 Are new your shoes?)

Where ...? / What ...? / Who ...? / How ...? / Why ...?

Where is your mother? Is she at home? 你的媽媽在哪裏？她在家嗎？

'Where are you from?' 'Canada.' 「你從哪裏來？」「加拿大。」

'What colour is your car?' 'It's red.' 「你的車是甚麼顏色的呢？」「是紅色的。」

'How old is Joe?' 'He's 24.' 「喬今年有多大？」「24歲。」

How are your parents? Are they well? 你的父母好嗎？身體可好？

These postcards are nice. How much are they? 這些明信片很美。它們要多少錢？

This hotel isn't very good. Why is it so expensive? 這家酒店不很好。為甚麼它收費這麼昂貴？

what's = what is who's = who is how's = how is where's = where is

What's the time? 現在是甚麼時間？

Where's Lucy? 露西在哪裏？

Who's that man? 那人是誰？

How's your father? 你的爸爸近況如何？

簡短回答

<table><tr><td rowspan="3">Yes,</td><td>I</td><td>am.</td><td rowspan="3">No,</td><td>I&#x27;m</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>is.</td><td>he&#x27;s she&#x27;s it&#x27;s not.</td></tr><tr><td>we you they</td><td>are.</td><td>we&#x27;re you&#x27;re they&#x27;re</td></tr></table>

That's my seat.

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">或</td><td rowspan="2">No,</td><td>he she it</td><td>isn&#x27;t.</td></tr><tr><td>we you they</td><td>aren&#x27;t.</td></tr></table>

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/47bf28d90ab14173a9a3969c62dee8f1d56d1d18ba20cf69b5b83845bb750958.jpg)

'Are you tired?' 'Yes, I am.' 「你感到疲倦嗎？」「是的，我覺得倦。」

'Are you hungry?' 'No, I'm not, but I'm thirsty.' 「你覺得餓嗎？」「我不餓，但我覺得口渴。」

'Is your friend English?' 'Yes, he is.' 「你的朋友是英國人嗎？」「對，他是英國人。」

'Are these your keys?' 'Yes, they are.' 「這些是你的鑰匙嗎？」「是。」

'That's my seat.' 'No, it isn't.' 「那是我的座位。」「不，那不是你的座位。」

# I am doing 3 現在進行式(present continuous)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1457be6fdd1bcadb706eac0148a74a44c5aba6f98f61a294ac82a13a98045e77.jpg)

She's eating. 她正在進餐。 She isn't reading. 她不是在閱讀。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ffc81d3dd8271305cdaf076e5b2bd9dcf2f8621c816dc7b73eacc4bdfdb4e0e2.jpg)

It's raining. 現在正下雨。 The sun isn't shining. 太陽不是在照耀着。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6988da1b0b4f6bee62a1b4e212cb16fa2ddde3590ca67f2fdb103143353e5361.jpg)

They're running. 他們正在跑步。 They aren't walking. 他們不是在步行。

# 現在進行式

am / is / are + doing / eating / running / writing 等等。

<table><tr><td>I</td><td>am (not)</td></tr><tr><td>he</td><td>is (not)</td></tr><tr><td>she</td><td rowspan="2">-ing</td></tr><tr><td>it</td></tr><tr><td>we</td><td>are (not)</td></tr><tr><td>you</td><td rowspan="2">are (not)</td></tr><tr><td>they</td></tr></table>

I'm working. I'm not watching TV. 我正在工作。我不是在看電視。

Maria is reading a newspaper. 瑪麗亞正在看報紙。

She isn't eating. (或 She's not eating.) 她並不是在吃東西。

The phone is ringing. 電話正在響着。

We're having dinner. 我們正在吃晚餐。

You're not listening to me. (或 You aren't listening...) 你不是在聽我說話。

The children are doing their homework. 孩子們正在做家課。

am / is / are + - ing = 此刻正在發生的事情

I'm working

she's wearing a hat

they're playing football

I'm not watching television

過去

現在

將來

Please be quiet. I'm working. (= I'm working now) 請保持肅靜,我正在工作。

Look, there's Sarah. She's wearing a brown coat. (= she is wearing it now)

看,薩拉在那裏。她穿了一件棕色的外套。

The weather is nice. It's not raining. 現在天氣很好,沒有下雨。

'Where are the children?' 'They're playing in the park.' [孩子們在哪裏?」「他們正在公園裏玩耍。」

(on the phone) We're having dinner now. Can I phone you later?

(在通電話)我們正在吃晚飯,可不可以跑些時候再打電話給你?

You can turn off the television. I'm not watching it. 你可以關掉電視機,我並不是在看電視。

拼寫方法: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄 5

come → coming write → writing dance → dancing

run → running sit → sitting swim → swimming

lie → lying

is / are + 主語 + - ing

'Are you going now?' 'Yes, I am.' 「你現在要走了嗎？」「是的，我要走了。」

# I do / work / like 等等 5 簡單現在式(present simple)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5cd37229f9feae5a4b1ab9890d995ce24c9b031ca047bb86ee0863d111c1deea.jpg)

They're looking at their books. 他們正看着書。 They read a lot. 他們讀很多書。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1e0db8c287efd642dcf3cc9a2ac684f121f5c2a216ec7164e6e6466e0c18a40a.jpg)

He's eating an ice- cream. 他正在吃冰淇淋。 He likes ice- cream. 他喜歡冰淇淋。

They read / he likes / I work 等等 = 簡單現在式:

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they</td><td>read</td><td>like</td><td>work</td><td>live</td><td>watch</td><td>do</td><td>have</td></tr><tr><td>he / she / it</td><td>reads</td><td>likes</td><td>works</td><td>lives</td><td>watches</td><td>does</td><td>has</td></tr></table>

# 注意

he works / she lives / it rains 等等

I work in a shop. My brother works in a bank. (不是 My brother work)

我在一家商店裏工作。我的哥哥在銀行裏工作。

Lucy lives in London. Her parents live in Scotland. 露西住在倫敦。她的父母住在蘇格蘭。

It rains a lot in winter. 冬天雨水很多。

I have  $\rightarrow$  he / she / it has:

John has a shower every day. 約翰每天都洗澡。

拼寫方法: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄5

- es 加在 
-s / 
-sh / 
-ch 之後: pass  $\rightarrow$  passes finish  $\rightarrow$  finishes watch  $\rightarrow$  watches  $\rightarrow$  -y  $\rightarrow$  -ies: study  $\rightarrow$  studies try  $\rightarrow$  tries 還有: do  $\rightarrow$  does go  $\rightarrow$  goes

# 簡單現在式用於表達常理、有時發生或經常發生的事情:

I like big cities. 我喜歡大城市。

Your English is good. You speak very well. 你的英語很好。你説得很流利。

Tim works very hard. He starts at 7:30 and finishes at 8 o'clock in the evening.

蒂姆很勤力工作。他清晨上時半便上班,到晚上八時才下班。

The earth goes round the sun. 地球繞着大陽運運行。

We do a lot of different things in our free time. 我們在空間時做許多不同的事情。

It costs a lot of money to build a hospital. 建造醫院要花很多錢。

always / never / often / usually / sometimes + 簡單現在式:

Sue always gets to work early. (不是 Sue gets always) 蘇總是很早上班。

I never eat breakfast. (不是 I eat never) 我從不吃早餐。

We often go away at weekends. 我們常常在週末出外。

Mark usually plays football on Sundays. 馬克通常在星期日踢足球。

I sometimes walk to work, but not very often. 我有時步行上班,但不是經常如此。

# 檢索

I don't... (否定句)  $\Rightarrow$  單元6 Do you...? (疑问句)  $\Rightarrow$  單元7 I am doing 和 I do  $\Rightarrow$  單元8 always / usually / often 等等 (詞序)  $\Rightarrow$  單元 94

# I don't ... 簡單現在式否定句 (present simple negative)

簡單現在式否定句的句式為don't／doesn't + 動詞：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/51c299db02b5953599d13eba79a305140d6f430ef08956f717ceca2d3c116aef.jpg)

She doesn't drink coffee. 她不喝咖啡。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4ce82c0050a0eaf572964e27af070a8846e3d282d05a4ffa7d9ef7fe7796b120.jpg)

He doesn't like his job. 他不喜歡自己的工作。

# 肯定句

# 否定句

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>work like do have</td><td>I we you they</td><td>don&#x27;t (do not)</td><td>work like do have</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>works likes does has</td><td></td><td>he she it</td><td>doesn&#x27;t (does not)</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>don&#x27;t (do not)</td><td rowspan="2">work like do have</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>doesn&#x27;t (does not)</td></tr></table>

I drink coffee, but I don't drink tea. 我喝咖啡, 但不喝茶。

Sue drinks tea, but she doesn't drink coffee. 蘇喝茶, 但她不喝咖啡。

You don't work very hard. 你工作得不夠努力。

We don't watch television very often. 我們不常看電視。

The weather is usually nice. It doesn't rain very often. 天氣通常很好, 並不當下雨。

Gary and Nicole don't know many people. 加里和尼科爾認識的人不多。

緊記don't和doesn't的用法：

# 注意

I / we / you / they don't... he / she / it doesn't...

I don't like football. 我不喜歡足球。

He doesn't like football. 他不喜歡足球。

I don't like Fred and Fred doesn't like me. (不是 Fred don't like) 我不喜歡弗雷德, 弗雷德也不喜歡我。

My car doesn't use much petrol. (不是 My car don't use) 我的汽車耗油量不多。

Sometimes he is late, but it doesn't happen very often. 他有時會遲到, 但這情況並非經常發生。

don't / doesn't + 不定式 (例如 don't like / doesn't speak / doesn't do 等等)：

I don't like washing the car. I don't do it very often. 我不喜歡洗車, 所以我不是經常洗車。

Sarah speaks Spanish, but she doesn't speak Italian. (不是 doesn't speaks)

薩拉說西班牙語,但她不會說意大利語。

Bill doesn't do his job very well. (不是 Bill doesn't his job) 比爾工作得不夠好。

Paula doesn't usually have breakfast. (不是 doesn't ... has) 葉拉通常不吃早餐。

# Do you ...? 簡單現在式疑問句(present simple questions)

在簡單現在式疑問句中,應該用do/does:

肯定句

疑問句

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>work like do have</td><td>do</td><td>I we you they</td><td rowspan="2">work? like? do? have?</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>works likes does has</td><td>does</td><td>he she it</td></tr></table>

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/05c76d907e042300e7e93ec94a4605f56e38e5182840bf3fb1e6cc8dabc1ae6d.jpg)

词序

do/does+主語+不定式

<table><tr><td rowspan="3"></td><td>Do</td><td>you</td><td>work</td><td>on Sundays?</td></tr><tr><td>Do</td><td>your friends</td><td>live</td><td>near here?</td></tr><tr><td>Does</td><td>Chris</td><td>play</td><td>tennis?</td></tr><tr><td>Where</td><td>do</td><td>your parents</td><td>live?</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>How often</td><td>do</td><td>you</td><td>wash</td><td>your hair?</td></tr><tr><td>What</td><td>does</td><td>this word</td><td>mean?</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>How much</td><td>does</td><td>it</td><td>cost</td><td>to fly to Rome?</td></tr></table>

疑問句中包含always/usually/often:

<table><tr><td rowspan="3">What</td><td>Do</td><td>you</td><td>always</td><td>have</td><td>breakfast?</td></tr><tr><td>Does</td><td>Chris</td><td>often</td><td>phone</td><td>you?</td></tr><tr><td>do</td><td>you</td><td>usually</td><td>do</td><td>at weekends?</td></tr></table>

What do you do?  $\mathbf{\Sigma} =$  What's your job?你做甚磨工作？

What do you do?'I work in a bank.'「你做甚磨工作？」「我在一間銀行裹工作。」

繁記do和does的用法:

注意

do I/we/you/they does he/she/it...

Do they like music?他們喜歡音樂嗎? Does he like music?他們喜歡音樂嗎?

D 簡短回答

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">Yes,</td><td>I / we / you / they do.</td></tr><tr><td>he / she / it does.</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">No,</td><td>I / we / you / they don&#x27;t.</td></tr><tr><td>he / she / it doesn&#x27;t.</td></tr></table>

Do you play tennis?'No,I don't.'「你會打球？」「我不會。」

Do your parents speak English?'Yes,they do.'「你的父母會説英语？」「他們會説英语。」

Does Gary work hard?'Yes,he does.'「加里工作勤力？」「他很勤力。」

Does your sister live in London?'No,she doesn't.'「你的妹妹住在倫敦？」「不是。」

# I am doing 和 I do

現在進行式(present continuous)和簡單現在式(present simple)

現在進行式和簡單現在式的分别：

Jack is watching television. 傑克正在看電視。 He is not playing the guitar. 他不是在彈吉他。

But Jack has a guitar. 但像克有一支吉他。

He often plays it and he plays very well. 他經常彈奏,並且彈得很好。

Jack plays the guitar, but he is not playing the guitar now. 傑克會彈吉他,但他現在沒有彈。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/88541e7becc3adcad6816793715c0b7df0e10dff1f6f528c42e46bb2d0172127.jpg)

Is he playing the guitar? No, he isn't. (現在進行式)他在彈吉他嗎?不是。 Does he play the guitar? Yes, he does. (簡單現在式)他會彈吉他嗎?他會。

現在進行式(Iamdoing)  $=$  此時此刻,正在話的時間:

# I'm doing

過去

現在

將來

Please be quiet.I'm working.(不是I work)請保持蕭靜,我正在工作。

Tom is having a shower at the moment.(不是Tom has)湯姆正在洗澡。

Take an umbrella with you.It's raining. 現正在下雨,外出時拿一把雨傘去吧。

You can turn off the television.I'm not watching it.你可以關掉電視機,我並不是在看電視。

Why are you under the table? What are you doing? 你為甚麼在桌子下?你在做甚麼?

簡單現在式(I do)  $=$  一般情況、經常發生或有時發生的事情：

I do

過去

現在

將來

I work every day from 9 o'clock to 5:30. 我每天由早上9時工作至下午5時30分。

Tom has a shower every morning. 湯姆每天早上都洗澡。

It rains a lot in winter. 冬天雨水很多。

I don't watch television very often. 我並不經常看電視。

What do you usually do at the weekend? 過末時你經常做甚麼?

下列動詞不用現在進行式(I am - ing):

<table><tr><td>like</td><td>love</td><td>want</td><td>know</td><td>understand</td><td>remember</td><td>depend</td></tr><tr><td>prefer</td><td>hate</td><td>need</td><td>mean</td><td>believe</td><td>forget</td><td></td></tr></table>

注意

這些動詞只能以簡單現在式來表達(例如I want / do you like?等等):

I'm tired.I want to go home.(不是I'm wanting)我感到疲倦,想回家去。

Do you know that girl?' 'Yes, but I don't remember her name.

「你識那女孩嗎？」「識,但我記不起她的名字。」

I don't understand.What do you mean?我不明白,你的意思是甚麼?

# I have ... 和 I've got ...

可以用 I have 或 I've got, he has 或 he's got:

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>have</td><td>或</td><td>I we you they</td><td>have got</td><td>(I&#x27;ve got) (we&#x27;ve got) (you&#x27;ve got) (they&#x27;ve got)</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>has</td><td>或</td><td>he she it</td><td>has got</td><td>(he&#x27;s got) (she&#x27;s got) (it&#x27;s got)</td></tr></table>

缩约式

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d596b53a9b247c51256afdfb7d26cf1a890715c8128071c11b45e25b27ce2df0.jpg)

I have blue eyes. 或 I've got blue eyes. 我有一雙藍眼睛。

Tim has two sisters. 或 Tim has got two sisters. 蒂姆有兩個姊姊。

Our car has four doors. 或 Our car has got four doors. 我們的車子是四門的。

Sarah isn't feeling well. She has a headache. 或 She's got a headache. 薩拉感到不適,她頭痛了。

They like animals. They have a horse, three dogs and six cats. 或 They've got a horse...

他們喜歡動物,共養了一匹馬、三隻狗和六隻貓。

I haven't got / have you got? 等等

否定句

疑问句

简短回答

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>have not (haven&#x27;t)</td><td rowspan="2">got</td><td rowspan="2">got?</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>has not (hasn&#x27;t)</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>have</td><td>I we you they</td><td rowspan="2">got?</td></tr><tr><td>has</td><td>he she it</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>Yes, No,</td><td>I we you they</td><td rowspan="2">has. hasn&#x27;t.</td></tr><tr><td>Yes, No,</td><td>he she it</td></tr></table>

I've got a motorbike, but I haven't got a car. 我有一輛摩托車,但沒有汽車。

Tracey and Jeff haven't got any children. 特蕾西和傑夫沒有子女。

It's a nice house, but it hasn't got a garden. 这是一所很好的房子,可惜沒有花園。

'Have you got a camera?' 'No, I haven't?' 「你有沒有照相機？」「沒有。」

'What have you got in your bag?' 'Nothing. It's empty.'

「你的袋裏有些甚麼？」「甚麼都沒有,是空的。」

'Has Helen got a car?' 'Yes, she has.' 「海倫有沒有汽車？」「有。」

What kind of car has she got? 她擁有甚麼類型的汽車？

I don't have / do you have? 等等

在否定句和疑問句中也可以用 do/does...

They don't have any children. (= They haven't got any children.) 他們沒有子女。

It's a nice house, but it doesn't have a garden. (= it hasn't got a garden) 這是一所很好的房子,可惜沒有花園。

Does Helen have a car? (= Has Helen got a car?) 海倫有沒有汽車？

What do you have in your bag? (= What have you got in your bag?) 你的袋裏有些甚麼？

am/is(現在式)→was(過去式):

Iam tired.(表示現在) 我覺得疲倦。

Where is Kate?(表示現在) 凱特在哪裹?

The weather is good today. 今天天氣很好。

are(現在式)→were(過去式):

You are late.(表示現在) 你遇到了。

They aren't here.(表示現在) 他們不在這裏。

I was tired last night. 昨晚我覺得疲倦。 Where was Kate yesterday? 凱特昨天在哪裹? The weather was good last week. 上星期天氣很好。

You were late yesterday. 昨天你遇到了。

They weren't here last Sunday.

上星期日他們不在這裹

Was the weather nice when you were on holiday? 你度假時天氣可好?

# 簡短回答

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">Yes,</td><td>I / he / she / it was.</td></tr><tr><td>we / you / they were.</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">No,</td><td>I / he / she / it wasn&#x27;t.</td></tr><tr><td>we / you / they weren&#x27;t.</td></tr></table>

'Were you late?' 'No, I wasn't.' 「你是否遲到了？」「沒有。」

'Was Ted at work yesterday?' 'Yes, he was.' 「泰德昨天有沒有上班？」「有的。」

'Were Sue and Steve at the party?' 'No, they weren't.'

「蘇和史蒂夫是否參加了派對？」「沒有。」

# 王元 11

# worked / got / went 等等 簡單過去式(pastsimple)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/24e3c0a486a02f890912a8b1c0ba8fb8843adbd2ebf63c6bf2c3a8d5c009cbe8.jpg)

watched是簡單過去式：

I/we/you/they watched he/she/it

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/814e2d1cd94d839842ad02c1641d9fa3bb4ad7d666b474ef5e289af54001a45b.jpg)

簡單過去式通常是在動词後加上- ed（规则動词）。例如：

work  $\rightarrow$  worked dance  $\rightarrow$  danced clean  $\rightarrow$  cleaned stay  $\rightarrow$  stayed start  $\rightarrow$  started need  $\rightarrow$  needed

I clean my teeth every morning. This morning I cleaned my teeth. 我每天早上都刷牙。我今早刷了牙。 Terry worked in a bank from 1996 to 2003. 泰利由1996年至2003年都在一周銀行裏工作。 Yesterday it ruined all morning. It stopped at lunchtime. 昨天整個早上都下着雨。現在午飯時間停了。 We enjoyed the party last night. We danced a lot and talked to a lot of people. The party finished at midnight. 昨晚的派對我們玩得很高興。我們跳舞跳了很久，又和很多人談天。派對在午夜時結束。

拼寫方法：檢索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄5

try  $\rightarrow$  tried study  $\rightarrow$  studied copy  $\rightarrow$  copied stop  $\rightarrow$  stopped plan  $\rightarrow$  planned

有些動词是不规则的，其簡單過去式不是加上- ed：

以下是一些重要的不规则動词：檢索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄2- 3

<table><tr><td>begin → began</td><td>fall → fell</td><td>leave→ left</td><td>sell → sold</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>break</td><td>break</td><td>find</td><td>lose</td><td>lost</td></tr><tr><td>bring</td><td>brought</td><td>fly</td><td>make</td><td>make</td></tr><tr><td>build</td><td>built</td><td>forget</td><td>meet</td><td>met</td></tr><tr><td>buy</td><td>bought</td><td>get</td><td>pay</td><td>paid</td></tr><tr><td>catch</td><td>caught</td><td>give</td><td>put</td><td>put</td></tr><tr><td>come</td><td>came</td><td>go</td><td>read</td><td>read /red/*</td></tr><tr><td>do</td><td>did</td><td>have</td><td>ring</td><td>think</td></tr><tr><td>drink</td><td>drank</td><td>hear</td><td>say</td><td>won</td></tr><tr><td>eat</td><td>ate</td><td>know</td><td>see</td><td>wrote</td></tr></table>

发音

I usually get up early, but this morning I got up at 9 o'clock. 我經常很早起，但今早我在9時才起床。 We did a lot of work yesterday. 昨天我們做了很多事情。 Caroline went to the cinema three times last week. 卡羅琳上星期看了三次電影。 James came into the room, took off his coat and sat down. 詹姆斯進入房間，脫下大衣，然後坐下來。

# 罪元 12

# I didn't ... Did you ...? 簡單過去式否定句和疑問句(past simple negative and questions)

A 在簡單過去式否定句和疑問句中用did：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dcff0db1c456cf16f5ae58ba57fb7330112b9b4babb9854876c9c2c2332c2acb.jpg)

do／does（現在式）  $\Rightarrow$  did（過去式）：

I don't watch television very often. 我不當看電視。  I didn't watch television yesterday. 我昨天没有看電視。  Does she often go away? 她是否經常離開？  Did she go away last week? 她上星期是否離開了？

did／didn't + 不定式（例如watch／play／go等等）：

注意

I watched 但 I didn't watch (不是I didn't watched)  they went did they go? (不是 did they went?)  he had he didn't have  you did did you do?

I played tennis yesterday, but I didn't win. 昨天我打網球，但没有勝出。

Did you do the shopping?' 'No, I didn't have time'「你去了購物嗎？」「我沒有時間。」  We went to the cinema, but we didn't enjoy the film. 我們去了看电影，但那電影不好看。

D 疑問句中的词序

<table><tr><td colspan="5">did + 主語 + 不定式</td></tr><tr><td>What How Where</td><td>Did did did did</td><td>your sister you the accident your parents</td><td>phone do happen? go</td><td>you? last night? for their holiday?</td></tr></table>

E 簡短回答

<table><tr><td>Yes,</td><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>did.</td><td>No,</td><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>didn&#x27;t.</td></tr></table>

Did you see Joe yesterday?' 'No, I didn't. 「昨天你有没有見過喬？」「沒有。」

Did it rain on Sunday?' 'Yes, it did.'

「星期日有下雨嗎？」「有。」

Did Helen come to the party?' 'No, she didn't.'

「海倫參加了派對嗎？」「沒有。」

Did your parents have a good holiday?' 'Yes, they did.'

「你的父母假期裹玩得開心嗎？」「開心。」

arrive cost go go to bed late happen have a nice time stay win

What were you doing at 11:30 yesterday? Were you working? 昨天11時30分你在做甚麼？是不是在工作？  What did he say? 'I don't know. I wasn't listening.' 「他說過甚麼？」「我不知道，我並沒有聽着他說話。」  It was raining, so we didn't go out. 那時正下着雨，所以我們沒有外出。  In 2001 we were living in Canada. 2001年的時候，我們正在加拿大居住。  Today she's wearing a skirt, but yesterday she was wearing trousers. 今天她穿了一條裙子，但昨天她穿褲子。  I woke up early yesterday. It was a beautiful morning. The sun was shining and the birds were singing. 昨天我很早便起牀。那是一個美麗的早晨，不但陽光普照，還有鳥兒在唱歌。

高方法（live  $\Rightarrow$  living / run  $\Rightarrow$  running / lie  $\Rightarrow$  lying 等等）： 檢素  $\Rightarrow$  附註5

am/is/are  $+$  - ing（現在式）  $\Rightarrow$  was/were  $+$  - ing（過去式）：

# 現在式

I'm working (now). 我正在工作。  It isn't raining (now). 現在沒有下雨。  What are you doing (now)? 你在做甚麼？

# 過去式

I was working at 10:30 last night. 昨晚10時30分我正在工作。  It wasn't raining when we went out. 我們外出時沒有下雨。  What were you doing at three o'clock? 三時正時你在做甚麼？

# I was doing 和 I did 14 過去進行式(past continuous)和簡單過去式(past simple)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3db7add18ca93676c715ac50abe9900724d067d53eca36df487170b13090eeb9.jpg)

Jack was reading a book. 佛克正在閱讀。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8ad8914ee3200cc5b9ecf8293100f5d190ffc6e1034b5af53ffa81d91bb705b3.jpg)

The phone rang. 電話響起。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/953bca1a73022f2598a78783300c48cedca380a429cebc7deae6ad4d4bbc349e.jpg)

He stopped reading. 他停止閱讀。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/62d5b19b374f56536652226f04017424095b0bdb195488dbf1f10c4ee93cca73.jpg)

He answered the phone. 他按曉電話。

What happened? The phone rang. 發生甚事？電話響起。（簡單過去式）  What was Jack doing when the phone rang? 電話響起時佛克正在做甚麼？ （過去進行式）  He was reading a book. 他正在閱讀。

What did he do when the phone rang? 電話響起時他怎樣做？  He stopped reading and answered the phone. （簡單過去式）  他停止閱讀，然後接聽電話。

佛克在電話響起前已開始閱讀。

所以：When the phone rang, he was reading. 當電話響起時，他正在閱讀。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/83b579c3a440ac46ec2d27988402e140f256c82c22f369f44610050bf1f4b912.jpg)

# 簡單過去式

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1fd953d3e65811418e32f94016ce63efb31c5c5c8269b450e36f28c202b66eeb.jpg)

Jack read a book yesterday.

（=由開始至結束）佛克昨天看了一本書。 Did you watch the film on television last night? 昨晚你有没有看電視播放的電影？ It didn't rain while we were on holiday. 我們度假時並沒有下雨。

# 過去進行式

A: What were you doing at 10:30? 10時30分時你們在做甚麼？ B: We were playing tennis. 我們正在打網球。

開始 (10時)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3decab685574a4e983e117951b87d9172a3c4b20bb5a796820647d17341faee2.jpg)

Jack was reading a book when the phone rang. 電話響起時候克正在閱讀。 Were you watching television when I phoned you? 我致電給你時你是否在看電視？ It wasn't raining when I got up. 我起時並沒有下雨。

I started work at 9 o'clock and finished at 4:30. At 2:30 I was working. 我9時開始工作，4時30分完成工作。2時30分的時候我正在工作。 It was raining when we went out. （=我們外出前已開始下雨）我們外出時正在下雨。 I saw Lucy and Steve this morning. They were waiting at the bus stop. 我今早看見露西和史蒂夫，他們當時正在巴士站候車。 Kelly fell asleep while she was reading. 凱莉閉讀時睡着了。

1 A: What ... were you doing (you / do) when the phone rang (ring)? B: I was watching (watch) television.

B: Yes, she (study).

3 A: What time (the post / arrive) this morning?

B: It (come) while I (have) breakfast.

B: No, she (not / go) to work. She was ill.

B: I'm not sure, but I (not / drive) very fast.

B: The weather was very bad, so we (not / play).

B: Yes, she (wear) a very nice jacket.

9 A: What (you / do) at 2 o'clock this morning?

B: I was asleep.

10 A: I (lose) my key last night.

B: How (you / get) into your room?

A: I (climb) in through a window.

# I have done

# 現在完成式1(present perfect 1)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/db4e7ebb24e32b03128f1699bfa70ac66d4bab875a8b228859086a942a614f40.jpg)

His shoes are dirty. 他的鞋子髒了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6dd710ce0ce311efffd125df424314c74dbdb9a4ae75f9e4b393b39235051c63.jpg)

He is cleaning his shoes. 他正在清潔鞋子。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5b73638a41df1078ec4a19cd89fb3ab8bacb92122b6c618992432c09d7d9cb88.jpg)

He has cleaned his shoes. 他已把鞋子弄乾凈。 (=他的鞋子現在是乾凈的)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fbf816d173539798e0091264da6ef3768b7be3374ea1a73bf39ed781b7ebbeca.jpg)

They are at home. 他們在家裏。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f8c6aa7cf508f807ec8e00fbd452c6f3490b9d5ae68cbb386486849be56d4303.jpg)

They are going out. 他們正外出。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/977f27b1c0e3336c1f58179614eda171e52679048c2cdc3a5c0a22ab80ae3b9a.jpg)

They have gone out. 他們已經外出了。 (=他們現時不在家)

has cleaned/have gone等等是現在完成式(have + 過去分詞):

<table><tr><td>I we you they</td><td>have (‘ve) have not (haven’t)</td><td rowspan="2">cleaned finished started lost done been gone</td><td>have</td><td>I we you they</td><td rowspan="2">cleaned? finished? started? lost? done? been? gone?</td><td>规则動詞</td></tr><tr><td>he she it</td><td>has (‘s) has not (hasn’t)</td><td>has</td><td>he she it</td><td>不规则動詞</td></tr></table>

# 過去分詞

规则動詞: 過去分詞是加上- ed(與簡單過去式相同):

clean  $\Rightarrow$  I have cleaned finish  $\Rightarrow$  we have finished start  $\Rightarrow$  she has started

不规则動詞: 過去分詞不是- ed,

有時簡單過去式和過去分詞相同:

buy  $\Rightarrow$  I bought / I have bought have  $\Rightarrow$  he had / he has had

有時簡單過去式和過去分詞不同:

break  $\Rightarrow$  I broke / I have broken see  $\Rightarrow$  you saw / you have seen

fall  $\Rightarrow$  it fell / it has fallen

go  $\Rightarrow$  they went / they have gone

現在完成式用於表示過去的動作,其影響現今仍然存在:

I've lost my passport. (= I can't find my passport now) 我遺失了護照。

'Where's Rebecca?' 'She's gone to bed.' (= she is in bed now) 「麗貝卡在哪裏？」 「她已睡了。」

We've bought a new car. (= we have a new car now) 我們買了一輛新車。

It's Rachel's birthday tomorrow and I haven't bought her a present. (= I don't have a present for her now) 明天是雷切爾的生日,我還沒有買禮物給她。

'Bob is away on holiday.' 'Oh, where has he gone?' (= where is he now?)

「鮑勃正在放假。」「噢！他去了哪兒？」

Can I take this newspaper? Have you finished with it? (= do you need it now?)

我可否拿走這份報紙？你看完了嗎？

# 16

# I've just ... I've already ... I haven't ... yet 現在完成式2(present perfect 2)

A I've just ...

just（剛剛）  $=$  很短時間之前

A:Are Diane and Paul here?黛安和保羅是否在這裹？ B:Yes,they've just arrived.是，他們剛剛到達。

A:Are you hungry?你肚子饿？

B:No,I've just had dinner.不，我剛吃過晚餐。

A:Is Tom here?湯姆是否在這裹？

B:No,I'm afraid he's just gone.  $(=$  he has just gone) 不，恐怕他剛離去了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b4022d37c2182b6ed24edc574a8231e2807bcd26d1c158df6e717d4c4f9f2fc4.jpg)  
They have just arrived.

B I've already ...

already（已經）  $=$  在你预期之前/在我预期之前

A:What time are Diane and Paul coming?

黛安和保羅會何時到來？

B:They've already arrived.他們已經來了。

$(=$  在你预期之前）

It's only 9 o'clock and Anna has already gone to bed. 現在只是9時，但安娜已經睡了。（  $=$  在我预期之前）

A:Jon,this is Emma.喬恩，這位是埃瑪。

B:Yes,I know.We've already met.

我知道，我們已經見過面了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e806da620bc819ab89879917fb45768f3396d065c9848d16b124d96b449b7963.jpg)

C I haven't ... yet / Have you ... yet?

yet（還/至今）  $=$  直到现在。亦可用於否定句及疑問句中，通常放在句末。

yet用於否定句中（I haven't ... yet）：

A:Are Diane and Paul here?黛安和保羅是否在這裹？ B:No,they haven't arrived yet.不，他們還未到達。

（但B頂期黛安和保羅快將到達）

A:Does James know that you're going away?

詹姆斯知道你將離開嗎？

B:No,I haven't told him yet.不，我還没有告他。（但B即將告他）

Silvia has bought a new dress, but she hasn't worn it yet. 西爾維亞買了一條新裙子，但她還沒有穿過。

yet用於疑問句中（Have you ... yet?）：

A:Have Diane and Paul arrived yet?

黛安和保羅已經到了嗎？

B:No, not yet. We're still waiting for them. 還沒有。我們仍在等候他們。

A:Has Nicole started her new job yet?

尼科爾開始了新工作嗎？

B:No, she starts next week.

還沒有，她下星期才開始新工作。

A:This is my new dress.

這是我的新裙子。

B:Oh, it's nice. Have you worn it yet?

噢！很漂亮，你穿過了沒有？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5f03becb9a1daa8551634766b09c73d4abbb9fb080feaa8f67843b5659ed165e.jpg)  
The film hasn't started yet.

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9f614277a73c99cfc0fbf94863398c601a6e12b522b53e9b8235b4abc9084d31.jpg)

# Have you ever ...? 現在完成式3(present perfect 3)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/39ba9759e16269e46105df2bfe38c8519bd33566cf0c076b179cfe4e07b36056.jpg)

現在完成式（例如have been／have had／have played等等）用於表示很過去直到現在的時間。例如某人的一生：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9e313e6a36526bd61780dcb1db9f8f69b44adb519d3b9eca31137dc6fb9b2e43.jpg)

Have you been to France?'No,I haven't.'「你曾去過法國？」「我未曾去過。」

I've been to Canada,but I haven't been to the United States.

我曾去過加拿大，但没有去過美國。

Mary is an interesting person. She has had many different jobs and has lived in many places. 瑪莉是一個有趣的人。她曾經做過很多份不同的工作，正在很多地方居住過。

I've seen that woman before,but I can't remember where．我曾見過那女子，但記不起在哪裹見過。

How many times has Brazil won the World Cup?巴西曾赢過多少次世界杯？

Have you read this book?'Yes,I've read it twice'(twice  $=$  two times)

「你曾看過這本書嗎？」「我看過兩次了。」

現在完成式  $^+$  ever（在疑問句中）及never：

Has Ann ever been to Australia?'Yes,once'  $\mathbf{\sigma}_{\mathbf{once}} = \mathbf{\sigma}_{\mathbf{once}}$  time)

「安曾否去過澳洲？」「有，她去過一次。」

Have you ever played golf?'Yes,I play a lot.'「你曾否打過高爾夫球？」「有，我經常打高爾夫球。」

My sister has never travelled by plane．我的妹妹徙來没有坐過飛機旅行。

I've never ridden a horse．我徙來没有骑過馬。

Who is that man?'I don't know.I've never seen him before.

# 试比较

# 简单現在式

简单現在完成式（例如have been / have lived / have known等等）

Dan and Kate are married. 丹和凱特已結婚。

Are you married? 你已結婚了嗎？

Do you know Lisa? 你認識莉莎嗎？

I know Lisa. 我認識莉莎。

Vicky lives in London. 雜基住在倫敦？

I have a car. 我有一輛車。

They have been married for five years. 他們已結婚5年了。（不是They are married for five years.）

How long have you been married? 你結了婚多久？

（不是How long are you married?）

How long have you known her? 你認識她多久？

（不是How long do you know her?）

I've known her for a long time. 我已認識她很久了。

（不是I know her for ...)

How long has she lived in London? 她住在倫敦多久？

She has lived there all her life. 她一直都是住在那裹。

How long have you had your car? 你擁有屬於自己的汽車多久？

I've had it since April. 我從四月開始便擁有它。

# 現在進行式

# 現在完成進行式 (have been + -ing)

I'm learning German. 我正在學習德文。

How long have you been learning German? 你學了德文多久？（不是How long are you learning German?）

I've been learning German for two years. 我學了德文兩年。

David is watching TV. 大衛正在看電視。

How long has he been watching TV? 他看了電視多久？

He's been (= He has been) watching TV since 5 o'clock.

他從5時開始便看電視。

It's raining. 現正在下雨。

It's been (= It has been) raining all day. 已經下了一整天雨。

for + 一段時間

例如 three days / two years 等等):

months. (不是 since six months)  理查德已到了加拿大六個月。

(不是 since two hours)

我們已經等了兩小時。

I've lived in London for a long time.

我在倫敦已住了一段長時間。

Richard has been in Canada since

January. (= 從一月到現在)

理查德自一月起已經在加拿大。

(= 從9時到現在)

我們從9時起已開始等候。

years old. 我從10歲起便住在倫敦。

ago 解作以前:

ago = before now:

Susan started her new job three weeks ago. 蘇珊三星期前開始了她的新工作。

(= 從現在開始計算的三星期前)

"When did Tom go out?" "Ten minutes ago." 「湯姆何時外出？」「10分鐘前。」 (= 從現在開始計算的10分鐘前)

I had dinner an hour ago. 我一小時前吃過晚餐。

Life was very different a hundred years ago. 一百年前的生活是很不同的。

ago 跟過去式一起用 (例如 started / did / had / was 等等)。

試比較 ago 和 for 的用法及其分別:

When did Jane arrive in Ireland? 珍何時到達愛爾蘭？

She arrived in Ireland three days ago. 她三天前到達愛爾蘭。

How long has she been in Ireland? 她到了愛爾蘭多久？

She has been in Ireland for three days. 她已到了愛爾蘭三天。

# I have done 和 I did

# 現在完成式(present perfect)和簡單過去式(past)

如句中指明動作的完成時間（例如yesterday/lastweek等等），便要使用過去式（例如arrived/saw/was等等）：

簡單過去式  $^+$  完成的時間

<table><tr><td>We arrived</td><td>yesterday.
last week.
at 3 o&#x27;clock.
in 2002.
six months ago.</td></tr></table>

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e1785fab42646b3087017a51fba7949db213bb98e7ed8c28f99307a6d6a64bf6.jpg)  
過去 現在

別把現在完成式（例如have arrived／have done／have been等等）跟指明的完成時間一起運用：

I saw Paula yesterday.（不是I have seen）我昨天看見葆拉。

Where were you on Sunday afternoon?（不是Where have you been）星期日下午你在哪裹？

We didn't have a holiday last year.（不是We haven't had）去年我們没有假期。

What did you do last night?'‘I stayed at home'「昨晚你做甚磨？」「我留在家裹。」

William Shakespeare lived from 1564 to 1616. He was a writer. He wrote many plays and poems.

莎士比亞徒1564年活到1616年。他是一位作家，寫過許多劇和詩篇。

# 注意

提出When...？或Whattime...？的疑問時，用簡單過去式：

When did you buy your computer?（不是When have you bought?）你的電腦是甚时候買的？

What time did Andy go out?（不是What time has Andy gone out?）安迪是甚時候出去的？

# B 被動語態是:

# 過去分詞

簡單現在式 am / is / are (not) 簡單退去式 was / were

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1b28ebe5128a0fff9572ebe24ba6e5ebceb68583da422a5f281c246069853790.jpg)

規則動詞的過去分詞是加上- ed(例如cleaned/damaged等等)。

有關不規則動詞的過去分詞(例如done/built/taken等等)的列表,

檢索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄2- 3。

Butter is made from milk. 牛油是從牛奶提煉而成的。

Oranges are imported into Britain. 檀是從國外輸入英國的。

How often are these rooms cleaned? 這些房間隔多久才有人打掃?

I am never invited to parties. 我從來沒有獲邀請參加派對。

This house was built 100 years ago. 這屋子是100年前建成的。

These houses were built 100 years ago. 這些屋子是100年前建成的。

When was the telephone invented? 電話是何時發明的?

We weren't invited to the party last week. 我們沒有獲邀請參加上星期的派對。

'Was anybody injured in the accident?' 'Yes, two people were taken to hospital.'

「是否有人在意外中受傷？」「是，有兩個人被送往醫院了。」

# C was / were born

I was born in Berlin in 1989. (不是I am born) 我於1989年在柏林出生。

'Where were you born?' 'In Cairo.' 「你在哪裏出生？」「開羅。」

你喜歡這些油畫嗎？它們是我的一個朋友畫的。

单元 22

# is being done has been done 被動語態2 (passive 2)

A is / are being... (現在進行式被動語態)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/421ce6eba586a55ab9b14eeb4e6a5ebee0848ffeab7394480fc01a8087b4943d.jpg)

有人正將門塗上油漆。

Somebody is painting the door. (主動語態)

The door is being painted. (被動語態)

門正被塗上油漆。

My car is at the garage. It is being repaired. (= somebody is repairing it)

我的車正在修理廠維修。

Some new houses are being built opposite the park. (= somebody is building them)

公園對面正在興建一些新房子。

試比較

現在進行式和簡單現在式被動語態的分別：

The office is being cleaned at the moment. (現在進行式) 那辦公室現正有人打掃。

The office is cleaned every day. (簡單現在式) 那辦公室每天都有人打掃。

In Britain football matches are often played at the weekend, but no matches are being played next weekend. 在英國，足球比賽通常是在星期六舉行的，但下星期六沒有賽事舉行。

有關現在進行式及簡單現在式，檢索 → 罪元8和25。

has / have been... (現在完成式被動語態)

之前 → 之後

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/51ffc0db805ca7a7fc66c8365dd7e61755b55570f94bd57a07669b1cb46d17ff.jpg)

有人已將門塗上油漆。

Somebody has painted the door. (主動語態)

The door has been painted. (被動語態)

門已被塗上油漆。

My key has been stolen. (= somebody has stolen it) 我的鑰匙被偷了。

My keys have been stolen. (= somebody has stolen them) 我的鑰匙被偷了。

I'm not going to the party. I haven't been invited. (= nobody has invited me)

我不打算參加那派對，因為我未獲邀請。

Has this shirt been washed? (= has somebody washed it?) 這件襯衣洗過了沒有？

試比較 現在完成式和簡單過去式被動語態的分別：

The room isn't dirty any more. It has been cleaned. (現在完成式)

那房間現在很清潔，它曾經被打掃過。

The room was cleaned yesterday. (簡單過去式) 昨天那房間被打掃過。

I can't find my keys. I think they've been stolen. (現在完成式) 我找不到鑰匙，我想它們被偷去了。My keys were stolen last week. (簡單過去式) 上星期我的鑰匙被偷去了。

有關現在完成式和簡單過去式，檢索 → 罪元20。

檢索 is done / was done → 罪元21 主動語態和被動語態 → 附錄1

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2d7cca163721500e12562a74ba68977a2e2485f7d8284232c02be3bef5e3b701.jpg)

单元 23

# be / have / do 現在式(in present)和過去式(past tenses)

be (= am / is / are / was / were) + - ing (例如 cleaning / working 等等)

am / is / are + - ing (現在進行式)

$\Rightarrow$  職元3- 4和26

was / were + - ing (過去進行式)

$\Rightarrow$  職元13

Please be quiet.I'm working. 保持萧静。我正在工作。 It isn't raining at the moment. 現在没有下雨。 What are you doing this evening? 今天傍晚你會做甚麼？

I was working when she arrived. 她到達時我正在工作。

It wasn't raining, so we didn't need an umbrella.

當時不是下着雨,所以我們不需要雨傘。

What were you doing at 3 o'clock?

三時正的時候你在做甚麼？

# be +過去分詞(例如cleaned / made / eaten 等等)

am / is / are + 過去分詞 (簡單現在式被動語態)

$\Rightarrow$  職元21

was / were + 過去分詞 (簡單過去式被動語態)

$\Rightarrow$  職元21

I'm never invited to parties. 我從沒有獲邀請參加派對。

Butter is made from milk. 牛油是從牛奶提煉而成的。

These offices aren't cleaned every day.

這些辦公室不是每天都有人打拂。

The office was cleaned yesterday. 辦公室昨天被打拂過了。

These houses were built 100 years ago.

這些房子是100年前興建的。

How was the window broken? 窗子是怎樣被打破的？

Where were you born? 你在哪裏出生？

# have / has + 過去分詞(例如cleaned / lost / eaten / been 等等)

have / has + 過去分詞 (現在完成式)

$\Rightarrow$  職元15- 18

I've cleaned my room. 我已把自己的房間打拂過了。 Tom has lost his passport. 湯姆遺失了護照。 Kate hasn't been to Canada. 凱特從未去過加拿大。 Where have Paul and Nicole gone? 保羅和尼科爾去了哪裏？

# do / does / did + 不定式(例如clean / like / eat / go 等等)

do / does + 不定式 (簡單現在式否定句和疑問句)

$\Rightarrow$  職元6- 7

did + 不定式 (簡單過去式否定句和疑問句)

$\Rightarrow$  職元12

I like coffee, but I don't like tea. 我喜歡喝咖啡,但不受喝茶。

Chris doesn't go out very often. 克里斯不是經常外出的。

What do you usually do at weekends? 週末時你通常會做甚麼？

Does Silvia live alone? 西爾維亞是否獨居？

I didn't watch TV yesterday. 昨天我沒有看電視。

It didn't rain last week. 上星期沒有下雨。

What time did Paul and Nicole go out?

保羅和尼科爾是何時外出的？

# 规则動词(regular verbs)和不规则動词(irregular verbs)

规则動词

其簡單過去式和過去分词是加上- ed:

clean  $\rightarrow$  cleaned live  $\rightarrow$  lived paint  $\rightarrow$  painted study  $\rightarrow$  studied

簡單過去式: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元11

I cleaned my room yesterday. 昨天我打掃了我的房間。

Charlie studied engineering at university. 查利在大學裹修讀工程學。

過去分词:

現在完成式  $\mathbf{\sigma} = \mathbf{\sigma}$  have/has  $^+$  過去分词: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元15- 18

I have cleaned my room. 我已打掃了我的房間。

Tina has lived in London for ten years. 蒂娜在倫敦已居住了10年。

被動語態  $\mathbf{\sigma} = \mathbf{\sigma}$  be(is/are/were/has been等等)  $^+$  過去分词: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元21- 22

These rooms are cleaned every day. 這些房間每天都有人打掃。

My car has been repaired. 我的汽車已給修理過。

不规则動词

其簡單過去式和過去分词是不是加上- ed:

<table><tr><td>簡單過去式</td><td>make</td><td>break</td><td>cut</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">過去分詞</td><td>made</td><td>broke</td><td>cut</td></tr><tr><td>made</td><td>broken</td><td>cut</td></tr></table>

有時簡單過去式和過去分詞是相同的,例如:

<table><tr><td>簡單過去式</td><td>make</td><td>find</td><td>buy</td><td>cut</td></tr><tr><td>過去分詞</td><td>made</td><td>found</td><td>bought</td><td>cut</td></tr></table>

I made a cake yesterday. (簡單過去式)昨天我做了一個蛋糕。

I have made some coffee. (過去分詞——現在完成式)我已沖調了一些咖啡。

Butter is made from milk. (過去分詞——現在式被動語態)牛油是從牛奶提煉而成的。

有時簡單過去式和過去分詞是不同的,例如:

<table><tr><td>簡單過去式</td><td>break</td><td>know</td><td>begin</td><td>go</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">過去分詞</td><td>broke</td><td>knew</td><td>began</td><td>went</td></tr><tr><td>broken</td><td>known</td><td>begin</td><td>gone</td></tr></table>

Somebody broke this window last night. (簡單過去式)昨晚有人把這扇窗子打破了。

Somebody has broken this window. (過去分詞——現在完成式)有人已把這扇窗子打破了。

This window was broken last night. (過去分詞——過去式被動語態)昨晚這扇窗子被打破了。

# 25

# What are you doing tomorrow?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e2caf20aa4b5656ead478871c4911b56242dc56bb472f816d970f2459f2ccbee.jpg)

They are playing tennis (now). 他們（現時）正在打網球。

today is Sunday

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/658779baa71140c8e56012abd6f46a67644ad91ac4a666ce318c7ff3a0fecd44.jpg)

He is playing tennis tomorrow. 明天他打算去打網球。

am / is / are + - ing（現在進行式）用於表達正在發生的事情：

'Where are Sue and Amanda?' 'They're playing tennis in the park.'

「蘇和阿曼達在哪裹？」「她們正在公園裏打網球。」

Please be quiet. I'm working.請保持肃靜，我正在工作。

am / is / are + - ing也可用於表達將來的事情（例如tomorrow／nextweek等等）：

Andrew is playing tennis tomorrow. 安德鲁明天打算去打網球。

I'm not working next week. 下星期我不上班。

I am doing something tomorrow = 我已安排或已計劃明天做某事情。

Sophie is going to the dentist on Friday.

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} =$  she has an appointment with the dentist)

索菲將於星期五見牙醫。

We're having a party next weekend.

下個週末我們將要舉行一個派對。

Are you meeting your friends tonight?

今晚你將要見你的朋友嗎？

What are you doing tomorrow evening?

明天傍晚你會做甚麼？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/28a253aeb14861c08128201fcee59985e4c68148aa9d4d339288487aeec2ca26.jpg)

I'm not going out tonight. I'm staying at home.今晚我不打算外出，我會留在家裏。

也可以用I'm going to do something' 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元26。

引用簡單現在式（例如Istay／doyougo等等）來表示計劃和安排：

I'm staying at home this evening.（不是Istay）今天傍晚我打算留在家裹。

Are you going out tonight?（不是Do you go）今晚你打算外出嗎？

Lisa isn't coming to the party next week.（不是Lisa doesn't come）莉莎下星期不來參加派對。

注意

但簡單現在式可用於時間表，或有關節目、火車、巴士時間等情況：

The train arrives at 7:30. 火車於7時30分到達。

What time does the film finish? 這電影於何時結束？

# 試比較

現在進行式——通常用於人

I'm going to a concert tomorrow. 明天我打算去聽音樂會。

What time are you leaving?

你將會何時離開？

簡單現在式——通常用於時間表、節目程序表等等

The concert starts at 7:30.

音樂會於7時30分開始。

What time does your train leave?

你乘的火車在甚時候開出？

# I'm going to ...

I'm going to do something

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/63e9389fa841e58d9d4127f047f8f5da7f09199706544883e8e3a28049250ab2.jpg)

She is going to watch TV this evening. 今天晚上她打算看電視。

am / is / are going to... 用於表達將來發生的事情:

<table><tr><td>am he / she / it we / you / they</td><td>am is he / she / it are</td><td>(not) going to</td><td>do ... drink ... watch ...</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>am</td><td>I</td><td>going to</td><td>buy ...?</td></tr><tr><td>is</td><td>he / she / it</td><td rowspan="2">eat ...?</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>are</td><td>we / you / they</td><td>wear ...?</td></tr></table>

I am going to do something = 我已決定做某事情或我意圖做某事情:

I decided to do it  $\rightarrow$  I'm going to do it

過去

現在

將來

I'm going to buy some books tomorrow. 明天我打算買一些書本。

Sarah is going to sell her car. 薩拉打算賣掉她的汽車。

I'm not going to have breakfast this morning. I'm not hungry.

今早我不打算吃早餐,因為我不覺得餓。

What are you going to wear to the wedding next week? 你打算穿甚麼出席下星期的婚禮?

'Your hands are dirty.' 'Yes, I know. I'm going to wash them.'

「你的手髒了。」「我知道,我正準備洗手。」

Are you going to invite Martin to your party? 你打算邀請馬田參加你的派對嗎?

現在進行式 (I am doing) 也可用於表示將來發生的事,例如常用於安排事情方面:

檢索

單元25:

I am playing tennis with Julia tomorrow. 明天我打算跟茱莉姬打網球。

Something is going to happen

Something is going to happen = 現在很明確知道某事情必定會發生:

Look at the sky! It's going to rain.

看看天空! 快要下雨了。

(現時有黑雲  $\rightarrow$  下雨)

Oh dear! It's 9 o'clock and I'm not ready.

I'm going to be late.

哎呀! 已經 9 時了,但我還未準備好。我將會遲到了。(現在已 9 時,加上未準備好  $\rightarrow$  遲到)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c8c58332cc7164d474b4fab35c339d3ac8665fa13d636ddc48b5db98d02f645c.jpg)

单元 27 will / shall 1

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e31988ee1713197bc5513351883f7a8aafc38a4dafb539e2ace9ca4d963bc9ad.jpg)  
SAOAH

Sarah goes to work every day. She is always there from 8:30 until 4:30.

It is 11 o'clock now. Sarah is at work.

现在是11时,薩拉正在工作。

At 11 o'clock yesterday, she was at work.

昨天11时,她正在工作。

At 11 o'clock tomorrow, she will be at work.

明天11时,她將會在工作。

will + 不定式(例如 will be / will win / will come 等等):

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>will (‘ll) will not (won’t)</td><td>be win eat come 等等</td><td>will</td><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>be? win? eat? come? 等等</td></tr></table>

'll = will: I'll (I will) / you'll / she'll 等等

won't = will not: I won't (= I will not) / you won't / she won't 等等

will 用於將來式(例如 tomorrow / next week 等等):

Sue travels a lot. Today she is in Madrid. Tomorrow she'll be in Rome. Next week she'll be in Tokyo.

蘇經常旅遊。今天她在馬德里,明天她將會在羅馬,下星期她將會在東京。

You can call me this evening. I'll be at home. 今天傍晚你可致電給我,我將會在家裏。

Leave the old bread in the garden. The birds will eat it. 把過期的麵包留在花園里,鳥兒會吃的。

We'll probably go out this evening. 今天傍晚我們可能會外出。

Will you be at home this evening? 今天傍晚你會在家嗎?

I won't be here tomorrow. (= I will not be here) 明天我不會在這裏。

Don't drink coffee before you go to bed. You won't sleep. 睡前別喝咖啡,否則你將不能入睡。

注意

I think ... will ... 也是很常用的:

I think Kelly will pass the exam. 我想凱莉這次考試將會及格。

I don't think it will rain this afternoon. 我不認為今天下午會下雨。

Do you think the exam will be difficult? 你認為這次考試會困難嗎?

引用 will 來表示我們已經安排或決定做的事情: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元 25- 26

We're going to the cinema on Saturday. Do you want to come with us? (不是 We will go)

星期六我們打算去看電影。你想和我們一起去嗎?

I'm not working tomorrow. (不是 I won't work) 明天我不上班。

Are you going to do the exam? (不是 Will you do) 你打算參加考試嗎?

D

shall

可用於 I shall (= I will) 和 we shall (= we will):

I shall be late tomorrow. 或 I will (I'll) be late tomorrow. 明天我將會遲到。

I think we shall win. 或 I think we will (we'll) win. 我想我們會勝出。

注意

別把 shall 跟 you / they / he / she / it 一起用:

Tom will be late. (不是 Tom shall be) 湯姆將會遲到。

# will / shall 2

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7fdd333f91c7c21b0380421ccb9450bdff290ad7c9e1d6393bcc5653f9c67ffd.jpg)

I'll carry it for you. Bye, I'll phone you tomorrow, OK? 'I'll phone you tomorrow, OK?' 'OK, bye.' 「我明天致電給你，好嗎？」「好，再見。」此外，當決定做某事情時，也會經常用 I think I'll ... / I don't think I'll ... : I'm tired. I think I'll go to bed early tonight. 我感到疲倦，我想今晚會早點睡。 It's a nice day. I think I'll sit outside. 今天天氣很好，我會到外面坐坐。 It's raining. I don't think I'll go out. 下雨了，我想我不會外出。

# 注意

別在以下類型的句子中用簡單現在式（例如 I go / I phone 等等）：

I'll phone you tomorrow, OK? （不是 I phone you） 我明天致電給你，好嗎？ I think I'll go to bed early. （不是 I go to bed） 我想我會早點睡覺。

B 別用I'll來表示一些你之前已決定的事情： 檢索 →單元25- 26

I'm working tomorrow. (不是 I'll work) 明天我打算工作。

There's a good film on TV tonight. I'm going to watch it. (不是 I'll watch)

今晚電視會播放一齣好電影，我打算收看。

What are you doing at the weekend? (不是 What will you do) 本週末你打算做甚麼？

Shall I ...? Shall we ...?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0c736163fd0b896ca811dc8f6505e54b256fccdec5d6faee06d4bd1923c249d6.jpg)

Shall I / Shall we ...? = 你認為這樣做好嗎？或你覺得這是個好主意嗎？

It's very warm in this room. Shall I open the window? 這房間十分暖，我可否把窗子打開？

Shall I phone you this evening? 'Yes, please.' 「今天傍晚我應否致電給你？」「好的，請你致電給我。」

I'm going to a party tonight. What shall I wear? 今晚我打算參加派對。我穿甚麼好呢？

It's a nice day. Shall we go for a walk? 今天天氣很好，我們外出散步好嗎？

Where shall we go for our holidays this year? 今年的假期我們到哪裏去好呢？

Let's go out this evening. 'OK, what time shall we meet?'

「今晚不如外出吧。」「好的，我們何時見面？」

# might

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b69d7ba40e7aa6ee8563742e3778fc1b989aeb63be8e97b78c43fad40435e486.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8630d207ff07a9800fc1d224bbc7233b426ed84d6267c590bd286b4264eb99e8.jpg)

He might go to New York. 他可能會去紐約。 (= it is possible that he will go to New York)

It might rain. 可能會下雨。 (= it is possible that it will rain)

might + 不定式 (例如 might go / might be / might rain 等等)：

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>might (not)</td><td>be
go
play
come</td><td>等等</td></tr></table>

I might = 我可能會...：

I might go to the cinema this evening, but I'm not sure. (= it is possible that I will go) 今天傍晚我可能會去看電影，但我不肯定。

A: When is Rebecca going to phone you? 屬貝卡打算何時致電給你？

B: I don't know. She might phone this afternoon. 我不知道。她可能會在今天下午致電給我。

Take an umbrella with you. It might rain. 可能會下雨，你帶備雨傘吧。

Buy a lottery ticket. You might be lucky. (= perhaps you will be lucky)

買一張獎券吧，你可能會中獎。

'Are you going out tonight?' 'I might.' (= I might go out) 「今晚你打算外出嗎？」「我可能會。」

# 注意

下列句子的分別：

I'm playing tennis tomorrow. 明天我打算打網球。 (肯定的) I might play tennis tomorrow. 明天我可能會打網球。 (可能的) Rebecca is going to phone later. 屬貝卡打算待會兒打電話來。 (肯定的) Rebecca might phone later. 屬貝卡可能待會兒會打電話來。 (可能的)

否定式為 might not：

I might not go to work tomorrow. (= it is possible that I will not go) 明天我未必會上班。 Sue might not come to the party. (= it is possible that she will not come) 蘇未必會來參加派對。

D

may

可以同樣方式使用 I may = I might：

I may go to the cinema this evening. (= I might go) 今天傍晚我可能會去看電影。 Sue may not come to the party. (= Sue might not come) 蘇未必會來參加派對。

May I...? = Is it OK to...? / Can I...?：

May I ask a question? (= is it OK to ask / can I ask?) 我可否問一條問題？

'May I sit here?' 'Yes, of course.' 「我可否坐在這裏？」「當然可以。」

單元 30

# can 和 could

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f14be938141be4bc2629b74d045831e180e59f2da211c52a071e9e1a4952ce28.jpg)

He can play the piano. 他懂得彈琴。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/20295c31709d44999e0498cca684b58670bad3d04548160a75c357656d6481b7.jpg)

can + 不定式 (例如 can do / can play / can come 等等)：

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they
he / she / it</td><td>can
can&#x27;t (cannot)</td><td>do
play
see
come 等等</td><td>can</td><td>I / we / you / they
he / she / it</td><td>do?
play?
see?
come? 等等</td></tr></table>

I can do something = 我懂得怎樣做或我能夠做到某事情：

I can play the piano. My brother can play the piano too. 我懂得彈鋼琴，我哥哥也懂得彈鋼琴。

Sarah can speak Italian, but she can't speak Spanish. 薩拉懂得說意大利語，但不懂得說西班牙語。

'Can you swim?' 'Yes, but I'm not a very good swimmer.' 「你懂得游泳嗎？」「懂，但不是游得很好。」

'Can you change twenty pounds?' 'I'm sorry, I can't.'

「你能把20鎊換成零錢嗎？」「對不起，我不能。」

I'm having a party next week, but Paul and Rachel can't come.

下星期我打算舉行派對，但保羅和雷切爾不能來。

could / couldn't 用於過去式 (例如 yesterday / last week 等等)：

When I was young, I could run very fast. 我年輕時能跑得很快。

Before Maria came to Britain, she couldn't understand much English. Now she can understand everything.

瑪麗亞來英國之前不怎麼懂得英語，但現在她完全懂得了。

I was tired last night, but I couldn't sleep. 雖然昨晚我疲倦得很，但卻不能入睡。

I had a party last week, but Paul and Rachel couldn't come.

上星期我舉行了一個派對，但保羅和雷切爾不能來。

Can you ...? Could you ...? Can I ...? Could I ...?

Can you ...? 或 Could you ...? 用於請求他人做某事情：

Can you open the door, please? 或 Could you open the door, please? 請問你可否開門？

Can you wait a moment, please? 或 Could you wait ...? 請問你可否等一會？

Can I have ...? 或 Could I have ...? 用於要求一些東西：

(in a shop) Can I have these postcards, please? 或 Could I have ...?

(在商店內) 請問我可否要這些明信片？

Can I ...? 或 Could I ...? = 是否可以做某事情？

Tom, can I borrow your umbrella? 或 Tom, could I borrow your umbrella? 湯姆，我可否借你的雨傘？

(on the phone) Hello, can I speak to Gary, please? 或 ... could I speak ...? (在電話中) 你好，請問加里在嗎？

decide eat find go go sleep

30.4 在以下情況你會說些甚麼?用can或could及括號內的詞語造句。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ab6cc7ff3a3a9ad15b48bea135cc4f52d881a18aa261232bf0b56bbafb2beeee.jpg)

# must mustn't don't need to

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a6f108bbb3d158bb755543630fb4bda88f1622604309c93669e8c69375f3239c.jpg)

It's a fantastic film. You must see it.

must + 不定式（例如 must do / must work 等等）：

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>must</td><td>do go see eat 等等</td></tr></table>

I must (do something) = 我有必要做某事：

I'm very hungry. I must eat something. 我很饿，必须吃東西。  It's a fantastic film. You must see it. 這電影好看極了，你一定要看。  The windows are very dirty. We must clean them. 那些窗子十分骯髒，我必須清潔它們。

注意 had to...（不是 must）用於過去式（例如 yesterday / last week 等等）：

I was very hungry. I had to eat something.（不是 I must eat）當時我很餓，不得不吃東西。  We had to walk home last night. There were no buses.（不是 We must walk）昨晚沒有巴士，我們只得步行回家。

mustn't (= must not)

I mustn't (do something) = 必定不能做某事情，因為這是不恰當的：

I must hurry. I mustn't be late.

我一定要赶快，我不能遇到的。

I mustn't forget to phone Jane.

(=I must remember to phone her)

我千萬不能忘記致電給珍。

Be happy! You mustn't be sad. (= don't be sad)

開心一點吧！你不要感到悲傷。

You mustn't touch the pictures.

(=don't touch the pictures) 你不得觸摸那些書。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/87d5174a14d97432d042437bc62f99c696e462f117cea1d484ab2c19f5ed07fe.jpg)

don't need to

I don't need (to do something) = 乖乖必定要做某事情，我不必做某事情：

I don't need to go yet. I can stay a little longer. 我還不需要走，我可以多留一會。

You don't need to shout. I can hear you OK. 你不需大叫。我可以聽見你的說話。

你也可以說 don't have to...：

I don't have to go yet. I can stay a little longer. 我還不需要走。我可以多留一會。

試比較

don't need to 和 mustn't 的分別：

You don't need to go. You can stay here if you want. 你不必離開，你想的話可以留在這裏。  You mustn't go. You must stay here. 你不可離開，必須留在這裏。

# 单元 32

# should

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9ec2b9bef5d6a39777efdc210d869557feac0e52dd872f91e53b278e1167c4aa.jpg)

should  $^+$  不定式（例如shoulddo/shouldwatch等等）：

<table><tr><td></td><td></td><td>do</td><td>stop</td></tr><tr><td>I / we / you / they he / she / it</td><td>should shouldn&#x27;t</td><td>go watch 等等</td><td></td></tr></table>

You should do something  $=$  你應該做某事：

You should do something  $=$  你應該做某事：- Tom doesn't study enough. He should study harder. 湯姆學習得不夠。他應該更努力學習。- It's a good film. You should go and see it. 這是一齣好電影，你應該去看。- When you play tennis, you should always watch the ball. 你打網球時，應該總是看着那個球。

You shouldn't do something  $=$  你不應該做某事：shouldn't  $=$  shouldn't:

Tom shouldn't go to bed so late. 湯姆不應該像晚才睡。

You watch TV all the time. You shouldn't watch TV so much.

你由早到晚都看電視。你不應這長時間看電視的。

D 我們通常I think...should...

I think...should...

I think Lisa should buy some new clothes.

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{I}$  think it is a good idea.) 我為莉莎應該買一些新衣服。

It's late. I think I should go home now.

很晚了，我想我現在應該回家去。

A: Shall I buy this coat? 我買這件大衣好嗎？B: Yes, I think you should. 好，我認為你應該買。

I don't think... should...

I don't think you should work so hard.

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{I}$  don't think it is a good idea.)

我不為你應該工作得這麽辛苦。

I don't think we should go yet. It's too early.

時候還早，我不為我們應該離去。

Do you think... should...?

Do you think I should buy this hat? 你認為我應該買這帽子嗎？

What time do you think we should go home? 你認為我們應何時回家呢？

用must的語氣较用should強：

It's a good film. You should go and see it. 這是一齣好電影，你應該去看。

It's a fantastic film. You must go and see it. 這電影好看極了，你一定要看。

除了should之外，也可用ought to表達同樣的意思：

It's a good film. You ought to go and see it.  $\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{y}$  ou should go）這是一齣好電影，你應該去看。

I think Lisa ought to buy some new clothes.  $\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{L}$  isa should buy)

我為莉莎應該買一些新衣服。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1ab34ea51749e4b4591ab24cc0937692431906544ea5f7a50c2142e553c378bc.jpg)

I have to do something = 我有必要或有義務做某事情:

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they</td><td>have</td><td rowspan="2">to do
to work
to go
to wear</td><td rowspan="2">等
等</td></tr><tr><td>he / she / it</td><td>has</td></tr></table>

I'll be late for work tomorrow. I have to go to the dentist. 明天我會晚些上班,因為我要去看牙醫。Jane starts work at 7 o'clock, so she has to get up at 6. 珍7時開始工作,所以她6時必須起。You have to pass a test before you can get a driving licence. 你必須考試及格才可獲得駕駛執照。

過去式（例如yesterday／lastweek等等）是hadto...

I was late for work yesterday. I had to go to the dentist. 昨天我選了上班,因我要去看牙醫。

We had to walk home last night. There were no buses. 昨晚我們不得不步行回家,因為没有巴士。

在疑問句和否定句中,可用do／does（現在式）和did（過去式）:

现在式

<table><tr><td>do I / we / you / they</td><td rowspan="2">have to ...?</td><td>I / we / you / they don&#x27;t</td><td rowspan="2">have to ...</td></tr><tr><td>does he / she / it</td><td>he / she / it doesn&#x27;t</td></tr></table>

過去式

<table><tr><td>did</td><td>I / we / you / they
he / she / it</td><td>have to ...?</td><td>I / we / you / they
he / she / it</td><td>didn&#x27;t have to ...</td></tr></table>

What time do you have to go to the dentist tomorrow? 你明天甚时候要去看牙醫?

Does Jane have to work on Sundays? 珍星期日要上班嗎?

Why did they have to leave the party early? 他們為甚麼要提早離開派對?

I don't have to (do something) = 不必要做某事情:

I'm not working tomorrow, so I don't have to get up early. 明天我不用上班,所以不必早起。

Ian doesn't have to work very hard. He's got an easy job.

伊恩無需工作得太辛苦,他的工作是十分容易的。

We didn't have to wait very long for the bus — it came in a few minutes.

我們無需花太長時間等巴士——幾分鐘後它就來了。

must和haveto

表達個人意見時,用must和haveto表示你超為有必要做的事:

It's a fantastic film. You must see it. 或 You have to see it. 這會電影好看極了,你一定要看。

不是表達個人意見時,用have to（不用must）試比較

Jane won't be at work this afternoon. She has to go to the doctor. 珍今天下午將不上班,她要去看醫生。

（這不是個人意見,而是事實）

Jane isn't well. She doesn't want to go to the doctor, but I told her she must go.

珍身體不舒服。她不想去看醫生,但我叫她必須去。（這是個人意見）

# Would you like ...? I'd like ...

Would you like ...? = Do you want ...?

A: Would you like some coffee? 你想要一些咖啡嗎？ B: No, thank you. 不用了，謝謝。 A: Would you like a chocolate? 你想吃巧克力嗎？ B: Yes, please. 好的，謝謝。 A: What would you like, tea or coffee? 你要茶還是咖啡？ B: Tea, please. 菜，謝謝。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ae73186f64be054d22a34c23c47f3c4adea909a45ff3d2f400a2758cecf06dc5.jpg)

Would you like to go for a walk? 你想去散步嗎？

A: Would you like to have dinner with us on Sunday? 星期日你想跟我們一起吃晚餐嗎？ B: Yes, I'd love to. (= I would love to have dinner with you) 好的。 What would you like to do this evening? 今天晚上你想做甚麼？

I'd like / I would like ... 是表示 'I want' 的較有禮貌的說法：

I'm thirsty. I'd like a drink. 我覺得口渴，想要一杯飲品。

(in a tourist office) I'd like some information about hotels, please.

(在旅行社內) 我想要一些有關酒店的資料。

I'm feeling tired. I'd like to stay at home this evening. 我感到很累。我今晚想留在家裏。

Would you like ...? 和 Do you like ...? 的分別：

Would you like ...? / I'd like ...

Do you like ...? / I like ...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/27332c550b52fe4316e5a008f763bf707b488658b02bc80398320a6e2c0c19f0.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3baa11cb6c847beaaaacbbd1ea50f2fded1df773c1d749f1e3dc12854fa64336.jpg)

Would you like some tea? = Do you want some tea? 你想要一些茶嗎？

A: Would you like to go to the cinema tonight? (= do you want to go tonight?) 今晚你想去看電影嗎？ B: Yes, I'd love to. 好的。

I'd like an orange, please. (= can I have an orange?) 我想要一個橙，謝謝。

What would you like to do next weekend? 下個週末你想做甚麼？

Do you like tea? = Do you think tea is nice? 你喜歡喝茶嗎？

A: Do you like going to the cinema? (in general) 你喜歡看電影嗎？(用於一般情況) B: Yes, I go to the cinema a lot. 喜歡，我經常去看電影。

I like oranges. (in general) 我喜歡吃橙。(用於一般情況)

What do you like to do at weekends? 週末時你喜歡做甚麼？

# 34.3 選擇正確的答案。

1 'Do you like / Would you like a chocolate?' 'Yes, please.' (Would you like 是正確答案) 2 'Do you like / Would you like bananas?' 'Yes, I love them.' 3 'Do you like / Would you like an ice- cream?' 'No, thank you.' 4 'What do you like / would you like to drink?' 'A glass of water, please.' 5 'Do you like / Would you like to go out for a walk?' 'Not now. Perhaps later.' 6 I like / I'd like tomatoes, but I don't eat them very often. 7 What time do you like / would you like to have dinner this evening? 8 'Do you like / Would you like something to eat?' 'No, thanks. I'm not hungry.' 9 'Do you like / Would you like your new job?' 'Yes, I'm enjoying it.' 10 I'm tired. I like / I'd like to go to sleep now. 11 'I like / I'd like a sandwich, please.' 'Sure. What kind of sandwich?' 12 'What kind of music do you like / would you like?' 'All kinds.'

# 联元 35

# Do this! Don't do that! Let's do this!

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2ebdc8fdf3d2810742ccaf5b1dcd325abedf8cbd128019c26d020ac8149243cc.jpg)

蕾我們叫别人做某事時,我們用come/look/go/wait/do/be等等:

Come here and look at this! What is it? 「來這裹看看這個！」「這是甚麼？」

I don't want to talk to you. Go away! 我不想和你說話。走開！

I'm not ready yet. Please wait for me. 我還未準備好。請等一等我。

Please be quiet. I'm working. 請保持肅靜。我正在工作。

同様

Bye! Have a good holiday! 再見！希望你有一個愉快的假期！/Have a nice time! 希望你有快樂的時光。Have a good flight! 希望你坐飛機旅途愉快！/Have fun! 蛊情玩吧！ (= I hope you have a good holiday 等等)

Have a chocolate' 'Oh, thanks' (= would you like a chocolate?)

「吃巧克力吧。」「噢,謝謝。」

蕾我們叫别人不要做某事時,我們用Don't...：

Be careful Don't fall. 小心！不要跌下來。

Please don't go. Stay here with me. 請不要走,留在這裹和我一起。

Be here on time. Don't be late. 要準時來到這裹。不要遲到。

蕾你想別人和你一起做某事時,你可以說Let's...。Let's = Let us。

It's a nice day. Let's go out. (= you and I can go out) 今天天氣好。我們出去吧。

Come on Let's dance. (= you and I can dance) 來吧！我們跳舞吧。

Are you ready? Let's go.

你準備好嗎？我們走吧。

Let's have fish for dinner tonight.

我們今晚晚飯吃魚吧。

A: Shall we go out tonight? 我們今晚將會出去嗎？

B: No, I'm tired. Let's stay at home.

不,我很累。我們留在家裹吧。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b5db417d2655991522b996e6798378f8d9744c16146ae5997408dd65c1a783b8.jpg)

否定式是Let's not...：

It's cold. Let's not go out. Let's stay at home. 天氣冷。我們不要出去。我們留在家裹吧。

Let's not have fish for dinner tonight. Let's have chicken. 我們今晚晚飯不要吃魚。我們吃雞吧。

或者,你可以说Don't let's...：

It's cold. Don't let's go out. Let's stay at home. 天氣冷。我們不要出去。我們留在家裹吧。

# 单元 36

# I used to

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/97265e498ebce78ea6ba6ef132619263bce77ab472885bebcb323506f5d4638c.jpg)

Dave used to work in a factory. Now he works in a supermarket.

戴夫過去曾在工廠裏工作,現時他在超級市場裏工作。

Dave used to work in a factory = 他以前曾在工廠裏工作,但現已不在那裏工作了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7756bc92f3255c032e8f7c6589ef1f74f313fe9d544f34b1701260d6742556ab.jpg)

可以用I used to work... / she used to have... / they used to be...等等:

<table><tr><td>I / you / we / they he / she / it</td><td>used to</td><td>be work have play 等等</td></tr></table>

When I was a child, I used to like chocolate.

小時候我喜愛吃巧克力。

I used to read a lot of books, but I don't read much these days. 我以前看很多書,但現在不怎看書了。

Liz has got short hair now, but it used to be very long.

利茲現在蓄短髮,但她以前的頭髮是很長的。

They used to live in the same street as us, so we used to see them a lot. But we don't see them very often these days.

他們曾跟我們同住在一條街道,所以以前我們經常碰見他們,但現在我們已不常見到他們了。

Helen used to have a piano, but she sold it a few years ago. 海倫曾擁有一座鋼琴,但數年前給她賣了。

否定式為I didn't use to...

When I was a child, I didn't use to like tomatoes. 我小時候並不喜愛吃番茄。

疑問式為did you use to...?

Where did you use to live before you came here? 你來到這裏之前曾住在哪兒?

used to 只用於表示過去發生的事情,即過去式,不能說'I use to...' (現在式):

I used to play tennis. These days I play golf. (不是 I use to play golf) 我以前打鋼球,但現在打高爾夫球。

We usually get up early. (不是 We use to get up early) 我們經常很早起。

# 罩数

there is... (there's)  is there...?  there is not... (there isn't or there's not)

There's a big tree in the garden. 花園裏有一棵大樹。  There's nothing on TV tonight. 今晚電視沒有甚麼好節目。  A: Have you got any money? 你有錢嗎？  B: Yes, there's some in my bag. 有，我的發裏有一些錢。  A: Excuse me, is there a hotel near here?  對不起，請問附近有酒店嗎？  B: Yes, there is. / No, there isn't.  有，附近有酒店。/附近沒有酒店。  We can't go skiing. There isn't any snow.  我們不能去滑雪，那裏一點雪也沒有。

# 穎數

there are...  are there...?  there are not... (there aren't)

There are some big trees in the garden. 花園裏有一些大樹。

There are a lot of accidents on this road. 這條路上有很多意外發生。

A: Are there any restaurants near here? 请問附近有餐廳嗎？

B: Yes, there are. / No, there aren't.

有，附近有一些餐廳。/附近沒有餐廳。

This restaurant is very quiet. There aren't many people here. 這餐廳十分寧靜，人不多。

How many players are there in a football team?

一隊足球隊內有多少個球員？

There are 11 players in a football team.

一隊足球隊內有11個球員。

# there was / were there will be

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b2932197a6c5d8bce2c02d0f37f111a58b522f2afc180ebe83cb79208f76cd86.jpg)

# 试比較

there is / are (現在式)

There is a good film on TV tonight. 今晚電視會播放一齣好電影。

We are staying at a very big hotel. There are 550 rooms. 我們住在十間非常宏偉的酒店裏，那裏有550間房間。

Are there any phone messages for me this morning? 今早有電話留言給我嗎？

I'm hungry, but there isn't anything to eat. 我肚子餓了，但沒有東西可以吃。

there was / were (過去式)

There was a good film on TV last night. 昨晚電視播放了一齣好電影。

We stayed at a very big hotel. There were 550 rooms. 我們曾住在一間非常宏偉的酒店裏，那裏有550間房間。

Were there any phone messages for me yesterday? 昨天有電話留言給我嗎？

I was hungry when I got home, but

there wasn't anything to eat.

我回到家後覺得餓，但沒有東西可以吃。

there has been / there have been (現在完成式)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fb8c9d97c311e312ac23e566ae8a15eeade007b0d166fa3137ce77ad73c5afac.jpg)

Look! There's been an accident.

(there's been = there has been) 看！那裏發生了意外。

This road is very dangerous. There have been many accidents. 這條路非常危險，已發生過很多意外。

# 試比較

There was an accident last night.

(不是 There has been an accident last night.) 昨晚發生了一宗意外。

有關簡單過去式和現在完成式，檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元20。

there will be

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/bbe7c02d1d7e9602512f1b37dd8845c19222da97e0767290ef58d88f9e7877e6.jpg)

Do you think there will be a lot of people at the party on Saturday? 你認為星期六的派對將會有很多人嗎？

The manager of the company is leaving, so there will be a new manager soon.

公司的經理將要離開，不久將會有一位新的經理上任。

I'm going away tomorrow. I'll do my packing today because there won't be time tomorrow.

there won't be = there will not be

我打算明天離開，今天我將要收拾行李，因為明天不會有時間了。

it用於表达時間/日子/距離/天氣:

日子

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9191768559311c9af4c8d2297fc6317ef06c82b509d51ba1ba4d067654d6eb91.jpg)

距離

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3db14403c6678909b9e0b157df3e2b997cb84d9b24a56d492e8d9cd399a45705.jpg)

It's three kilometres from our house to the city centre. 我們家離市中心三公里。  How far is it from New York to Los Angeles? 紐約離洛杉矶機有多遠？  It's a long way from here to the station. 由這裏至車站的路程很遠。  We can walk home. It isn't far. 我們可以步行回家，路程不算遠。

far 用於疑問句 (is it far?) 和否定句中 (it isn't far)。  a long way (it's a long way) 用於肯定句中。

天氣

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/484371070c309f925feda5074727c520f800b42cd323289738e3a24ff2753104.jpg)

It's raining. It isn't raining. Is it snowing? 正在下雨。 現在沒有下雨。 現在有沒有下雪？  It rains a lot here. It didn't rain yesterday. 這裏經常下雨。 昨天沒有下雨。

Does it snow very often? 是不是經常下雪？

It's warm / hot / cold / fine / cloudy / windy / sunny / foggy / dark etc. 天氣溫暖 / 炎熱 / 寒冷 / 很好 / 多雲 / 大風 / 晴朗 / 多霧 / 天色陰暗等等。  It's a nice day today. 今天天氣很好。

There is a lot of rain in winter. 冬天雨水很多。

It was very windy. 風很大。

There was a strong wind yesterday. 昨天飚起很強的風。

It's nice to... 等等

<table><tr><td>It&#x27;s</td><td>easy / difficult / impossible / dangerous / safe expensive / interesting / nice / wonderful / terrible 等等</td><td>to ...</td></tr></table>

It's impossible to understand her. 要了解她是不可能的。

It wasn't easy to find your house. 找你的房子實在不易。

It's raining again. (不是 Is raining again) 又再下雨了。

Is it true that you're going away? (不是 Is true that ...) 你是不是真的要離開？

# I am, I don't 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d3cdb8d24c012e881c8f8e76bd58c15a1a7432fdd259770aa4c307771572fb75.jpg)

She isn't tired, but he is. (he is = he is tired) 她不覺得疲倦,但他感到疲倦。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1f1ea8d8e035f22973b49cf48cd4a06997d244b5405c8058b52c5ca19517254f.jpg)

He likes tea, but she doesn't. (she doesn't = she doesn't like tea) 他喜歡喝茶,但她不喜歡。

在這些例子中,不必重複'tired'、'like tea'等話語。

下列動词也可像上面的例子一樣以同樣方式表達:

am / is / are I haven't got a car, but my sister has. (= my sister has got a car) was / were 我沒有汽車,但我妹妹有。

have / has A: Please help me. 請你幫忙。

do / does / did B: I'm sorry. I can't. (= I can't help you) 對不起,我不能。

can A: Are you tired? 你覺得疲倦嗎?

will B: I was, but I'm not now. (= I was tired, but I'm not tired now) 我之前覺得,但現在並不倦了。

might 我之前覺得,但現在並不倦了。

must A: Do you think Jane will phone this evening? 你認為今晚珍會打電話來嗎?

B: She might. (= she might phone) 她可能會。

注意 B: Yes, I'm afraid I must. (= I must go) 是的,我必須要離開。

在下列情況不可以用'm / 's / 've 等等縮約式,必須用am / is / have 等等:

She isn't tired, but he is. (不是... but he's) 她不覺得疲倦,但他感到疲倦。

但可以用isn't/haven't/won't等等否定的縮約式:

My sister has got a car, but I haven't. 我妹妹有一輛汽車,但我沒有。

'Are you and Jane working tomorrow?' 'I am, but Jane isn't.'

「你和珍明天要工作嗎？」「我要,但珍不用。」

在Yes和No之後可以用Iam/I'mnot等等:

'Are you tired?' 'Yes, I am. / No, I'm not.' 「你覺得疲倦嗎？」「我覺得。」我不覺得。」

'Will Alan be here tomorrow?' 'Yes, he will. / No, he won't.' 「艾倫明天會在這裏嗎？」「他會。」他不會。」

'Is there a bus to the airport?' 'Yes, there is. / No, there isn't.' 「有巴士到機場的嗎？」「有。」沒有。」

do / does 用於現在式: 檢索 → 單元 6- 7

I don't like hot weather, but Sue does. (= Sue likes hot weather) 我不喜歡炎熱的天氣,但蘇喜歡。

Sue works hard, but I don't. (= I don't work hard) 蘇動力工作,但我卻不。

'Do you enjoy your work?' 'Yes, I do.' 「你喜歡自己的工作嗎？」「我喜歡。」

did 用於過去式: 檢索 → 單元 12

A: Did you and Chris enjoy the film? 你和克里斯喜歡那電影嗎?

B: I did, but Chris didn't. (= I enjoyed it, but Chris didn't enjoy it) 我喜歡,但克里斯卻不。

'I had a good time.' 'I did too.' (= I enjoyed it too) 「我度過了快樂的時光。」「我也是。」

'Did it rain yesterday?' 'No, it didn't.' 「昨天有下雨嗎？」「沒有。」

# 练習

# 40.1 每次只可用一個動詞(例如is/have/can等等)完成下列句子。

1 Kate wasn't hungry, but we were 4 I haven't seen the film, but Tom 2 I'm not married, but my brother 5 Karen won't be here, but Chris 3 Bill can't help you, but I 6 You weren't late, but I

# 40.2 用否定式的動詞(例如isn't/haven't/can't等等)完成下列句子。

1 My sister can play the piano, but I can't 4 Mark has been to China, but I 2 Sam is working today, but I 5 I'm ready to go, but Tom 3 I was working, but my friends 6 I've got a key, but Sally

# 40.3 用do/does/did或don't/doesn't/didn't完成下列句子。

1 I don't like hot weather, but Sue does.

2 Sue likes hot weather, but I don't

3 My mother wears glasses, but my father

4 You don't know Paul very well, but I

5 I didn't enjoy the party, but my friends

6 I don't watch TV much, but Peter

7 Kate lives in London, but her parents

8 You had breakfast this morning, but I

B: No, but Sandra She went there on holiday last year.

# Have you? Are you? Don't you? 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/072bfc476516a7ffd84b25aaf6a524a9964d3e9ded8ab92736a78548b60c21bd.jpg)

have you? / is it? / can't he? 等等可用於表示感興趣或驚訝：

You're late. 'Oh, am I? I'm sorry. 「你遲到了。」「噢，我遲了嗎？對不起。」  I was ill last week. 'Were you? I didn't know that. 「我上星期病倒了。」「是嗎？我不知道。」  It's raining again. 'Is it? It was sunny ten minutes ago. 「又再下雨了。」「是嗎？十分鐘前還是陽光普照的。」  'There's a letter for you. 'Is there? Where is it? 「有一封信是給你的。」「是嗎？在哪裏？」  'Bill can't drive. 'Can't he? I didn't know that. 「比爾不懂得駕駛。」「是嗎？我並不知道。」  'I'm not hungry. 'Aren't you? I am. 「我不覺得餓。」「是嗎？我覺得餓。」  'Sue isn't at work today. 'Isn't she? Is she ill? 「蘇今天沒有上班。」「是嗎？她是否病了？」

do / does 用於簡單現在式，did 用於簡單過去式：

'I speak four languages. 'Do you? Which ones? 「我懂得說四種語言。」「是嗎？你懂得哪些語言？」  'Tim doesn't eat meat. 'Doesn't he? Does he eat fish? 「蒂姆不吃肉。」「是嗎？他吃魚嗎？」  'Nicole got married last week. 'Did she? Really? 「尼科爾上星期結婚了。」「是嗎？真的嗎？」

# B 附加疑問

have you? / is it? / can't she? 等等可用於句子的末端。這些簡略問句就是附加疑問。

# 注意

肯定句：後加否定附加疑問  否定句：後加肯定附加疑問

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/776f834418bf191de3997d5852e3c99bc6be26bc5f03dbc804527df1f6618eed.jpg)

# 肯定句  $\rightarrow$  否定附加疑問

It's a beautiful day, isn't it? 今天天氣很好，是嗎？  Sally lives in London, doesn't she?  莎莉住在倫敦，對嗎？  You closed the window, didn't you?  你把窗子關上了，是嗎？  Those shoes are nice, aren't they?  那些鞋子很漂亮，是嗎？  Tom will be here soon, won't he?  湯姆即將來到，是嗎？

# 否定句  $\rightarrow$  肯定附加疑問

That isn't your car, is it?  那不是你的車吧，是嗎？  You haven't met my mother, have you?  你從未見過我的母親吧？  Sally doesn't go out much, doesn't she?  莎莉並不常常出去，是嗎？  You won't be late, will you?  你不會遲到吧？

Yes, it's perfect.  是的，天氣很怡人。  Yes, that's right.  對。  Yes, I think so.  我想是的。  Yes, very nice.  是的，很漂亮。  Yes, probably.  可能吧。

No, it's my mother's.  這是我媽媽的車。  No, I haven't.  從未見過。  No, she doesn't.  她不常常出去。  No, I'm never late.  我從不遲到的。

# 41.4 用肯定附加疑問（例如is it? / do you? 等等）或否定附加疑問（例如isn't it? / don't you? 等等），完成下列句子。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1e9344bb2274b0844a509c20d8e40863566ba0f3ba2ff4f0c8ab319636135963.jpg)

# too / either so am I / neither do I 等等

too 和 either

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/bfacdf8f5dc4bbbc095e9dff5192d95d4637c3ff374eda4497751dc35132d23d.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6a9695fb3d23fed635a1974c8b5e29732378ecdeb9b79b51c7dbd35cbe9e59ac.jpg)

too 和 either 用於句末。

too 用於肯定動詞之後:

A: I'm happy. 我很開心。  B: I'm happy too. 我也很開心。

A: I enjoyed the film. 我喜歡那電影。  B: I enjoyed it too. 我也喜歡。

Jane is a doctor. Her husband is a doctor too. 珍是一位醫生,她的丈夫也是一位醫生。

either 用於否定動詞之後:

A: I'm not happy. 我不開心。  B: I'm not happy either. (不是 I'm not ... too) 我也不開心。

A: I can't cook. 我不懂烹饪。  B: I can't either. (不是 I can't too) 我也不懂。

Bill doesn't watch TV. He doesn't read

newspapers either. 比爾不看電視,也不看報紙。

so am I / neither do I 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/54e9e0183ff87e39bc040fc910933a6a440545112ad60159b3dcf38b53716382.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/caafbfe31c0ec6560e3c17890b7b2e0227dcdb7492a762d54471b5f1661077ae.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/002c8d7673eddcaf6e4b3bef3c913212bb6541a0b4b265ee699052cefb2e4cf0.jpg)

so am  $\mathbf{I} = \mathbf{I}$  am too

so have  $\mathbf{I} = \mathbf{I}$  have too 等等:

A: I'm working. 我在工作。  B: So am I. (= I'm working too) 我也在工作。

A: I was late for work today. 今天我上班遲到了。

B: So was Sam. (= Sam was late too) 森姆也是。

A: I work in a bank. 我在銀行裏工作。  B: So do I. 我也是。

A: We went to the cinema last night. 昨晚我們去看電影。

昨晚我們去看電影。

B: Did you? So did we. 是嗎?我們也是。

A: I'd like to go to Australia. 我想到澳洲去。

B: So would I. 我也想。

neither am  $\mathbf{I} = \mathbf{I}$  'm not either

neither can  $\mathbf{I} = \mathbf{I}$  can't either 等等:

A: I haven't got a key. 我沒有鑰匙。

B: Neither have I. (= I haven't either) 我也沒有。

我也没有。

A: Kate can't cook. 奶特不懂烹饪。

B: Neither can Tom. (= Tom can't either) 湯姆也不懂。

A: I won't (= will not) be here tomorrow. 明天我不會在這裏。

B: Neither will I. 我也不會。

A: I never go to the cinema. 我從不去看電影。

B: Neither do I. 我也不去。

可以用 Nor (= Neither):

A: I'm not married. 我是未婚的。  B: Nor am I. 或 Neither am I. 我也是未婚的。

# 注意

緊記用 So am I (不是 So I am)、Neither have I (不是 Neither I have) 等等。
