42.3 你正在跟瑪莉亞（Maria）對話。試在適當時用So...I或Neither...I寫出有關你自己的句子。先細閱下列例句：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8b943d164cc6d7e9655a1dd8ad842ce918852cdf9398dc2ecacf27dd7d917cb2.jpg)

回答時可說： So am I. 或 I'm not.

回答時可說： Neither do I. 或 I do.

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b0005093dbc12cd77bdda91867b8c567dc8f3d7965fc8b10cacee7b2e2ad0662.jpg)

# isn't, haven't, don't 等等否定式(negatives)

A

not(n't)用於否定句中:

肯定句  $\rightarrow$  否定句

am am not (m not) is is not (isnt 或 's not) are are not (aren't 或 're not) was was not (wasn't) were were not (weren't) have have not (haven't) has has not (hasn't) will will not (won't) can can not (can't) could could not (couldn't) must must not (mustn't) should should not (shouldn't) would would not (wouldn't)

I'm not tired.我不覺得疲倦。

It isn't (或 It's not) raining. 現在沒有下雨。

They aren't (或 They're not) here. 他們不在這裏。

Julian wasn't hungry. 朱利安不覺得餓。

The shops weren't open. 商店沒有開門營業。

I haven't finished my work. 我還沒有完成我的工作。

Sue hasn't got a car. 蘇沒有汽車。

We won't be here tomorrow. 明天我們不會在這裏。

George can't drive. 喬治不懂得駕駛。

I couldn't sleep last night. 昨晚我不能入睡。

I mustn't forget to phone Jane. 我必須記得致電給珍。

You shouldn't work so hard. 你不應工作得那麼辛苦。

I wouldn't like to be an actor. 我不想成為演員。

don't / doesn't / didn't

簡單現在式否定句:

簡單過去式否定句:

<table><tr><td>I / we / you / they</td><td>do not (don&#x27;t)</td><td rowspan="3">work / live / go 等等</td></tr><tr><td>he / she / it</td><td>does not (doesn&#x27;t)</td></tr><tr><td>I / they / he / she 等等</td><td>did not (didn&#x27;t)</td></tr></table>

肯定句  $\rightarrow$  否定句

I want to go out. 我想外出。 They work hard. 他們努力工作。 Liz plays the guitar. 利茲懂得彈吉他。 My father likes his job. 我的爸爸喜歡他的工作。

I got up early this morning. 今晨我很早起。 They worked hard yesterday. 昨天他們努力工作。

We played tennis. 我們打網球。

Diane had dinner with us. 黨安和我們一起吃晚飯。

I don't want to go out. 我不想外出。

They don't work hard. 他們不努力工作。

Liz doesn't play the guitar. 利茲不懂得彈吉他。

My father doesn't like his job.

我的爸爸不喜歡他的工作。

I didn't get up early this morning.

今晨我並不早起。

They didn't work hard yesterday.

昨天他們並不努力工作。

We didn't play tennis. 我們沒有打網球。

Diane didn't have dinner with us.

當安沒有和我們一起吃晚飯。

Don't...用於下列情況:

<table><tr><td>Look! 看!</td><td>Don&#x27;t look!</td><td>別看!</td></tr><tr><td>Wait for me. 等我一會。</td><td>Don&#x27;t wait for me. 不用等我了。</td><td></td></tr></table>

有時 do 會作為主要動詞用 (例如 don't do / doesn't do / didn't do) :

Do something! 做一點事!  $\rightarrow$  Don't do anything! 甚麼也不要做!

Sue does a lot at weekends.  $\rightarrow$  Sue doesn't do much at weekends.

蘇在週末時做很多事情。蘇在週末時並沒甚麼事做。

I did what you said.  $\rightarrow$  I didn't do what you said.

我照你所說的做了。我沒有照你所說的去做。

He prefers to talk on the phone. 5 We can walk to the station from here. It very far. 6 'Where's Jane?' 'I know. I seen her today.' 7 Be careful! fall! 8 We went to the cinema last night. I like the film very much. 9 I've been to Japan many times, but I been to Korea. 10 Julia be here tomorrow. She's going away. 11 'Who broke that window?' 'Not me. I do it.' 12 We didn't see what happened. We looking at the time. 13 Lisa bought a new coat a few days ago, but she worn it yet. 14 You drive so fast. It's dangerous.

# is it...? have you...? do they...? 等等 疑問句1(questions 1)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/966f16a3a9ae265f5d9e1a63674a46f556d82136d0eaf19bdc072dd563fd9c3c.jpg)

在疑問句中,第一個動詞(例如is/are/have等等)是放在主語前的:

肯定句

主語+動詞

疑問句

動词+主語

I am late.我遇到了。

That seat is free.那座位是没有人坐的。

She was angry.她感到愤怒。

David has gone.大衛已經走了。

You have got a car.你有一輛汽車。

They will be here soon.他們快將到這裹來。

Paula can swim.葆拉懂得游泳。

Am I late?我是否遇到了？Is that seat free?那個座位有人坐的？Why was she angry?她為何感到愤怒？Where has David gone?大衛往哪裹去了？Have you got a car?你有没有汽車？When will they be here?他們何時會到這裹來？Can Paula swim?葆拉懂得游泳？

注意

序：第一個動詞放在主語前：

Where has David gone?（不是Where has gone David?）大衛往哪裹去了？

Are those people waiting for something?（不是Are waiting...？）那些人是否在等待甚麼？

When was the telephone invented?（不是When was invented...？）電話是何時發明的？

do...?/does...?/did...?

<table><tr><td rowspan="3">簡單現在式疑問句：</td><td>do</td><td>I / we / you / they</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>does</td><td>he / she / it</td><td rowspan="2">work / live / go 等等...?</td></tr><tr><td>did</td><td>I / they / he / she 等等</td></tr></table>

肯定句

疑問句

They work hard.他們努力工作。

You watch television.你看電視。

Chris works hard.克里斯努力工作。

She gets up early.她很早起。

They worked hard.他們努力工作。

You had dinner.你吃了晚餐。

She got up early.她很早起了。

Do they

How often do you

Does Chris

What time does she

work hard?他們是否努力工作？

watch television?你多久看一次電視？

work hard?克里斯是否努力工作？

get up?她甚時候起林？

Did they

What did you

What time did she

work hard?他們努力工作？

have for dinner?你晚餐吃了甚？

get up?她甚時候起了？

有時do會作為主要動词使用（例如doyoudo/didhedo等等）：

What do you usually do at weekends? 週末時你經常做甚？

What does your brother do?' 'He works in a bank.'「你哥哥的工作是甚？」他在銀行裹工作。」

I broke my finger last week.'How did you do that?'（不是How did you that?）

「上星期我的手指断了。」「你怎樣弄傷的？」

Why isn't...? / Why don't...?等等（Why + 否定式）：

Where's John? Why isn't he here?（不是Why he isn't here?）約翰在哪裹？他為何不在這裹？

Why can't Paula come to the meeting tomorrow?（不是Why Paula can't...？）

為何葆拉明天不能來聚會？

Why didn't you phone me last night? 你為何昨晚不致電給我？

(and you?) 6 I'm going out this evening. (and Paul?) 7 I like my job. (and you?) 8 I live near here. (and Nicole?) 9 I enjoyed the film. (and you?) 10 I had a good holiday. (and you?)

# 44.4 完成下列的疑問句。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/455f53f3b85ee1291b15d17ebca2c7ccfa3a59bb4601984d24135c5c2229bad5.jpg)

# Who saw you? Who did you see? 疑問句2(questions 2)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dde8a6d51b5423cdbc329ce66eee7495e6aea304e306807d3d2eb640d2afa36d.jpg)

在以下疑問句中,who/what是主語:

Who lives in this house? (= somebody lives in it—who?) (不是Who does live?)

誰住在這屋子裏?

What happened? (= something happened—what?) (不是What did happen?)

發生了甚麼事?

What's happening? (What's = What is) 正在發生甚麼事?

Who's got my key? (Who's = Who has) 誰人拿去我的鑰匙?

在以下疑問句中,who/what是賓語:

Who did you meet yesterday? (= you met somebody—who?) 昨天你遇見誰?

What did Paul say? (= Paul said something—what?) 保羅說了甚麼?

Who are you phoning? 你在致電給誰?

What was Silvia wearing? 西爾維亞身上穿戴着甚麼?

試比較 主語和賓語的用法:

George likes oranges. → 主語: Who likes oranges? — George.

喬治喜歡吃橙。 誰人喜歡吃橙?—喬治。

賓語: What does George like? — Oranges.

喬治喜歡吃甚麼?—橙。

Jane won a new car. → 主語: Who won a new car? — Jane.

珍贏了一輛新車。 誰人贏了一輛新車?—珍。

賓語: What did Jane win? — A new car.

珍贏了甚麼?—一輛新車。

# Who is she talking to? What is it like? 疑問句3(questions 3)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/47f1b08f556e6ce53811fa07fe76e79370fb7633624c85dec6333c22d5b0ac2b.jpg)

Julia is talking to somebody. 茱莉亞正在跟某人談話。

Who is she talking to? 她在跟誰人談話？

介詞放於句末

以Who...?/What...?/Where...?/Which...?開頭的疑問句，介詞（例如to/from／with等等）通常會放於句末：

'Where are you from?' 'I'm from Thailand.' 「你從哪裏來？」「我從泰國來？」

'Jack was afraid.' 'What was he afraid of?' 「傑克感到害怕。」「他害怕甚麼？」

'Who do these books belong to?' 'They're mine.' 「這些書是屬於誰的？」「是我的。」

'Tom's father is in hospital.' 'Which hospital is he in?'

「湯姆的父親在醫院裏。」「他在哪間醫院裏？」

'Kate is going on holiday.' 'Who with?' / 'Who is she going with?'

「凱特打算去度假。」「她打算跟誰去度假？」

'Can we talk?' 'Sure. What do you want to talk about?'

「我們可以談談嗎？」「可以的。你想跟我談甚麼？」

What's it like? / What are they like? 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/34dea436735e72cf082796677592efb47b65b14c2be4d446117d0fc464e7739f.jpg)

What's it like? = What is it like?

What's it like? = 告訴我關於某事物的情況——是好還是壞？是大還是小？是新還是舊？等等。

注意

在'What is it like?'中，like是介詞，它並不是動詞like（例如'Do you like your new house? 等等）。

A: There's a new restaurant in our street. 在我們住的街道上有一間新餐廳。

B: What's it like? Is it good? 是怎麼樣的？好不好？

A: I don't know. I haven't eaten there yet. 我不知道，我沒有在那裏吃過飯。

A: What's your new teacher like? 你的新老師怎麼樣？

B: She's very good. We learn a lot. 她十分好，我們學到很多東西。

A: I met Nicole's parents yesterday. 昨天我遇見尼科爾的父母。

B: Did you? What are they like? 是嗎？他們是怎麼樣的？

A: They're very nice. 他們很和善。

A: Did you have a good holiday? What was the weather like? 你的假期愉快嗎？天氣怎樣？

B: It was lovely. It was sunny every day. 天氣很好，每天都陽光普照。

# What ...? Which ...? How ...? 疑問句4（questions 4）

What + 名詞（例如What colour...？/What kind...？等等）：

What colour is your car? What colour are your eyes? 你的車是甚颜色？ 你的眼睛是甚颜色？ What size is this shirt? What make is your TV set? 這衫是甚尺碼？ 你的電視機是甚品牌？

What time is it? 現在是甚時間？ What day is it today? 今天是星期

What kind of job do you want? （或What type of job...？/What sort of job...？）你想做甚麽原型的工作？

What之後可以没有名词：

What's your favourite colour? What do you want to do tonight? 你最喜爱甚颜色？ / 天晚上你想做甚麽？

Which + 名詞（用於指事物或人物）：

Which train did you catch — the 9:50 or the 10:30? 你趕上了哪一班火車——是9時50分的還是10時30分的？ Which doctor did you see — Doctor Ellis, Doctor Gray or Doctor Hill? 你看了哪位醫生——埃利斯醫生、格雷醫生還是希爾醫生？

# 注意

如which之後没有名词，则只可用於指事物，不能用於指人物：

Which is bigger — Canada or Australia? 哪個較大——加拿大還是澳洲？

Who用於指人物（who之後没有名词）：

Who is taller — Joe or Gary? （不是Which is taller?）誰人較高——喬還是加里？

What或which？

當考虑到少数量的可能性（可能是兩個、三個或四個）時，應該用which：

We can go this way or that way. 我們可以朝着這個方向或那個方向走。

Which way shall we go? 我們應往哪一方走？

There are four umbrellas here. 這裹有四把雨伞。 Which is yours? 哪一把是你的？

What用於一般情况：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1b998bdcc5648d6cd8a40f3b7176b89a58264547b2d32f316380cad510e4931a.jpg)  
WHICH?

What's the capital of Argentina? (of all the cities in Argentina) 阿根廷的首都是甚麼？ What sort of music do you like? (of all kinds of music) 你喜歡哪類型音樂？

# 注意

What和Which的用法：

What colour are his eyes? （不是Which colour?）他的眼睛是甚顏色？Which colour do you prefer, pink or yellow? 你寧選選哪一種顏色，粉紅色還是黃色？What is the longest river in the world? 世界上最長的河流是甚麼？Which is the longest river — the Mississippi, the Amazon or the Nile? 哪一條是最長的河流——密西西比河、亞馬遜河還是尼羅河？

D How...?

How was the party last night? 'It was great.' 「昨晚的派對怎樣？」「很精彩。」 'How do you usually go to work?' 'By bus.' 「你經常是怎樣上班的？」「乘巴士。」

How + 形容詞（副詞（例如how tall / how old / how often 等等）：

tall are you?' 'I'm 1 metre 70. ' 「你有多高？」「我有1.7米高。」 big is the house?' 'Not very big.' 「那房子有多大？」「不是很大。」 old is your mother?' 'She's 45. ' 「你媽媽年紀有多大？」「她現年45。」 far is it from here to the airport?' 'Five kilometres.' 「由這裏到機場有多遠？」「5公里。」 often do you use your car?' 'Every day.' 「你隔多久會使用你的汽車一次？」「每天。」 long have they been married?' 'Ten years.' 「他們結了婚多久？」「10年了。」 much was the meal?' 'Thirty pounds.' 「那頓飯要多少錢？」「30英鎊。」

# How long does it take ...?

How long does it take from ... to ...?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e0a2e9a33ae822d563a568b4278489b1fca0a56084003fc2bfc3d218edfc5d27.jpg)

How long does it take by plane from New York to Washington? 由紐約乘飛機至華盛頓需時多久？

It takes an hour.

要一小時。

How long does it take by train from London to Manchester? 由倫敦乘火车至曼徹斯特需時多久？

It takes two hours by train from London to Manchester. 由倫敦乘火车至曼徹斯特需要兩小時。

How long does it take by car from your house to the station? 由你的住所乘汽車至車站需時多久？

It takes ten minutes by car from my house to the station. 由我的住所乘汽車至車站需要10分鐘。

How long does it take to do something?

How long does it take to ...?

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">How long</td><td>takes
took
will take</td><td>a week
a long time
three hours</td><td>to ...</td></tr><tr><td>doesn&#x27;t
didn&#x27;t
won&#x27;t</td><td>take</td><td>long</td></tr></table>

How long does it take to cross the Atlantic by ship? 乘船横渡大西洋需時多久？

I came by train. 'Did you? How long did it take (to get here)?

我乘火车來的。[是嗎？(到這裏來)需時多久？]

How long will it take to get from here to the hotel? 從這裏到酒店將需要多少時間？

It takes a long time to learn a language. 帶智一種語言需要很長時間。

It doesn't take long to cook an omelette. 煎奄列需時不久。

It won't take long to fix the computer. 修理這電腦不需花很多時間。

How long does it take you to do something?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f16732f3e216ee1ad8df909917bb10433ae9bc60ec76513e1ea6b48c992b6a49.jpg)

<table><tr><td>How long</td><td>does did will</td><td>it take</td><td>you Tom them</td><td>to ...?</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>It</td><td>takes
took
will take</td><td>me
Tom
them</td><td>a week
a long time
three hours</td><td>to ...</td></tr></table>

I started reading the book on Monday. 我由星期一開始閱讀那本書。

I finished it on Wednesday evening. 我在星期三傍晚讀完。

It took me three days to read it. 我用了三天時間閱讀那本書。

How long will it take me to learn to drive? 我需要花多少時間才能學懂駕駛呢？

It takes Tom 20 minutes to get to work in the morning. 湯姆早上到公司需時20分鐘。

It took us an hour to do the shopping. 我們花了一小時購物。

Did it take you a long time to find a job? 你是否花了很長時間來找工作？

It will take me an hour to cook dinner. 我將會用上一小時來預備晚飯。

單元 49

# Do you know where ...? I don't know what ... 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c1f98b2e24f950d0fac50126c00293620a6ab06762dd5489f7131f4597edce78.jpg)

我們會說： Where is Paula? 菜拉在哪裏？

但：Do you know where Paula is?

（不是Do you know where is Paula?）你知道葆拉在哪裏嗎？

同樣地，我們應該說：

I know I don't know Can you tell me

我知道葆拉在哪裏。我不知道葆拉在哪裏。你可否告訴我葆拉在哪裏？

試比較 以下兩種句式及其詞序的分別：

Who are those people? 那些人是誰？How old is Nicole? 尼科爾的年紀有多大？What time is it? 現在是甚麼時間？Where can I go? 我可以往哪裏去？

How much is this camera? 這部照相機要多少錢？When are you going away? 你打算何時離去？Where have they gone? 他們往哪裏去了？What was Kate wearing? 凱特身上穿戴着甚麼？

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">但</td><td>Do you know Can you tell me</td><td rowspan="2">who those people are how old Nicole is what time it is where I can go how much this camera is when you’re going away where they have gone what Kate was wearing</td></tr><tr><td>I know I don’t know I don’t remember</td></tr></table>

疑問句中包含do／does／did（簡單現在式和簡單過去式）：

Where does he live? 他住在哪裏？

但 Do you know where he lives? （不是Do you know where does he live?）你知道他住在哪裏嗎？

你知道他住在哪裏嗎？

試比較 以下兩種句式及其詞序的分別：

How do aeroplanes fly? 飛機怎樣飛行？What does Jane want? 珍想要甚麼？Why did she go home? 她為何回家去？Where did I put the key? 我把鑰匙放在哪裏？

<table><tr><td rowspan="4">但</td><td>Do you know</td><td>how aeroplanes fly</td><td>?</td></tr><tr><td>I don’t know</td><td>what Jane wants</td><td>.</td></tr><tr><td>I don’t remember</td><td rowspan="2">why she went home</td><td rowspan="2">where I put the key</td></tr><tr><td>I know</td></tr></table>

以Is...?／Do...?／Can...？等等開始的疑問句（yes／no疑問句）：

試比較 以下兩種句式及其詞序的分別：

Is Jack at home? 傑克是否在家裏？Have they got a car? 他們有沒有汽車？Can Brian swim? 布賴恩懂得游泳嗎？Do they live near here? 他們住在附近嗎？Did anybody see you? 有人看見你嗎？

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">但</td><td>Do you know</td><td rowspan="2">Jack is at home if they’ve got a car or whether they live near here anybody saw you</td><td>?</td></tr><tr><td>I don’t know</td><td>.</td></tr></table>

if或whether可用於以下句子中：

Do you know if they've got a car? 或 Do you know whether they've got a car? 你知道他們有沒有汽車嗎？I don't know if anybody saw me. 或 I don't know whether anybody saw me. 我不知道有沒有人看見我。

# 睿

49.1 用I don't know where / when / why ... 等等回答下列問題。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/35ce2caa4b7424303f7e4132b6c160d183ab3da6dd0d882e7734c78d592ce6ad.jpg)

# 49.5 用Do you know...？寫出相關的疑問句。

# She said that ... He told me that ...

上星期你参加了一個派對。你的很多朋友也有出席，以下是他們跟你說過的話：

今天你遇見保羅。你告訴他有關派對的事情，並把朋友們所說的話轉述給他知道。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/bc4710f2b97f78a58c51ec4578cd43c6f4e15a2da184cce1cda2f3a05c23b286.jpg)

am  $\rightarrow$  was is

Diane said that she was enjoying her new job. 黨安說她喜歡她的新工作。 She said that her father wasn't well. 她說她的爸爸身體不太好。

are  $\rightarrow$  were

Sarah and Tim said that they were going to buy a house. 薩拉和蒂姆說他們打算買一間房子。

have  $\rightarrow$  had has

can  $\rightarrow$  could

Peter said that he had to leave early. 彼得說他必須提早離去。 He said that his sister had gone to Australia. 他說他的妹妹去了澳洲。

Kate said that she couldn't find a job. 凱特說她找不到工作。

will  $\rightarrow$  would

Steve said that he would phone me. 史蒂夫說他將會致電給我。

do  $\rightarrow$  did

Rachel said that she didn't like her job. 雷切爾說她不喜歡自己的工作。

She said that her son didn't like school. 她說她的兒子不喜歡上學。

look  $\rightarrow$  looked

Mike said that I looked tired. 適克說我面露倦容。

feel  $\rightarrow$  felt 等等 现在式

I said that I felt fine. 我說我很好。

# say and tell

say  $\rightarrow$  said)

He said that he was tired. (不是 He said me) 他說他感到疲倦。 What did she say to you? (不是 say you) 她跟你說甚麼？

tell  $\rightarrow$  told)

He told me that he was tired. (不是 He told that...) 他告訴我他感到疲倦。 What did she tell you? (不是 tell to you) 她告訴你甚麼？

# 注意

我們說：he said to me、 I said to Ann 等等。 不要說：'he said me'、'I said Ann'。

# 注意

我們說：he told me、 I told Ann 等等。 不要說：'he told to me'、'I told to Ann'。

# 可以在句中加上 that...：

He said that he was tired. 或 He said he was tired. (不用 that) 他說他感到疲倦。 Kate told me that she couldn't find a job. 或 Kate told me she couldn't find a job. 剿特告訴我她找不到工作。

# 檢索

I told you to...  $\Rightarrow$  第五53

# 单元 51

# work / working go / going do / doing

work/go/be等等（不定式）：

不定式跟will/can/must等等一起用：

will shall might may can could must should would

Anna will be here soon. 安娜快將來到這裹。  $\Rightarrow$  單元27- 28 Shall I open the window? 我開窗好嗎？ I might phone you later. 我可能晚些時候會致電給你。  $\Rightarrow$  單元29 May I sit here? 我可否坐在這裹？ I can't meet you tomorrow. 明天我不能見你。  $\Rightarrow$  單元30 Could you pass the salt, please? 請你把鹽傳過來好嗎？ I's late. I must go now. 已很晚了，我現在一定要走了。  $\Rightarrow$  單元31 You shouldn't work so hard. 你不應工作得這麼辛勞。  $\Rightarrow$  單元32 Would you like some coffee? 你想要一些咖啡嗎？  $\Rightarrow$  單元34

不定式跟do/does/did一起用：

do/does (簡單現在式)

Do you work? 你有工作嗎？

They don't work very hard. 他們不是太努力工作。

Helen doesn't know many people. 海倫認識的人不多。

How much does it cost? 遺東西要多少錢？

did

(簡單過去式)

What time did the train leave? 那班火車是甚麼時候離開了的？  $\Rightarrow$  單元12

We didn't sleep well. 我們睡得不好。

to work/to go/to be等等（to  $^+$  不定式）：

(I'm) going to ...

(I) have to ...

(I) want to ...

(I) would like to ...

(I) used to ...

I'm going to play tennis tomorrow. 明天我打算打網球。  $\Rightarrow$  單元26

What are you going to do? 你打算做甚麼呢？

I have to go now. 我現在要走了。

Everybody has to eat. 每個人都必須吃東西。

Do you want to go out? 你想外出嗎？

They don't want to come with us. 他們不想跟我們一起來。

I'd like to talk to you. 我想跟你談談。

Would you like to go out? 你想外出嗎？

Dave used to work in a factory. 戴夫以前在工廠裏工作。  $\Rightarrow$  單元36

working / going / playing 等等：

am/is/are- - ing (現在進行式)

was/were+ - ing (過去進行式)

Please be quiet. I'm working. 請保持肅靜，我正在工作  $\Rightarrow$  單元3- 4,8,25

Tom isn't working today. 湯姆今天不上班。

What time are you going out? 你打算何時外出？

It was raining, so we didn't go out.

因為下雨，所以我們沒有外出。

What were you doing when the phone rang?

電話響起時你在做甚麼？

do / doing get / getting sleep / sleeping watch / watching eat / eating go / going stay / staying wear / wearing fly / flying listen / listening wait / waiting work / working

11 'What are you tonight?' 'I'm at home.'

51.3 寫出下列動詞的正確形式，例如：

不定式（例如 work / go 等等）或

to ...（例如 to work / to go 等等）或

- ing（例如 working / going 等等）

1 Shall I ...even the window? (open)

2 It's late. I have ...to go now. (go)

3 Amanda isn't ...working this week. She's on holiday. (work)

4 I'm tired. I don't want out. (go)

5 It might , so take an umbrella with you. (rain)

6 What time do you have tomorrow morning? (leave)

7 I'm sorry I can't you. (help)

8 My brother is a student. He's physics. (study)

9 Would you like on a trip round the world? (go)

10 When you saw Maria, what was she ? (wear)

11 When you go to London, where are you going ? (stay)

12 I'm hungry. I must something to eat. (have)

13 'Where's Gary?' 'He's a bath.' (have)

14 I used a car, but I sold it last year. (have)

# to ... (I want to do) 和 -ing (I enjoy doing)

动词 + to ... (I want to do)

want plan decide try hope expect offer forget need promise refuse learn

+ to ... (to do / to work / to be 等等)

What do you want to do this evening? 今晚你想做甚麽呢?

It's not very late. We don't need to go home yet. 现在未算很晚, 我們仍無需回家。

Tina has decided to sell her car. 带娜已决定賣掉她的汽車。

You forgot to switch off the light when you went out. 你外出時忘了關燈。

My brother is learning to drive. 我的弟弟正學習駕駛。

I tried to read my book, but I was too tired. 我嘗試看書, 但我實在太疲倦了。

B 动词 + - ing (I enjoy doing)

enjoy stop + - ing (doing / working / being 等等) mind finish

I enjoy dancing. (不是 enjoy to dance) 我享受跳舞之樂。

I don't mind getting up early. 我不介意早起。

Has it stopped raining? 是否已經停雨?

Sonia suggested going to the cinema. 桑妮亞提議去看電影。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/06002704fd65c85e21d1e7cce41a39a4da614aad03e8652d5933f50b8d45f44a.jpg)

动词 + - ing 或 to ...

like love start continue + - ing (doing 等等) 或 to ... (to do 等等) prefer hate begin

Do you like getting up early? 或 Do you like to get up early? 你喜歡早起嗎?

I prefer travelling by car. 或 I prefer to travel by car. 我寧願乘汽車旅行。

Anna loves dancing. 或 Anna loves to dance. 安娜喜歡跳舞。

I hate being late. 或 I hate to be late. 我討厭遲到。

It started raining. 或 It started to rain. 開始下雨了。

would like to ... 等等

would like would love + to ... (to do / to work / to be 等等) would prefer would hate

Julia would like to meet you. 英莉亞想見見你。

I'd love to go to Australia. (I'd = I would) 我想到澳洲去。

'Would you like to sit down?' 'No, I'd prefer to stand, thank you.'

「你想坐下來嗎？」「不用了,我寧願站着,謝謝你。」

I like this city very much. I wouldn't like to move.

我非常喜愛這個城市。我不想搬遷。

I'd hate to lose my address book. 我不願遺失我的地址簿。

# I want you to ... I told you to ...

A I want you to ...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7ece5ff69a2a646338060ac7ab05c1f16aa3c4c40851068495e00a84ce5015cb.jpg)

The woman wants to go. 那女子想離去。 The man doesn't want the woman to go. 那男子不想那女子離去。 He wants her to stay. 他想她留下来。

准時

動词 + 人物 + to ...

<table><tr><td>I want</td><td>you
somebody
Sarah</td><td>to do something</td></tr></table>

I want you to be happy. (不是 I want that you are happy) 我想你快樂。

They didn't want anybody to know their secret. 他們不想任何人知道他們的秘密。

Do you want me to lend you some money? 你想我借一些錢給你嗎？

would like 也可用同様結構：

Would you like me to lend you some money? 你想我借一些錢給你嗎？

下列動詞也可用同様結構（動詞 + 人物 + to ...）：

<table><tr><td colspan="5">动词 + 人物 + to ...</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>ask</td><td>Sue</td><td>asked</td><td>a friend</td><td>to lend</td><td>her some money. 朝一位朋友借錢。</td></tr><tr><td>tell</td><td>I</td><td>told</td><td>you</td><td>to be</td><td>careful. 我曾告訴你要小心點。</td></tr><tr><td>advise</td><td>What do you</td><td>advise</td><td>me</td><td>to do?</td><td>你建議我做甚麼?</td></tr><tr><td>expect</td><td>I didn&#x27;t</td><td>expect</td><td>them</td><td>to be</td><td>我沒有料到他們在這事。</td></tr><tr><td>persuade</td><td>We</td><td>persuaded</td><td>Gary</td><td>to come</td><td>我們說服了加里與我們同來。</td></tr><tr><td>teach</td><td>I</td><td>am teaching</td><td>my brother</td><td>to swim.</td><td>我正在教弟弟游泳。</td></tr></table>

I told you to ... / I told you not to ...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ccfa29499d457d6f43b6262792daced765ae76ff48aef78983491c98678ad2f8.jpg)  
ME

JANE

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/491e02500ec93d11ee31a9ef1d451023216bc2bd7f5db72a6554f617d7d64006.jpg)  
SUE

$\rightarrow$  Jane told me to wait for her.

珍叫我等她。

$\rightarrow$  Paul told Sue not to wait for him. 保羅叫蘇不用等他。

D make 和 let

make 和 let 之後不用 to：

He's very funny. He makes me laugh. (不是 makes me to laugh) 他十分有趣，令我發笑。

At school our teacher made us work very hard. 在學校裏，我們的老師使我們十分用功。

Sue let me use her computer because mine wasn't working. (不是 let me to use) 因為我的電腦失靈，所以蘇讓我用她的。

Let's ... (= Let us) 用於要求他人與你一起做某事情：

Come on. Let's dance. 來！我們跳舞吧。

'Do you want to go out tonight?' 'No, I'm tired. Let's stay at home.'

「今晚你想不想外出？」「不，我感到疲倦，我們還是留在家裏好了。」

Let's ...  $\Rightarrow$  聲元 35 He told me that ...  $\Rightarrow$  聲元 50

# went to the shop to

Paula wanted a newspaper, so she went to the shop.

Why did she go to the shop? 她為何到商店去？

To get a newspaper.

去買報纸。

She went to the shop to get a newspaper. 她到商店去買報纸。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8c372e9c8cfd658b2c92296f3a147f05f4356353129d592b3176896c71e49c8e.jpg)

to...（例如to get／to see等等）说明某人物做某事情的目的：

'Why are you going out?' 'To get some bread.' 「你為何外出？」「去買麵包。」  Catherine went to the station to meet her friend. 凱瑟琳到車站去見她的朋友。  Sue turned on the television to watch the news. 蔣開電視看新聞報道。  I'd like to go to Spain to learn Spanish. 我想去西班牙學西班牙語。

money / time to (do something):

We need some money to buy food. 我們需要一些金錢買食物。  I haven't got time to watch television. 我沒有時間看電視。

to...和for...

# to+動詞

（例如to get／to see等等）

I went to the shop to get a newspaper. （不是for get）我到商店去買報纸。  They're going to Brazil to see their friends. 他們正在去巴西探朋友。  We need some money to buy food. 我們需要一些金錢買食物。

# for+名詞

（例如for a newspaper／for food等等）

I went to the shop for a newspaper. 我到商店去買報纸。

They're going to Brazil for a holiday.

他們正在去巴西度假。

We need some money for food.

我們需要一些金錢買食物。

wait for...

Please wait for me. 請等我。

Are you waiting for the bus? 你是不是在等巴士？

wait to (do something):

Hurry up! I'm waiting to go. 趙快一點！我等着離去。  Are you waiting to see the doctor? 你是不是等待着看醫生？

wait for (somebody / something) to...

I can't go out yet. I'm waiting for John to phone. 我還未能外出，我在等待約翰來電。

Are you waiting for the doctor to come? 你是不是等待着那位醫生到來？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/737e965e68e943b22e127f319003e96f708c1b03c277fc46a642dcfa22bcf5af.jpg)

11 A: Excuse me, are you waiting use the phone? B: No, I'm waiting somebody.

# 54.5 選擇適當的詞組完成句子。

John / phone it / to arrive you / tell me the film / begin

1 I can't go out yet. I'm waiting for John to phone

2 I sat down in the cinema and waited

3 We called an ambulance and waited

4 Do you know what to do? No, I'm waiting

# 55 go to ... go on ... go for ... go -ing

go to ... (例如 go to work / go to London / go to a concert 等等)

What time do you usually go to work? 你經常何時上班？

I'm going to China next week. 我打算下星期去中國。

Sophie didn't want to go to the concert. 索菲不想去聽音樂會。

'Where's Tom?' 'He's gone to bed.' [湯姆在哪裹？]「他已上林睡覺。」

I went to the dentist yesterday. 昨天我去了看牙醫。

go to sleep = start to sleep:

I was very tired and went to sleep quickly. 我感到十分疲倦，很快便入睡了。

# 注意

go home (不用 to)

I'm going home now. (不是 going to home) 我現在要回家了。

B

go on ...

<table><tr><td>go on</td><td>holiday
a trip
a tour
an excursion
a cruise
strike</td></tr></table>

We're going on holiday next week. 我們打算下星期去度假。

Children often go on school trips. 孩子們常常參加學校旅行。

When we were in Scotland, we went on a lot of excursions to different places.

我們在蘇格蘭時，去了很多不同地方遊玩。

Workers at the airport have gone on strike. (= they are refusing to work) 機場的工人已經罷工。

go for ...

<table><tr><td>go (somewhere) for</td><td>a walk
a run
a swim
a drink
a meal</td></tr></table>

'Where's Emma?' 'She's gone for a walk.'

「埃瑪在哪裹？」「她去了散步。」

Do you go for a run every morning? 你每天都跑步嗎？

The water looks nice. I'm going for a swim.

水看來很不錯。我打算游泳。

I met Chris in town, so we went for a coffee.

我在鎮裹遇見克里斯，所以我們去了喝咖啡。

Shall we go out for a meal? I know a good restaurant.

我們外出吃飯好嗎？我知道有一間很好的餐廳。

go + - ing

go + - ing 用於表示做運動（例如 swimming / skiing 等等），也可用於表示購物（shopping）：

<table><tr><td>I go he is going we went they have gone she wants to go</td><td>shopping swimming fishing sailing skiing jogging</td><td>等等</td></tr></table>

Are you going shopping this afternoon?

這個下午你打算去購物嗎？

It's a nice day. Let's go swimming. (或 Let's go for a swim.) 今天天氣很好，我們一起去游泳吧。

Richard has a small boat and he often goes sailing.

理查德有一艘小艇，他經常出海。

I went jogging before breakfast this morning.

今早我吃早餐前去了缓步跑。

get a letter / get a job 等等 (get + 名詞) = 收到或得到 / 購買 / 請求或接來 / 找到：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ce2770cd67a4f6288bfb8bd00618550078ba8e6ab5cdbe04b71d4dafa389cf28.jpg)

Did you get my postcard?' 'Yes, I got it yesterday. (= receive)

「你收到我的明信片嗎？」「我昨天收到了。」

I like your sweater. Where did you get it? (= buy) 我喜歡你的毛衣，你在哪兒買的？

Is it difficult to get a job at the moment? (= find) 現時是否很難找到工作？

(on the phone) 'Hello, can I speak to Lisa, please?' 'Sure. I'll get her.'

（電話中）「你好！麻烦你我想找莉莎，可以嗎？」「請等一會，我找她來。」

此外，get a bus / a train / a taxi (= take a bus / train 等等)：

Did you walk here?' 'No, I got the bus.' 「你步行來這裏嗎？」「不，我乘巴士來的。」

get hungry / get cold / get tired 等等 (get + 形容詞) = 變得 / 成為：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/74b8d8270936f3d44f95959ad12cc38c647b6143debcf29bd5098b3d91c8c48f.jpg)

If you don't eat, you get hungry. 如果你不吃東西，你會餓的。

Drink your coffee. It's getting cold. 你先喝咖啡吧，它開始涼了。

I'm sorry your mother is ill. I hope she gets better soon.

我為你媽媽病倒的事感到難過，希望她早日康復。

It was raining very hard. We didn't have an umbrella, so we got very wet.

雨下得很大，我們沒有帶雨傘，所以全身都濕透了。

此外 get married

Nicole and Frank are getting married soon.

尼科爾和弗蘭克快將結婚。

get dressed (= put your clothes on) get lost (= lose your way)

I got up and got dressed quickly. 我起床，並趕快穿上衣服。

We didn't have a map, so we got lost.

我們沒有地圖，所以迷了路。

C

get to a place = 到達：

I usually get to work before 8:30. (= arrive at work) 我經常在 8 時 30 分前到達辦公室。

We left London at 10 o'clock and got to Manchester at 12:45.

我們在 10 時離開倫敦，然後於 12 時 45 分到達曼徹斯特。

get here / there (不用 to)：

How did you get here? By bus? 你是怎樣來到這裏的？乘巴士的嗎？

get home (不用 to)：

What time did you get home last night? 時晚你何時回到家裏？

get in / out / on / off

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/93f12776be5aad398c99282374dc778e1b562100a862a791ad4bb0de7f5058a4.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fde53263fb668b101f8bc88c56505ec536c5d3f152ecec73eaf8c2497669b789.jpg)

get in (a car)

get out (of a car)

get on (a bus / a train / a plane)

Kate got in the car and drove away. (也可說：Kate got into the car and ...) 凱特上了車，然後駕車離去。

# do 和 make

do 是表達動作或行動的常用詞：

What are you doing this evening?（不是What are you making?）今天晚上你打算做甚麼？  ‘Shall I open the window?’ ‘No, it's OK. I'll do it.’「我開窗好嗎？」「不，讓我來。」  Rachel's job is very boring. She does the same thing every day.  雷切爾的工作很沈悶，她每天都做相同的事情。  I did a lot of things yesterday. 昨天我做了很多事情。

What do you do? - What's your job? 你做甚麼工作？：

‘What do you do?’ ‘I work in a bank.’「你做甚麼工作？」「我在銀行裏工作。」

make = 製造 / 創造。例如：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/71881c15eb55fae0319cf1dc3622974252f50f1cd567b9771d0fd299345ce4d0.jpg)

She's making coffee. 她在煮咖啡。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7c3a5773a46614336e24fea77899d9eca056a76fa9efbc5943aadb8a29a4067a.jpg)

He has made a cake. 他做了一個蛋糕。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/282dfcbc6f22d539aa5f9134280635cce86c5a793ef07d23e6757decbc9faeef.jpg)

They make umbrellas. 他們生產雨傘。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ff95e396758e8325550c2c4cd0bf8240995901a587bac8e8e01ee3f768538736.jpg)

It was made in China. 這是在中國製造的。

# 试比較

I did a lot yesterday. I cleaned my room, I wrote some letters and I made a cake.

昨天我做了很多事情。我打掃了自己的房間、寫了一些信和做了一個蛋糕。

A: What do you do in your free time? 'Sport'? Reading? Hobbies?

你空間時會做甚麼？運動？閱讀？其他嗜好？

B: I make clothes. I make dresses and jackets. I also make toys for my children.

我會做衣服，例如裙子和外衣。我也會為我的孩子們製作玩具。

# 用 do 的表達方式：

an exam / a test  a course  homework  do housework  somebody a favour  an exercise

I'm doing my driving test next week. 下星期我會參加駕駛考試。  John has just done a training course. 約翰剛完成一個訓練課程。  Our children have to do a lot of homework. 我們的孩子們要做很多家庭作業。  I hate doing housework, especially cleaning. 我討厭做家務，尤其是清潔打掃。  Sue, could you do me a favour? 蘇，你可否幫我一個忙？  I go for a run and do exercises every morning. 我每天早上都跑步和做運動。

還有 do the shopping / do the washing / do the washing- up / do the ironing / do the cooking 等等：  I did the washing, but I didn't do the shopping. 我洗了衣服，但沒有購物。

# 用 make 的表達方式：

a mistake  an appointment  a phone call  a list  a noise  a bed

I'm sorry, I made a mistake. 對不起，我犯了一個錯誤。  I need to make an appointment to see the doctor. 我需要預約看醫生。  Excuse me, I have to make a phone call. 對不起，我要打一個電話。  Have you made a shopping list? 你準備了購物清單嗎？  It's late. Don't make a noise. 已經很晚了，我們切勿發出噪音。  Sometimes I forget to make my bed in the morning. 有時我忘記在早上把林鋪整理好。

# 注意

make a film 和 take a photograph 的用法：

When was this film made? 但是 When was this photograph taken? 這齣電影是在時拍攝的？這張照片是何時拍下的？

# 檢索

do / does / did（否定句和疑問句）  $\Rightarrow$  曝元 43- 44 make somebody do something  $\Rightarrow$  曝元 53

# have

A

have 和 have got

I've got (something) 或 I have (something) = 這些物件是屬於我的：

I've got a new car. 或 I have a new car. 我有一輛新車。

Sue has got long hair. 或 Sue has long hair. 蘇有一把長髮。

Have they got any children? 或 Do they have any children? 他們有小孩子嗎？

Tim hasn't got a job. 或 Tim doesn't have a job. 蒂姆沒有工作。

How much time have you got? 或 How much time do you have? 你有多多時間？

此外：

<table><tr><td>I&#x27;ve got</td><td>a headache / (a) toothache / a pain (in my leg 等等)</td></tr><tr><td>I have</td><td>a cold / a cough / a sore throat / a temperature / flu 等等</td></tr></table>

I've got a headache. 或 I have a headache. 我頭痛。

Have you got a cold? 或 Do you have a cold? 你患上感冒嗎？

過去式是：I had (不用 got) / I didn't have / Did you have? 等等：

When I first met Sue, she had short hair. 我初次見蘇時，她是短髮的。

He didn't have any money because he didn't have a job. 因為失業，所以他沒有錢。

Did you have enough time to do everything you wanted? 你有足夠的時間去做你想做的所有事情嗎？

have breakfast / have a shower 等等

# 注意

在下列問句中，have = eat / drink / talk 等等，不可用 'have got'。

<table><tr><td>have</td><td>breakfast / lunch / dinner
a meal / a sandwich / a pizza 等等
a cup of coffee / a glass of milk 等等
something to eat / drink</td></tr></table>

'Where's Liz?' 'She's having lunch.'

「利茲在哪裏？」「她在吃午飯。」

I don't usually have breakfast. 我不常吃早餐。

I had three cups of coffee this morning. 我今早喝了三杯咖啡。

我今早喝了三杯咖啡。

Have a biscuit!' 'Oh, thank you.'

「吃塊餅乾吧！」「謝謝。」

have (不是 have got) 也可用於以下表達方式：

a bath / a shower a rest / a holiday / a party a nice time / a good trip / fun 等等 have a walk / a swim / a game (of tennis 等等) a dream / an accident a baby a look (at something)

I had a shower this morning. 我今早洗了澡。

We're having a party next week. You must come.

我們打算下星期舉行派對，你一定要來。

Enjoy your holiday. Have a nice time!

好好享受你的假期，希望你玩得開心！

Did you have a good time in Tokyo?

你在東京時愉快嗎？

Sandra has just had a baby. 桑德拉剛誕下嬰兒。

Can I have a look at your newspaper?

我可否看看你的報紙？

# 試比較

have got 或 have

I've got / I have a new shower. It's very good. 我有一個新的淋浴間，十分不錯的。

have (不是 have got)

I have a shower every morning. (不是 I've got a shower every morning) 我每天早上都洗澡。

A: Where's Paul? 保羅在哪裏？

B: He's having a shower. (= he's washing now) 他正在洗澡。

# I/me he/him they/them 等等

A

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dade5c13fcb4c539a390844b2b3fd27c2e72ced8f808f2de880aa05bbb399e97.jpg)

主語

I me

we you

you

he she they

主語

I I know Tom. we We know Tom. you You know Tom. he He knows Tom. she She knows Tom. they They know Tom.

我認識湯姆。我們認識湯姆。你認識湯姆。他認識湯姆。他們認識湯姆。

湯姆認識我。湯姆認識我們。湯姆認識你。湯姆認識他。湯姆認識她。湯姆認識他們。

Tom knows me. me Tom knows us. us Tom knows you. you Tom knows him. him Tom knows her. her Tom knows them. them

# 物件

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/19acf9359ffbf24797990c2bdc5b9a0a96e54fe6b4b57353f7a61b3af75c4d45.jpg)

主語

it

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0725a89321bfb709996be5ff615b45ef30b95fe3103f860e85022c6484649088.jpg)

they them

I don't want this book. You can have it. 我不想要這本書,你可以要它。

I don't want these books. You can have them. 我不想要這些書,你可以要它們。

Diane never drinks milk. She doesn't like it. 偷安從不喝牛奶,她不喜歡喝牛奶。

I never go to parties. I don't like them. 我從不去派對,我不喜歡派對。

me / her / them 等等(賓語)用於介詞(例如 for / to / with 等等)之後。

This letter isn't for me. It's for you. 這封信不是給我的,是給你的。

Who is that woman? Why are you looking at her? 那女子是誰?你為何看着她?

We're going to the cinema. Do you want to come with us?

我們要去看電影,你想與我們一起去嗎?

Sue and Kevin are going to the cinema. Do you want to go with them?

蘇和凱文去看電影,你想跟他們一起去嗎?

'Where's the newspaper?' 'You're sitting on it.' 「報紙放在哪裹？」「你正坐着它。」

give it / them to ...

I want that book. Please give it to me.

我想要那本書,請你把它給我。

Robert needs these books. Can you give them to him, please?

羅伯特想要這些書,麻你可否拿給他?

# my/his/their 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7ba7be0f21245f82f0a169a68f13a17c34f39dc36f92048c129f821098425614.jpg)

I→ my we → our you → your he → his she → her they → their

I like my house. 我喜歡我的房子。 We like our house. 我們喜歡我們的房子。 You like your house. 你喜歡你的房子。 He likes his house. 他喜歡他的房子。 She likes her house. 她喜歡她的房子。 They like their house. 他們喜歡他們的房子。

it → its

Oxford (= it) is famous for its university. 牛津以其大學聞名。

my / your / his 等等 + 名詞：

my hands 我的雙手 his new car 他的新車 her parents 她的父母 our clothes 我們的衣服 your best friend 你的好朋友 their room 他們的房間

his/her/their

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/273e205bafe6240e13993befc95f0e42ce7aed629cec692f9d013a0b30bb6b02.jpg)

# its和it's

its Oxford is famous for its university. 牛津以其大學聞名。 it's (= it is) I like Oxford. It's a nice place. (= It is a nice place.) 我喜歡牛津，它是一個美麗的地方。

# B 元 61

# Whose is this? It's mine / yours / hers 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b6e753cf38f76fe1db55535f6587fe98209b07cd1bc4a1fe4a5652e13473e998.jpg)

我的 我們的 你的 他的 她的 他們的

I → my → mine  we → our → ours  you → your → yours  he → his → his  she → her → hers  they → their → theirs

It's my money. It's mine.  It's our money. It's ours.  It's your money. It's yours.  It's his money. It's his.  It's her money. It's hers.  It's their money. It's theirs.

# my / your 等等 + 名詞（例如 my hands / your book 等等）：

My hands are cold. 我的手很冷。

Is this your book? 這本書是你的嗎？

Helen gave me her umbrella. 海倫把她的雨車給我。

It's their problem, not our problem. 這是他們的問題，不是我們的問題。

# mine / yours 等等不與名詞一起運用：

Is this book mine or yours? (= my book or your book) 這本書是我的還是你的？  I didn't have an umbrella, so Helen gave me hers. (= her umbrella)  我沒有雨傘，所以海倫把她的給我。  It's their problem, not ours. (= not our problem) 這是他們的問題，不是我們的問題。  We went in our car, and they went in theirs. (= their car)  我們進入我們的車，他們進入他們的車。

# 注意

his 之後是否接用名詞也可以：

Is this his camera or hers? 'It's his.' 「這是他的照相機還是她的？」「這是他的。」

# a friend of mine / a friend of his / some friends of yours 等等

I went out to meet a friend of mine.（不是 a friend of me）我出去見我的朋友。  Tom was in the restaurant with a friend of his.（不是 a friend of him）湯姆在餐廳裏跟他的朋友一起。  Are those people friends of yours?（不是 friends of you）那些人是你的朋友嗎？

# Whose ...?

Whose book is this? (= Is it your book?  his book? my book? 等等）這是誰的書？

# 注意

Whose 之後是否接用名詞也可以：

Whose money is this? 這是誰的錢？  It's mine.  Whose is this? 這是誰的？  是是我的。  Whose shoes are these? 這是誰的鞋？  They're John's.  Whose are these? 這些是誰的？  它們是約翰的。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f27d47cb9751ac0618ebe62ce9beafcbb49a8ddae36709c1544215e552dfa8a6.jpg)

B

學習以下例句：

Do you know that man?' 'Yes, I know him, but I can't remember his name.' 「你認識那男子嗎？」「我認識他，但我記不起他的名字。」 She was very pleased because we invited her to stay with us at our house. 我們邀請她留在我們家裏，她感到很高興。 A: Where are the children? Have you seen them? 孩子們在哪裏？你見過他們嗎？ B: Yes, they are playing with their friends in the park. 見過，他們跟朋友在公園裏玩耍。 That's my pen. Can you give it to me, please? 那是我的筆，麻煩你可否給我？ 'Is this your umbrella?' 'No, it's yours.' 「這是你的雨傘嗎？」「不，它是你的。」 He didn't have an umbrella, so she gave him hers. (= she gave her umbrella to him) 他沒有帶雨傘，所以她把自己的雨傘給了他。 I'm going out with a friend of mine this evening. (不是 a friend of me) 今晚我打算跟我的朋友外出。

# 63 myself / yourself / themselves 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/37cdff7e3e448e9e987d703a709e4bca170faab30a067647131a8455fc1c2fcf.jpg)

He's looking at himself. 他正在看着自己。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ab8f708141960260db036c9260c553a9dfa82eff7b7a969a0b57df9dc0bc68c2.jpg)

Help yourself. 随便享用！

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8280ad6393f8235f893ebd88f255f986640137931ffeda6b3fabb0383949777d.jpg)

They're enjoying themselves. 他們正在玩樂。

I → me → myself  he → him → himself  she → her → herself  you → you → yourself  we → us → ourselves  they → them → themselves

I looked at myself in the mirror. 我看着鏡中的自己。

He cut himself with a knife. 他用刀割傷自己。

She fell off her bike, but she didn't hurt herself.

她從自行車上跌下來，但卻沒有弄傷自己。

Please help yourself. 請你隨便享用。（對象是一個人）

Please help yourselves. 請你們隨便享用。（對象是兩個人或以上）

We had a good holiday. We enjoyed ourselves.

我們度過了一個愉快的假期。我們玩得很高興。

They had a nice time. They enjoyed themselves.

他們度過了一段快樂的時光。他們玩得很高興。

# me/him/them 等等

# She is looking at him

# 不同的人

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a2ddc08056fc0e2e3b02f4facebf2b93f766adab6c6f53d006b0ec4611db6440.jpg)

# myself / himself / themselves 等等

He is looking at himself.

# 相同的人

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6e7703f30a895bd8429aa37649413c506d3bf26d39918ceb49f4e75953c9cbdc.jpg)

You never talk to me. 你從來不跟我說話。  I didn't pay for them. 我沒有為他們付錢。  I'm sorry. Did I hurt you? 對不起。我傷害了你嗎？

Sometimes I talk to myself. 有時我會跟自己說話。  They paid for themselves. 他們自己付錢。  Be careful. Don't hurt yourself. 小心一點，別傷害到你自己。

# by myself / by yourself 等等 = 獨自：

I went on holiday by myself. (= I went alone) 我獨自去度假。  'Was she with friends?' 'No, she was by herself.' 「她是否跟朋友一起？」「不，她獨自一人。」

# each other

Kate and Helen are good friends. They know each other well. (= Kate knows Helen / Helen knows Kate) 與特和海倫是好朋友，他們很熟悉對方。  Paul and I live near each other. (= he lives near me / I live near him) 保羅和我住在彼此的附近。

# 試比較

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/84d82b87c656ba1e1f813a09e9894f85aef533e8587e85e6db3fe008344e1697.jpg)  
each other 和 -selves 的用法及其分別：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/57615b32e486354d729dfa368f8af2cddc8f0da0852d650cc7cb2eadcc5ef906.jpg)  
TIM

Tim and Sue looked at each other. (= he looked at her, she looked at him) 蒂姆和蘇互相對望。

Tim and Sue looked at themselves. (= he looked at himself, she looked at herself) 蒂姆和蘇各自看着自己。

8 Diane and I are very good friends. We've known for a long time.

9 'Did you see Sam and Laura at the party?' 'Yes, but I didn't speak to

10 Many people talk to when they're alone.

# -'s (Kate's camera / my brother's car) 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0d985677835cc89d1875cf63d99575553091128dec0c0d191e09349c5ee9278f.jpg)

# -'s 通常用於人：

- 's 通常用於人：I stayed at my sister's house. (不是 the house of my sister) 我留在我妹妹的家裏。  
- Have you met Mr Black's wife? (不是 the wife of Mr Black) 你見過布萊克先生的妻子嗎？  
- Are you going to James's party? 你打算參加詹姆斯的派對嗎？  
- Paul is a man's name. Paula is a woman's name. 保羅是一個男子名，葆拉是一個女子名。

# 注意

- 's 之後不一定要接用名詞：

- 's 之後不一定要接用名詞：Sophie's hair is longer than Kate's. (= Kate's hair) 索菲的頭髮較凱特的長。  
- 'Whose umbrella is this?' 'It's my mother's.' (= my mother's umbrella) 「這是誰人的雨傘？」「這是我媽媽的。」  
- 'Where were you last night?' 'I was at Paul's.' (= Paul's house) 「昨晚你在哪裏？」「我在保羅的家裏。」

# friend's 和 friends'

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b9532afa254c6512b14d866134a426b1c09aae17bbc08c9cabc357606c91dea5.jpg)

my friend's house  $=$  指一個朋友（單數） $\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} =$  his house or her house)

- 's 用於 friend / student / mother 等等之後（單數）：

my mother's car（指一個媽媽）  my father's car（指一個爸爸）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7afa6e9347f12dae9c64548ec3f14def62b3cf1570e6b1106b5ab5c5d6e0d71f.jpg)

my friends' house  $=$  指兩個或以上的朋友（複數） $\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} =$  their house)

- '用於 friends / students / parents 等等之後（複數）：  my parents' car（指父親和母親二人）

# of... 用於物件、地方等等：

Look at the roof of that building.（不是 that building's roof）看看那建築物的頂部。  We didn't see the beginning of the film. (不是 the film's beginning) 我們看不到那飼電影的開頭部分。

What's the name of this village? 這村莊的名稱是甚麼？

Do you know the cause of the problem? 你知道那問題的原因嗎？

You can sit in the back of the car. 你可以坐在車子的後座。

Madrid is the capital of Spain. 馬德里是西班牙的首都。

单元 65 a/an...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d4ccc297db9102e8adb80b92dd79f5706caa67946830a8b5f7ea311ed2d2665d.jpg)

He's got a camera. 他有一架照相機。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/18f2bad8f25473f45d37dd1946b0e8ff62f6fdbdee74eea71cf6c3eb2f372783.jpg)

She's waiting for a taxi. 她在等計程車。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dc0182823a2e37966d1f5fb6adc7d1aa873fcca954407faad2b77a697196a630.jpg)

It's a beautiful day. 今天天氣很好。

a... = 一件物件或一個人物:

- Rachel works in a bank. (不是 in bank) 雷切爾在一間銀行裏工作。  
- Can I ask a question? (不是 ask question) 我可否問一個問題？  
- I haven't got a computer. 我沒有一部電腦。  
- There's a woman at the bus stop. 有一個女子在巴士站。

an (不是 a) 用於元音發音開頭 (一般是 a / e / i / o / u 開頭) 的單詞前面:

- Do you want an apple or a banana? 你想要一個蘋果還是一隻香蕉？  
- I'm going to buy a hat and an umbrella. 我打算買一頂帽子和一把雨傘。  
- There was an interesting programme on TV last night. 昨晚電視播放了一個有趣的節目。

# 注意

an hour (h 是不發音的: an hour)  a university (發音為 yuniversity)  a European country (發音為 yuopean)

another (= an + other) 是一個詞:

- Can I have another cup of coffee? 我可以再要一杯咖啡嗎？

a / an ... 用於說明某事物或某人物。例如:

- The sun is a star. 太陽是一顆恒星。  
- Football is a game. 足球是一項運動。  
- Dallas is a city in Texas. 達拉斯是德州一個城市。  
- A mouse is an animal. It's a small animal. 老鼠是動物。牠是一隻小動物。  
- Joe is a very nice person. 喬是一個很好的人。

a / an ... 用於表示工作等等:

- A: What's your job? 你的工作是甚麼？  
- B: I'm a dentist. (不是 I'm dentist) 我是一個牙醫。  
- 'What does Mark do?' 'He's an engineer.' 「馬克做甚麼工作？」「他是一個工程師。」  
- Would you like to be a teacher? 你想做一個教師嗎？  
- Beethoven was a composer. 貝多芬是一位作曲家。  
- Picasso was a famous painter. 罪加索是一位著名的畫家。  
- Are you a student? 你是不是學生？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5aa1c6cc7b5c39b96a9fd7eb6babaa79edac02e407bb8e4d2ac08e59f77f53c9.jpg)

# trains(s) bus(es)  留數 (singular) 和複數 (plural)

名词的複数通常是加上 - s:

單數  $(= -$  恒)  $\rightarrow$  摘數  $(=$  两恒或以上) a flower  $\rightarrow$  some flowers a train  $\rightarrow$  two trains one week  $\rightarrow$  a few weeks a nice place  $\rightarrow$  some nice places this student  $\rightarrow$  these students

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d5becbce6ce959dbda3483062b996bff8c49922693502e99d23686b6a3503aba.jpg)

拼寫方法: 檢索  $\rightarrow$  附錄 5

<table><tr><td rowspan="3">-s / -sh / -ch / -x</td><td rowspan="3">→</td><td>-es</td><td>bus → buses</td><td>dish → dishes</td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>church → churches</td><td>box → boxes</td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>還有</td><td>potato → potatoes</td><td>tomato → tomatoes</td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td></td><td>-y</td><td>→</td><td>-ies</td><td>baby → babies</td><td>dictionary → dictionaries</td><td></td></tr><tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td>party → parties</td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>但是 -ay / -ey / -oy</td><td>→</td><td>-ys</td><td>day → days</td><td>monkey → monkeys</td><td>boy → boys</td><td></td></tr><tr><td></td><td>-f / -fe</td><td>→</td><td>-ves</td><td>shelf → shelves</td><td>knife → knives</td><td>wife → wives</td></tr></table>

下列的物件在英語中是複數的:

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/bcbd6038ec84ca1aae3068d6babc5d4167c42ab37aba72321859ea7c7f2ca0b1.jpg)

scissors glasses trousers剪刀 眼鏡 長褲

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/017aed5e94753dfb158599cd6b5f9a327152d393184eae0e5752b148fd2ad61b.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7ac1698e9164acfc2a0d5ccb64c602398de70eb71418f9e8e13f46c239a724d5.jpg)

jeans牛仔褲

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8435fd060a906d7663b1360dab58122188d1056500969aec5c9dd01e094097ee.jpg)

shorts短褲

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f1b5c72cee3c9221acf3c629445a944d52dc0ca4847b93dd8e04c71b5308d07c.jpg)

pyjamas 睡衣

Do you wear glasses? 你戴眼镜嗎?

Where are the scissors? I need them. 剪刀在哪裏? 我需要用。

也可說 a pair of scissors / a pair of trousers / a pair of pyjamas 等等:

I need a new pair of jeans. 或 I need some new jeans. (不是 a new jeans) 我需要一條新的牛仔褲。 或 我需要一些新的牛仔褲。

有些複數詞不會在末尾加上 - s:

this man  $\rightarrow$  these men a woman  $\rightarrow$  some women a child  $\rightarrow$  many children

one foot  $\rightarrow$  two feet a tooth  $\rightarrow$  all my teeth a mouse  $\rightarrow$  some mice

that sheep  $\rightarrow$  those sheep a fish  $\rightarrow$  a lot of fish

還有: a person  $\rightarrow$  two people / some people / a lot of people 等等:

She's a nice person. 她是一個好人。

但是 They are nice people. (不是 nice persons) 他們是好人。

people 是複數 (= they), 所以應該說 people are / people have 等等:

A lot of people speak English. (不是 speaks) 很多人說英語。

I like the people here. They are very friendly. 我喜歡這裏的人,他們十分友善。

police 是複數:

The police want to talk to anybody who saw the accident. (不是 The police wants) 警方希望聯絡目標這次意外的任何人士。

換索 by  $\Rightarrow$  單元 21, 63, 109 at / on  $\Rightarrow$  單元 103, 106- 107 介詞 + - ing  $\Rightarrow$  單元 112

# a bottle / some water 可數的1（countable1）/不可數的1（uncountable1）

名词有可数的，也有不可数的。

# 可数名词

例如：(a) car (a) man (a) bottle (a) house (a) key (an) idea (an) accident 车 人 瓶子 房屋 鑰匙 主意 意外

one/two/three（等等）+可数名词：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/79e6515a4843dc24e73e97299c146c0f491eb4ade22fcc192227bc6114b97759.jpg)

one bottle 一個瓶子

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d8b20a4b0a98d961c7499bdb14012335d740265cc2401b8a72d6f0689d6f5738.jpg)

two bottles 两个瓶子

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0ce616b0e5044549de69cbec7c3b3ae1ef23d7cf57ec14a7a08ec1451de9de8f.jpg)

three men 三個男人

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ebce3211fd3dfcdc506c5a31fb6b7fa47b151709a369b57abd19bf5f40ad299a.jpg)

four houses 四間房屋

可數名可以是單數（  $=$  一個），也可以是複數（  $=$  兩個或以上）：

單數： a car the car my car 等等 穰数： cars two cars the cars some cars many cars 等等

I've got a car. 我有一辆汽車。

New cars are very expensive. 新車是十分昂贵的。

There aren't many cars in the car park. 停車場內的車不多。

單數名不能單獨使用（例如 car/bottle/key 等等），必須加上 a/an： 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元 64

We can't get into the house without a key.（不是 without key）我們没有鑰匙，不能入內。

# 不可數名词

例如： water air rice salt plastic money music tennis 水空氣米鹽塑膠金錢音樂 網球運動

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d46978b7cd7788484048fbea3defde4c3aa11e2db9b4e10591031a7a5e9b1165.jpg)  
water

水

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/cf2893d2026d752f1d25c1152f458aa5514ba43f2cb9d88d10ad21119754fa38.jpg)  
salt

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/17b3f4dc536778d0b62a92fb6c88d06823104b6baca701f84039694144615f5f.jpg)  
money

金錢

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8d39146a60ddaa82feec5da4d97b3d520bb27996a3c46f4112292ff65bec8125.jpg)  
music

音樂

不能用one/two/three（等等）+以下的物件：one- water two- music

不可數名只有一個形式：

money the money my money some money much money等等

I've got some money. 我有一些金錢。

There isn't much money in the box. 盒內的錢不多。

Money isn't everything. 金錢不是一切。

# 注意

不能用a/an  $^+$  不可數名： xmoney xmusic xwater

但可以用a piece of.../a bottle of...等等  $^+$  不可數名：

a bottle of water 一瓶水 a piece of cheese 一片芝士 a bowl of rice 一碗飯

a carton of milk 一盒牛奶 a bottle of perfume 一瓶香水 a cup of coffee 一杯咖啡

a bar of chocolate 一條巧克力 a piece of music 一首樂曲 a game of tennis 一場網球比赛

# 罪元 68

# a cake / some cake / some cakes 可數的2(countable2）/不可數的2（uncountable2）

A

a/an和some

a/an  $^+$  翼数的可数名词（例如car／apple/shoe等等）：

I need a new car. 我需要一輛新車。

Wouldyoulikeanapple？你想要一個果嗎？

some  $^+$  機數的可數名：（例如cars／apples/shoes等等）：

I need some new shoes. 我需要一些新鞋子。

Wouldyoulike someapples？你想要一些果嗎？

some  $^+$  不可數名（例如water／money／music等等）：

I need some water. 我需要一些水。

Wouldyoulikesomecheese？你想要些芝士？

（或Wouldyoulikeapieceofcheese？你想要一片芝士？）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a6683a523f360f30fb009be45145ca5d8daa1b1cbedcfc3626e9165f7ebedaeb.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1169df63d075918c6ec1d62de7f3b4e666d4407cf75015604de351f6c2465a41.jpg)

an apple

some apples

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/33a6a28ef245af5443cbeccdd1e47f6a2fc4be86d4f9d94dae8befa736971c70.jpg)

some cheese 或 a piece of cheese

# 試比較

a和some的用法及其分别：

Nicole bought a hat, some shoes and some perfume. 尼科爾買了一頂帽子、數對鞋子和一些香水。 I read a newspaper, made some phone calls, and listened to some music. 我讀了一分報紙、打了幾個電話和聽了一些音樂。

很多名词有時候是可數的，但有時候则是不可數的。例如：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/cec23a7d79326d336a9eaf17022095320d28957567340451dd74b7d73f7bb4b1.jpg)

a cake

一個蛋糕

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/386e680fcc66877b00eccbfe64fa58f7be3c08473de5beccd70fe051fdfda666.jpg)

some cakes 織件蛋糕

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/abd8fa055459201fe01c384dd8a4b556c69668154c05ceb1774fa6dfc16f09e0.jpg)

some cake or a piece of cake 一些蛋糕或一件蛋糕

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2a7cc72a08299e4d813ad7453be1789f2afe7b6c747f20da614636f433617ae4.jpg)

a chicken

一雙雞

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/889623b3383c55dfdc08674569a5dde33f64ac118ddfac7fae1ca860fe1c4db7.jpg)

some chickens

些

some chicken or a piece of chicken 一些雞肉或一塊雞肉

# 試比較

a paper  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  a newspaper)和some paper的用法及其分别：

I want something to read. I'm going to buy a paper. 我想讀一些東西，所以我打算買一份報紙。

I want to make a shopping list. I need some paper / a piece of paper. (不是 a paper)

我想列一份購物清單，我需要一些紙張／一張紙。

# 注意下列名词：

advice bread furniture hair information news weather work這些名通常是不可數的，所以不能a/an...（abread,anadvice），而且它們也没有複數（advees,funitures等等）。

CanI talk to you?I need someadvice.（不是anadvice）我可以和你該該嗎？我需要一些忠告。I'm going to buy some bread.（不是a bread）我打算買一些包。

They've got some very nice furniture in their house.（不是furnitures）他們的屋子裹有一些很精緻的傢具。Silvia has got very long hair.（不是hairs）西爾維亞有很長的頭髮。

I'd like some information about hotels in London.（不是informations）我想要一些倫敦的酒店資料。

Listen! I've just had some good news.（不是a good news）聽着！我剛有一些好消息。

It's nice weather today.（不是a nice weather）今天的天氣很好。

Do you like your job? 'Yes, but it's hard work'（不是a hard work)

「你喜歡自己的工作嗎？」「喜歡，但這工作頗辛苦。」

「你喜歡自己的工作嗎？」「喜歡，但這工作頗辛苦。」

# 注意

可以用a job來表達一份工作，但不能說'a work'：

I've got a new job.（不是a new work）我有一份新工作。

# a/an和the

A

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3f2e2c4697aa5a19ccb919102913d861534a1fa8284508558ac769f349f1cde8.jpg)

這裏有三扇窗子a window  $=$  window 1 or 2 or 3

I've got a car.我有一辆汽車。（有很多汽車，而我有一辆）

Can I ask a question?

我可否問一條問題？

（有很多問題——我可否問一條？）

Is there a hotel near here?

附近有一間酒店嗎？

（有很多酒店——附近有一間嗎？）

Paris is an interesting city.

巴黎是一個有趣的城市。

（有很多有趣的城市，而巴黎是其中一個）

Lisa is a student.

莉莎是一名學生。

（有很多學生，而莉莎是其中一個）

the

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/122023a66e28378bfbde604d33ee7aa479442563de829d94855ba13d35eeb020.jpg)

這裏只有一扇窗子——the window.

I'm going to clean the car tomorrow.

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{m}\mathbf{y}$  car)我打算明天洗車。

Can you repeat the question, please?

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} =$  the question that you asked)

請問你可否重複那條問題？

We enjoyed our holiday. The hotel was very

nice.  $\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} =$  our hotel)

我們十分享受我們的假期，那酒店非常舒適。

Paris is the capital of France.

巴黎是法國的首都。

（法國只有一個首都）

Lisa is the youngest student in her class.

莉莎是班中最年幼的學生。

（她的班裏只有一個最年幼的學生）

試比較 a和the的用法及其分別：

I bought a jacket and a shirt. The jacket was cheap, but the shirt was expensive. (= the jacket and the shirt that I bought) 我買了一件夾克和一件襯衣。那夾克很便宜，但那襯衣卻很昂貴。

the...用於特別指明某物件或某人物，而該話中的雙方都知道所指的是甚麼。例如：

（一間房間內的）the door / the ceiling / the floor / the carpet / the light 等等（一間屋子裏的）the roof / the garden / the kitchen / the bathroom 等等（一個市鎮裏的）the centre / the station / the airport / the town hall 等等

'Where's Tom? 'In the kitchen'

「湯姆在哪裏？」[在廚房裏。」

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{\rho}$  這房子或住宅單位裏的廚房）

Turn off the light and close the door.

關掉那煙，然後關門。

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{\rho}$  那房間的燈和門）

Do you live far from the centre?

你是否住在距離那中心很遠的地方？

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{\rho}$  你所住的市鎮內的中心）

I'd like to speak to the manager, please.

麻煩你，我想跟這裏的經理談談。

$\mathbf{\dot{\rho}} = \mathbf{\rho}$  這商店的經理）

# 69.1 在横線上填上a/an或the。

1 We enjoyed our trip. The hotel was very nice.

2 Can I ask a question? Sure. What do you want to know?

3 You look very tired. You need holiday.

4 Where's Tom? He's in garden.

5 Eve is interesting person. You should meet her.

6 A: Excuse me, can you tell me how to get to city centre?

B: Yes, go straight on and then take next turning left.

7 A: Shall we go out for meal this evening?

B: Yes, that's good idea.

8 It's nice morning. Let's go for walk.

9 Amanda is student. When she finishes her studies, she wants to be journalist. She lives with two friends in apartment near college where she is studying. apartment is small, but she likes it.

10 Peter and Mary have got two children, boy and girl. boy is seven years old, and girl is three. Peter works in factory. Mary hasn't got job at the moment.

# the

the用於特别指明某物件或某人物,而谈话中的雙方都知道所指的是甚磨:

Whatisthenameofthisstreet?這條街道的名是甚磨?(這條街道只有一個名)

Whoisthebestplayerinyourteam?誰是你隊中的最佳運動員?

Canyoutellmethetimeplease?請問你可否告我現在點鐘?(=現在的時間)

My officeis onthefirstfloor.我的辦公室在一樓。(=那幢大厦的一樓)

# 注意

别忘記加上the:

Do you live near the city centre?(不是near city centre)你住在市中心附近嗎?

Excuse me,whereisthenearestbank?(不是whereisnearest...)對不起,請問最近的銀行在哪裹?

B the same ...

We live in the same street.(不是in same street)我們住在同一條街道上。

Are these two books different?'No,they're the same'(不是they're same)

「這兩本書是不同的？」「不,它們是相同的。」

C

在下列情况必须用the:

the sun / the moon / the world / the sky / the sea / the country

The sky is blue and the sun is shining. 現在天色蔚藍,太陽在照耀着。

Do you live in a town or in the country? 你住在市鎮還是郊外?

(一個城市或國家的)the police / the fire brigade / the army

My brother is a soldier. He's in the army. 我哥哥是一名士兵,他在陸軍服役。

What do you think of the police? Do they do a good job?

你對警察有怎樣的看法?他們的工作做得好嗎?

the top / the end / the middle / the left 等等:

Write your name at the top of the page. 在這頁的上方寫上你的名字。the left

My house is at the end of the street. 我的房子在這條街道的盡頭。

The table is in the middle of the room. 那桌子在房間的中央。

Do you drive on the right or on the left in your country?

在你的國家裏駕駛是靠右還是靠左?

(play) the piano / the guitar / the trumpet 等等(樂器):

Paula is learning to play the piano. 菠拉正在學彈鋼琴。

the radio

I listen to the radio a lot. 我經常聽收音機。

the Internet

Do you use the Internet much? 你經常使用互聯網嗎?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dabbdde24773ba6074e49800d46bf261fe00a7610fb4011ec9934512016f3678.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fef3c195500b978ce0f9ee47d78f6f5bf373b0e1001a00a0a363adfcf3c720b3.jpg)

the bottom

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2eee5cacf860575ff3c7b846148d68a8a33b015f5541cc132d00a0c2721a5085.jpg)

在下列情况切勿用the:

television / TV

I watch TV a lot. 我常常看電視。

What's on television tonight? 今晚電視有甚麼節目?

Can you turn off the television? (= the TV set) 你可否關掉電視機?

breakfast / lunch / dinner

What did you have for breakfast? (不是the breakfast) 你早餐吃了甚麼?

Dinner is ready! 晚餐準備好了!

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b348bcc95d6ef940fac920df32c220eb915de078df3382807ecdd74238bc9376.jpg)

next / last + week / month / year / summer / Monday 等等:

I'm not working next week. (不是the next week) 我下星期不上班。

Did you have a holiday last summer? (不是the last summer) 去年夏天你有放假嗎?

# go to work go home go to the cinema

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c2555565634ebd102f7575c2aa0b7c7684181f1605a302de6fdaa949e798684b.jpg)

She's at work. 她在工作。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2ebc4d5e43f117b01ad5dd9c4c98ae44d8c42a9916816d7a85f8148967bfddb9.jpg)

They're going to school. 他们在上学去。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e5c80b12b407373e1c6098cabf221e4a6d87c0960fcff06a2c76259d103f1516.jpg)

He's in bed. 他在睡覺。

不用加 the:

(go) to work, (be) at work, start work, finish work

Bye! I'm going to work now. (不是 to the work) 再見! 我現在要去上班了。

I finish work at 5 o'clock every day. 我每天都是五時下班的。

(go) to school, (be) at school, start school, leave school 等等:

What did you learn at school today? (不是 at the school) 今天你在學校裏學習了甚麼?

Some children don't like school. 有些孩子不喜歡上學。

(go) to university / college, (be) at university / college

Helen wants to go to university when she leaves school.

海倫離開學校後想繼續升讀大學。

What did you study at college? 你在大學裏修讀甚麼?

(go) to hospital, (be) in hospital

Jack had an accident. He had to go to hospital. 傑克遇到意外, 他不得不去醫院。

(go) to prison, (be) in prison

Why is he in prison? What did he do? 他為何坐牢? 他做了甚麼?

(go) to church, (be) in / at church

David usually goes to church on Sundays. 大衛通常在星期日去教堂做禮拜。

(go) to bed, (be) in bed

I'm tired. I'm going to bed. (不是 to the bed) 我感到疲倦, 我去睡覺了。

'Where's Jane?' 'She's in bed.' 「珍在哪裏？」「她在睡覺。」

(go) home, (be) at home 等等:

I'm tired. I'm going home. (不是 to home) 我感到疲倦, 我正在回家。

Are you going out tonight, or are you staying at home? 你今晚打算外出還是留在家裏?

要加 the:

(go to) the cinema / the theatre / the bank / the post office / the station / the airport / the city centre

I never go to the theatre, but I go to the cinema a lot. 我從來不去劇院, 但我經常去電影院。

'Are you going to the bank?' 'No, to the post office.' 「你要去銀行嗎？」「不, 我去郵局。」

The number 5 bus goes to the airport; the number 8 goes to the city centre.

5 號巴士去機場; 8 號巴士去市中心。

(go to) the doctor, the dentist

You're not well. Why don't you go to the doctor?

你感到不適, 為何不去看醫生?

I have to go to the dentist tomorrow.

我明天我必須去看牙醫。

# like music I hate exams

# 别用the來表达一般的情况或常理：

I like music, especially classical music. 我喜歡音樂，尤其是古典音樂。（不是 the music ... the classical music）  We don't eat meat very often.（不是 the meat）我們並不常吃肉類。  Life is not possible without water. 生命中不可能沒有水。（不是 The life ... the water）  I hate exams.（不是 the exams）我討厭考試。  Do you know a shop that sells foreign newspapers? 你知道有商業售賣外國報紙嗎？  I'm not very good at writing letters. 我不善於寫信。

# 别用the來表达遊戲和運動：

My favourite sports are football and skiing.（不是 the football ... the skiing）我最喜歡的運動是踢足球和滑雪。

# 别用the來表达語言或學術科目（例如 history / geography / physics / biology 等等）：

Do you think English is difficult?（不是 the English）你認為英語困難嗎？  Tom's brother is studying physics and chemistry. 湯姆的哥哥正修讀物理和化學。

地方（例如洲丶國家丶州丶岛丶城镇等等）

在一般情况下，别用the  $^+$  地方名：

口France is a very large country.（不是the France）法國是一個很大的國家。Cairo is the capital of Egypt. 開羅是埃及的首都。口Corsica is an island in the Mediterranean. 科西嘉島是地中海的一個島。Peru is in South America. 秘魯位於南美洲。

the  $^+$  國家丶岛或山等等的複數名：

the  $^+$  海洋、海丶河流丶運河等名：

the Atlantic (Ocean) 大西洋 the Mediterranean (Sea) 地中海 the (River) Nile 尼羅河 the Suez Canal 蘇伊士河

the Amazon 亞馬逊河 the Black Sea 黑海

在一般情况下，别用the  $^+$  街道或廣場的名：

单元 74

# this / that / these / those

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0c4b0b828cb45021aab323f37802aecf0b2bdfc37dab1011c4c51830f59ec691.jpg)

this / that / these / those 可與名詞（例如 this picture / those girls 等等）一起運用，也可單獨使用：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/de9e675afbeb252627840c98ea5d1a18e6855248834893588ec203c01ec1a60f.jpg)

that = 某些已經發生的事情：

I'm sorry I forgot to phone you. 'That's all right.' 「對不起，我忘了致電給你。」「那不要緊。」 That was a really nice meal. Thank you very much. 那實在是很豐富的一頓飯，十分感謝你。

that = 某人物剛說過的事情：

You're a teacher, aren't you? 'Yes, that's right.' 「你是一位教師，是嗎？」「是的。」 Martin has got a new job. 'Has he? I didn't know that.' 「馬丁找到了一份新工作。」「是嗎？我不知道這事。」 'I'm going on holiday next week.' 'Oh, that's nice.' 「我打算下星期去度假。」「噢！真好。」

this is ... 和 is that ...? 用於電話中的對話：

Hi Sarah, this is David. (this = 説話者) 你好，這是大衛。 Is that Sarah? (that = 對方) 你是薩拉嗎？

this is ... 用於介紹他人：

A: Brian, this is Chris. 布賴恩，這位是克里斯。

B: Hello, Chris — nice to meet you. 你好，克里斯，很高興認識你。 C: Hi. 你好。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fde2ff4e152d59dbf199437fcbca8054e621f353cbf7dd066284ef6ca6e6a389.jpg)

a/an...one

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8835e0eb46c68e3b0d8a5939d47ab592ffd118ccadad8f7a5f8390e0ca4ac66b.jpg)

# some 和 any

some

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4d34cc26826c2080116b75678d27b797be43ba4aa49f20c44af4ee5da2b72c18.jpg)

在肯定句中用 some

I'm going to buy some clothes. 我打算買一些衣服。There's some ice in the fridge. 冰箱内有一些冰。We made some mistakes. 我們犯了一些錯誤。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/034ee7d220157906f302ae2887dd69fdbff9bc6b4a506b6159b38d46ad51fa5f.jpg)

在否定句中用 any

I'm not going to buy any clothes. 我不打算買任何衣服。There isn't any milk in the fridge. 冰箱內一點牛奶也沒有。We didn't make any mistakes. 我們沒有犯任何錯誤。

# 疑問句中的 any 和 some

any 用於大部分（但不是所有）的疑問句中（不是 some）：

Is there any ice in the fridge? 冰箱內有冰嗎？Has he got any friends? 他有朋友嗎？Do you need any help? 你需要幫助嗎？

當向他人提供東西時（Would you like...?）通常會用 some（不是‘any’）：

A: Would you like some coffee? 你想要一些咖啡嗎？B: Yes, please. 好的，謝謝。

又或是當要求某些東西時（例如 Can I have...？等等）：

A: Can I have some soup, please? 請問我可否要一些湯？

B: Yes. Help yourself. 好的，請自便吧。

A: Can you lend me some money? 你可否借一些錢給我？

B: Sure. How much do you need? 當然可以，你需要多少？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a720f4706aa294cbf53e1309fc3761b88c719eb5c7e2f357fa72f43c7121815d.jpg)

# some 和 any 之後可不用名詞：

I didn't take any photographs, but Jane took some. (= some photographs) 我沒有拍任何照片，但珍拍了一些。You can have some coffee, but I don't want any. (= any coffee) 你可以要一些咖啡，但我不想要。I've just made some coffee. Would you like some? (= some coffee) 我剛煮好一些咖啡，你想要一些嗎？'Where's your luggage?' 'I haven't got any.' (= any luggage) 「你的行李在哪裏？」「我沒有任何行李。」'Are there any biscuits?' 'Yes, there are some in the kitchen.' (= some biscuits) 「有沒有餚乾？」「有，廚房裏有一些。」

# something / somebody（或 someone）

She said something. 她說了一些事。I saw somebody（或 someone）. 我看見某人。Would you like something to eat? 你想要一些東西吃嗎？Somebody's at the door. 門外有人。

anything / anybody（或 anyone）She didn't say anything. 她沒有說任何事。I didn't see anybody（或 anyone）. 我沒看見任何人。Are you doing anything tonight? 今晚你有甚麼事做？Where's Sue? Has anybody seen her? 蘇在哪裏？有人見過她嗎？

# not + any no none

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7e41eac1881a1caa072eac01cfb40099de85c2386049569d97f70c1f4abf3199.jpg)

The car park is empty. 那停車場是空的。

There aren't any cars There are no cars in the car park. 停車場裹甚汽車也没有。

How many cars are there in the car park? 停車場裹有多少輛汽車？

None. 一輛也没有。

not (- n't) + any

There aren't any cars in the car park. 停車場裹甚汽車也沒有。

Tracey and Jeff haven't got any children. 特蕾西和傑夫沒有孩子。

You can have some coffee, but I don't want any. 你可以要一些咖啡,但我不想要。

no + 名詞 (例如 no cars / no garden 等等)

no ... = not any 或 not a

There are no cars in the car park. (= there aren't any cars) 停車場裹沒有汽車。

We've got no coffee. (= we haven't got any coffee) 我們沒有咖啡。

It's a nice house, but there's no garden. (= there isn't a garden) 這是一間不錯的房子,但沒有花園。

這是一間不錯的房子,但沒有花園。

no ... 尤其用於 have (got) 和 there is / are 之後。

# 注意

否定動詞 + any = 肯定動詞 + no :

They haven't got any children. 或 They've got no children.

(不是 They haven't got no children) 他們沒有孩子。

There isn't any sugar in your coffee. 或 There's no sugar in your coffee. 你的咖啡裹沒有糖。

# B no 和 none

no + 名詞 (例如 no money / no children 等等) :

We've got no money. 我們沒有金錢。

Everything was OK. There were no problems. 一切都好,沒有問題。

none 之後不加名詞 :

'How much money have you got?' 'None' (= no money) 「你有多少錢？」 「一點也沒有。」

'Were there any problems?' 'No, none' (= no problems) 「有沒有甚麼問題？」 「一個也沒有。」

# C none 和 no-one

none = 0 (零)

no- one = nobody

None 是用以回答 How much? / How many? (物件或人物) 的問題 :

'How much money have you got?' 'None' (= no money)

「你有多少錢？」 「一點也沒有。」

'How many people did you meet?' 'None' (= no people)

「你遇見多少個人？」 「一個也沒有。」

No- one 是用以回答 Who? (人物) 的問題 :

'Who did you meet?' 'No- one' 或 'Nobody' 「你遇見了誰？」 「沒人。」

# 練習

# 罪元 78

# not + anybody / anyone / anything nobody / no-one / nothing

A

not + anybody / anyone nobody / no- one (指人物)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/26a741b2c19a3de4aebf8c41e642d38103b4cc926be267d01832a0d0c957a740.jpg)

There isn't  $\left\{ \begin{array}{l}\text {anybody} \\ \text {anyone} \end{array} \right\}$  in the room. 房間裏沒有任何人。 There is  $\left\{ \begin{array}{l}\text {nobody} \\ \text {no- one} \end{array} \right\}$  in the room. 房間裏沒有任何人。 A: Who is in the room? 誰人在房間裏？ B: Nobody. / No- one. 沒有人。

- body和- one是相同的：anybody  $=$  anyone nobody  $=$  no- one

not + anything nothing (指事物/事情)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9057d9c3fb1b3543846305c10eb33ee5f39e54b0c5d520a21319bce37506f6a5.jpg)

There isn't anything in the bag. 袋裹沒有任何東西。

There is nothing in the bag. 袋裹沒有東西。

A: What's in the bag? 袋裹有甚麼？ B: Nothing. 甚麼也沒有。

not + anybody / anyone

I don't know anybody (或 anyone) here. 我不認識這裏的人。

nobody  $=$  not  $+$  anybody no- one  $=$  not  $+$  anyone

I'm lonely. I've got nobody to talk to. (= I haven't got anybody) 我覺得寂寞，找不到人傾訴。

The house is empty. There is no- one in it. (= There isn't anyone in it.) 那屋子是空的，裏面沒有人。

not + anything

I can't remember anything. 我甚麼事情也記不起。

nothing  $=$  not  $+$  anything

She said nothing. (= She didn't say anything.) 她沒說甚麼。

There's nothing to eat. (= There isn't anything to eat.) 沒甚麼可吃的。

# C

# nobody / no-one / nothing 可用於句子的開頭或在回答問題時單獨使用：

The house is empty. Nobody lives there. (不是 Anybody lives there) 那房子是空的，沒有人住在那裏。 Who did you speak to? 'No- one.' 「你跟誰人說話？」沒有人。

Nothing happened. (不是 Anything happened) 沒有事發生。 'What did you say?' 'Nothing.' 「你說甚麼？」「沒甚麼。」

# 注意

否定動詞 + anybody / anyone / anything 肯定動詞 + nobody / no- one / nothing

He doesn't know anything. (不是 He doesn't know nothing) 他甚麼都不知道。 Don't tell anybody. (不是 Don't tell nobody) 別告訴任何人。 There is nothing to do in this town. (不是 There isn't nothing) 在這城鎮裏沒甚麼可做。

# 檢索

some 和 any  $\Rightarrow$  單元 76 any 和 no  $\Rightarrow$  单元 77 somebody / anything / nowhere 等等  $\Rightarrow$  单元 79

# somebody / anything / nowhere 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b5eff9ce1b557c7aaddcc33a066576fa16f2693395de6de02b6bcb2537d2c35c.jpg)

Somebody (或 Someone) has broken the window. 有人把窗子打破了。

somebody / someone = 某人或有人，但我們不知道是誰

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/99d99b3c2e735409c8b037ca1bbd5e412c51661b03f51c98b561de343e65bd01.jpg)

She has got something in her mouth. 她口裹有些東西。

something = 某物件或有物件，不知道是甚麽

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/cf0bcf9c9c27a3279bb0f8d40a31531ceb893a240b44cc9e97b80d32fe602f44.jpg)

Tom lives somewhere near London. 湯姆住在倫敦附近的某處。

somewhere = 某處，但我們不知道在哪裏

# 用於表示人物 (-body 或 -one)

somebody 或 someone anybody 或 anyone nobody 或 no- one

There is somebody (或 someone) at the door. 門外有人。 Is there anybody (或 anyone) at the door? 門外有沒有人？ There isn't anybody (或 anyone) at the door. 門外沒有任何人。 There is nobody (或 no- one) at the door. 門外沒有人。

- body 和 
- one 是相同的：somebody = someone, nobody = no-one 等等

# 用於表示物件 (-thing)

something anything nothing

Lucy said something, but I didn't understand what she said.

露西說了一些東西，但我不明白她的話。

Are you doing anything at the weekend? 週末你有甚麼事情做嗎？

I was angry, but I didn't say anything. 我感到憤怒，但我甚麼也沒說。

'What did you say?' 'Nothing.' 「你說了甚麼？」「沒甚麼。」

# 用於表示地方 (-where)

somewhere anywhere nowhere

Ruth's parents live somewhere in the south of England. 鲁斯的父母住在英國南部的某處。 Did you go anywhere interesting for your holidays? 你放假時有沒有去過甚麼有趣的地方？ I'm staying here. I'm not going anywhere. 我會留在這裏，不會到任何地方去。 I don't like this town. There is nowhere to go. 我不喜歡這市鎮，這裏沒有甚麼地方可去。

# something / anybody 等等 + 形容詞 (例如 big / cheap / interesting 等等)

Did you meet anybody interesting at the party? 你在那派對中沒有遇到任何有趣的人？

We always go to the same place. Let's go somewhere different.

我們經常去相同的地方，不如去別處不同的吧。

'What's that letter?' 'It's nothing important.' 「那是甚麼信？」「沒甚麼重要的。」

# something / anybody 等等 + to ...

I'm hungry. I want something to eat. (= something that I can eat)

我肚子餓，想吃一點東西。

Tony hasn't got anybody to talk to. (= anybody that he can talk to) 托尼沒有傾訴的對象。

There is nowhere to go in this town. (= nowhere where people can go) 在這市鎮裏沒甚麼地方可去。

# 80

# every 和 all

A every

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5f83cee5d1e9873c78b4e640b0e2a25457ec440805c84dd0fe26abe5dcc075c5.jpg)

Every house in the street is the same. 那街道上的每一間房屋都是一樣的。

every house in the street 街道上的每一間房屋 = all the houses in the street 街道上的所有房屋

every + 單數名詞（例如 every house / every country 等等）：

Sarah has been to every country in Europe. 隨拉去過歐洲每一個國家。

Every summer we have a holiday by the sea. 每年夏天我們都會到海邊度假。

She looks different every time I see her. 我每次見她，她看起來都不同。

every ... 之後用單數動詞：

Every house in the street is the same.（不是 are the same）那街道上的每一間房屋都是一樣的。Every country has a national flag.（不是 have）每個國家都有一面國旗。

試比較 every 和 all 的用法及其分別：

Every student in the class passed the exam. 班裏的每個學生都考試及格。

Every country has a national flag.

每個國家都有一面國旗。

All the students in the class passed the exam. 班裏的所有學生都考試及格。

All countries have a national flag.

所有國家都有一面國旗。

every day 和 all day

every day = 每一天：

how often?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8e6fc59f9574795506263d719374d293e3314969e937740f4f5faf9271021f16.jpg)

# EVERY DAY

It rained every day last week.

上星期每天都下雨。

Bill watches TV for about two hours every

evening. (= on all evenings)

比爾每晚都看大約兩小時電視。

還有 every morning / night / summer 等等

all day = 一整天：

how long?

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4569d22d846857935c2f1d5211904457535f981e80412432d92d88264bb7669a.jpg)

It rained all day yesterday.

昨天下了一整天雨。

On Monday, I watched TV all evening. (= the complete evening) 在星期一，我整個晚上都在看電視。

還有 all morning / night / summer 等等

everybody (或 everyone) / everything / everywhere

everybody 或 everyone (表示人物)

everything

(表示事物)

everywhere

(表示地方)

Everybody (或 Everyone) needs friends.

(= all people need friends) 每個人都需要朋友。

Have you got everything you need?

(= all the things you need) 你已有一切你所需的嗎？

I lost my watch. I've looked everywhere for it.

(= I've looked in all places) 我遺失了手錶，我已找遍每一處了。

注意

everybody / everyone / everything 之後用單數動詞：

Everybody has problems. (不是 Everybody have) 每個人都有自己的問題。

Have you read these books? 你看過這些書嗎？

I often go out with my friends.

我經常與我的朋友外出。

most / most of ..., some / some of ... 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2523f75766aab37026f568af5ba60856c39e2471c6f63d2bd418c6ef829c6281.jpg)  
all

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/830aab3deafdb6cd957a815664c08c1cc210d5345f15025f012181e2df950198.jpg)  
most

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6821c3d7fa65183b47f6a40a8c71297bb7dbd574f8aed8810d50ec2abb15205f.jpg)  
some

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4f6f22aa8845560356f4dce77c9c6bb53023827a6d0324abb2c0631ec784246e.jpg)  
any

no / none / not + any

most / some 等等 + 名詞：

<table><tr><td>all</td><td>cities</td></tr><tr><td>most</td><td>children</td></tr><tr><td>some</td><td>books</td></tr><tr><td>any</td><td>money</td></tr><tr><td>no</td><td></td></tr></table>

Most children like playing.

大部分小孩子都喜歡玩耍。  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  一般的小孩子）

I don't want any money.

我不想要任何金錢。

Some books are better than others.

有些書比其他的好。

He's got no friends. 他沒有朋友。

All cities have the same problems.

所有城市都有同樣的問題。  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  一般的城市）

# 注意

在下列句子中不要用of：

Most people drive too fast.

（不是Most of people）

大部分人都駕駛得太快。

Some birds can't fly. (不是Some of birds)有些鳥兒不會飛。

most of / some of 等等 + the / this / my ... 等等

<table><tr><td>all</td><td>(of)</td><td>the ...</td></tr><tr><td>most</td><td>of</td><td>this / that ...</td></tr><tr><td>some</td><td rowspan="2">none</td><td>these / those ...</td></tr><tr><td>any</td><td>my / your ... 等等</td></tr></table>

Most of the children at this school are under 11 years old. 這學校大部分的孩子都是11歲以下的。

I don't want any of this money.

我不想要這金錢。

Some of these books are very old.

這些書中有些是十分舊的。

None of my friends live near me.

我沒有朋友住在我附近。

# 注意

你可以說all the ... 或all of the ...

（有或沒有of）：

All the students in our class passed the

exam. (或All of the students ...)

我們班中的所有學生都考試及格。

Silvia has lived in London all her life.

（或...all of her life.）西爾維亞一生都住在倫敦。

all of it / most of them / none of us 等等

<table><tr><td>all</td><td>most</td><td>of</td><td>it</td><td>them</td><td>us</td><td>you</td></tr></table>

You can have some of this cake, but not all of it.

你可以要這個蛋糕的一部分，但不是全部。

A: Do you know those people? 你認識那些人嗎？

B: Most of them, but not all of them. 大部分，但不是全部。

Some of us are going out tonight. Why don't you come with us? 我們當中有部分人今晚打算外出，不如你跟我們一起來吧！

I've got a lot of books, but I haven't read any of them. 我有很多書，但一本也沒看過。

'How many of these books have you read?' 'None of them.'

「這些書中有多少本你是看過的？」「一本也沒有。」

# 檢索

the... (children / the children 等等)  $\Rightarrow$  單元72 some 和 any  $\Rightarrow$  单元76 no / none / any  $\Rightarrow$  单元77 all 和 every  $\Rightarrow$  单元80

# 练習

81.1 用括號中的詞語（例如 some / most 等等）完成句子。適當時加上 of（例如 some of / most of 等等）。

1 Most children like playing. (most)

2 Some of ... this money is yours. (some)

3 people never stop talking. (some)

4 the shops in the city centre close at 6:30. (most)

5 You can change your money in banks. (most)

6 I don't like the pictures in the living room. (any)

7 He's lost his money. (all)

8 my friends are married. (none)

9 Do you know the people in this photograph? (any)

10 birds can fly. (most)

11 I enjoyed watching the film, but I didn't like the ending. (most)

12 sports are very dangerous. (some)

13 We can't find anywhere to stay. the hotels are full. (all)

14 You must have this cheese. It's delicious. (some)

15 The weather was bad when we were on holiday. It rained the time. (most)

1 How many of the people are women? Most of them. 2 How many of the boxes are on the table? 3 How many of the men are wearing hats? 4 How many of the windows are open? 5 How many of the people are standing? 6 How much of the money is Ben's?

2 All the students failed the exam.
