# both either neither

both/either/neither用於表示两件事物或两個人物：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/05f54a552fa5e00ccb6897e4c1863c62dcbf923a4786f6a06bc253eb63e6508a.jpg)  
both

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b8ec00b1b7582f912a6bf195445aef4fe1dc59c35ea1f66449f0458ad1bdd488.jpg)  
either

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/56a8a43d3bc2c26c5394f9aabc1392276bc6c98b6dba77f84c3ad16ee0d002cf.jpg)  
neither (not + either)

Rebecca has two children. Both are married. (both = 两者) 屬貝卡有兩個孩子,兩個都已婚。Would you like tea or coffee? You can have either. (either = 两者之一)你想要茶還是咖啡?你可以二選其一。

A: Do you want to go to the cinema or the theatre? 你想去電影院還是劇院？B: Neither. I want to stay at home. (neither = 两者都不) 两者都不想,我想留在家裏。

# 试比較

either和neither的用法及其分别：

'Either. I dont mind' (= tea or coffee)「其中一樣也可,我無所。」'Would you like tea or coffee?' 'I don't want either'（不是I don't want neither）「我两者都不要。」「你想要茶還是咖啡？」 'Neither' (= not tea or coffee)「两者都不要。」

# both/either/neither+名词

<table><tr><td>both</td><td>+複数名詞:</td><td>both</td><td>windows / books / children 等等</td></tr><tr><td>either</td><td rowspan="2">+單數名詞:</td><td>either</td><td rowspan="2">window / book / child 等等</td></tr><tr><td>neither</td><td>neither</td></tr></table>

Last year I went to Paris and Rome. I liked both cities very much.

去年我去過巴黎和羅馬,兩個城市我都很喜歡。

First I worked in an office, and later in a shop. Neither job was very interesting.

最初我在辦公室裏工作,後來到商店裏工作。兩份工作都不怎有趣味。

There are two ways from here to the station. You can go either way.

從這裏到事站有兩條路線,你可以走其中一條。

both of.../either of.../neither of...

<table><tr><td>both</td><td>(of)</td><td>the ...</td></tr><tr><td>either neither</td><td>of</td><td>these / those ... my / your / Paul&#x27;s ... 等等</td></tr></table>

Neither of my parents is British. 我的父母親都不是英國人。

I haven't read either of these books. 這兩本書我都沒有看過。

你可以說 both of the / those / my ... 或

both the / those / my ... (of 可用也可不用):

I like both of those pictures. 或 I like both those pictures. 那兩幅畫我都喜歡。

Both of Paul's sisters are married. 或 Both Paul's sisters are married. 保羅的兩個姊姊都已婚。但是 Neither of Paul's sisters is married. (不是 Neither Paul's sisters) 保羅的兩個姊姊都未婚。

both of them/neither of us等等+名词

<table><tr><td>both either neither</td><td>of</td><td>them us you</td></tr></table>

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e976381b2af0acd847d81f0ea112b377e103734a5dcde2f6a5f72ca0d10e1f87.jpg)

Paul has got two sisters. Both of them are married. 保羅有兩個姊姊,兩個都已婚。

Sue and I didn't eat anything. Neither of us was hungry. 蘇和我沒有吃東西,我們兩個都不覺得餓。

Who are those two people? I don't know either of them. 那兩個人是誰?兩個我都不認識。

# 82.1 在横線上填上both/either/neither，適當時加上of。

6 We went away for two days, but the weather wasn't good. It rained days.

7 A: I bought two newspapers. Which one do you want?

B: It doesn't matter which one.

8 I invited Donna and Mike to the party, but them came.

9 'Do you go to work by car or by bus?' I always walk.

10 'Which jacket do you prefer, this one or that one?' 'I don't like them.'

11 'Do you work or are you a student?' I work and I'm a student too.

12 Paula and I didn't know the time because us had a watch.

13 Helen has got two sisters and a brother. sisters are married.

14 Helen has got two sisters and a brother. I've met her brother, but I haven't met her sisters.

# a lot much many

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e4e8bbec0cb869a8012438337506707bc9350373dda9409e071c09c4be671465.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/48cb21c88043a6924b94d31eedee5874c7dbbc23e143be49fff76e6fe82dd0b6.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6efc2de96e2a8e2fb96ed8085432c5e46e8356872da2f2a80aa0f9e6ad4577a7.jpg)  
a lot of books

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/71801d69d4fcec9f407b13549d3b97398cd1ee4155678797b6d91eaf0f971171.jpg)  
not many books

# much  $^+$  不可數名词:

(much food / much money 等等):

Did you buy much food? 你買了很多食物嗎？ We haven't got much luggage. 我們的行李不多。 How much money do you want? 你想要多少錢？ A: Have you got any money? 你有錢嗎？ B: I've got some, but not much. 我有一些，但不是很多。

# many  $^+$  稔數名：

(many books / many people 等等):

Did you buy many books? 你買了很多書嗎？ We don't know many people. 我們認識的人不多。 How many photos did you take? 你拍了多少張照片？ A: Did you take any photos? 你有拍照嗎？ B: I took some, but not many. 我拍了一些，但不是很多。

# a lot of  $^+$  上述兩種類型的名：

We bought a lot of food. 我們買了很多食物。 Paula hasn't got a lot of free time. 保拉亞沒有很多空間時間。

單數動詞  $^+$  不可數名詞：

There is a lot of food / money / water (單數動詞) 有很多食物 / 金錢 / 水...

We bought a lot of books. 我們買了很多書。 Did they ask a lot of questions? 他們問了你很多問題嗎？

複數動詞  $^+$  複數名詞：

There are a lot of trees / shops / people (單數動詞) 有很多樹 / 萬店 / 人...

A lot of people speak English. (不是 speaks) 很多人說英語。

# much 用於疑問句和否定句：

Do you drink much coffee? 你喝很多咖啡嗎？ I don't drink much coffee. 我並不怎喝咖啡。

much 延不常用於肯定句：

I drink a lot of coffee. (不是 I drink much coffee) 我喝很多咖啡。

'Do you drink much coffee?' 'Yes, a lot.' (不是 Yes, much) 「你喝很多咖啡嗎？」「是的，我喝很多。」

many 和 a lot of 用於所有類型的句子 (肯定句 / 否定句 / 確問句):

We've got many friends / a lot of friends. 我們有很多朋友。

We haven't got many friends / a lot of friends. 我們的朋友不多。

Have you got many friends / a lot of friends? 你有很多朋友嗎？

# much 和 a lot 之後可不用名詞：

Donna spoke to me, but she didn't say much. 當娜跟我說話，但她說得不多。

'Do you watch TV much?' 'No, not much.' (= not often) 「你經常看電視嗎？」「不，我不常看。」

We like films, so we go to the cinema a lot. (不是 go to the cinema much)

我們喜歡看電影，所以我們經常到電影院去。

I don't like him very much.

我不太喜歡他。

# (a) little (a) few

(a) little + 不可数名词:

(a) little water 
(a) little time 
(a) little money 
(a) little soup

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6620ddb38b4bc8e7403dd4bbb5b174b45304f8b360d6747752dd5629148a1a0d.jpg)

a little water 少量水

(a) few + 簡數名詞:

(a) few books 
(a) few questions 
(a) few people 
(a) few days

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/cad7a2c0dd162b587b73b9ee36cb856e1a59eb317ab2027bab858174199738ad.jpg)

a few books 數本書

a little = 一點,不是很多:

She didn't eat anything, but she drank a little water. 她沒有吃東西,但喝了一點水。 I speak a little Spanish. (= some Spanish but not much) 我會說一點西班牙語。 A: Can you speak Spanish? 你會說西班牙語嗎？ B: A little. 一點。

a few = 少數,不是很多:

Excuse me, I have to make a few phone calls. 對不起,我要打幾個電話。 We're going away for a few days. 我們打算離開數天。 I speak a few words of Spanish. 我會說幾句西班牙語。 A: Are there any shops near here? 附近有沒有商店？

B: Yes, a few. 有數間。

little (不用 a) = 近乎沒有:

There was little food in the fridge. It was nearly empty. 冰箱裏只有很少食物,近乎是空的。

也可用 very little:

Dan is very thin because he eats very little. (= nearly nothing) 丹十分瘦,因為他吃得很少。

few (不用 a) = 近乎沒有:

There were few people in the theatre. It was nearly empty. 劃腕裏很少人,近乎是空的。

也可用 very few:

Your English is very good. You make very few mistakes. 你的英語十分正確,很少錯誤。

little 和 a little

a little 帶有肯定的意思:

They have a little money, so they're not poor. (= they have some money) 他們有一點金錢,所以他們並不貧窮。

little (或 very little) 帶有否定的意思:

They have little money. They are very poor. (= nearly no money) 他們只有很少錢,十分貧窮。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ffeda6b418474ed75cf1da8f41236862c72376c16d56c11949bc95d15551985f.jpg)

few 和 a few

a few 帶有肯定的意思:

I've got a few friends, so I'm not lonely. (= I've got some friends) 我有幾個朋友,所以我不覺得寂寞。

few (或 very few) 帶有否定的意思:

I'm sad and I'm lonely. I've got few friends. (= nearly no friends) 我覺得難過和寂寞,我只有很少朋友。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/06c04517b32bc998b72f696c2916fefec9530e64cb3fe60252384581a113bfbd.jpg)

# old / nice / interesting 等等 形容詞(adjectives)

形容詞+名詞（例如nice day／blue eyes等等）

# 形容詞+名詞

It's a nice day today. 今天天氣很好。Laura has got brown eyes. 羅拉有一雙棕色的眼睛。There's a very old bridge in this village. 這村落裹有一條很古老的橋。Do you like Italian food? 你喜歡吃意大利菜嗎？I don't speak any foreign languages. 我不會說任何外國語言。There are some beautiful yellow flowers in the garden. 花園裹有一些美麗的黃色花朵。

# 形容詞用於名詞它畜：

形容詞用於名詞它畜：- They live in a modern house.（不是a house modern）他們住在一間現代化的房屋裏。- Have you met any famous people?（不是people famous）你曾遇過任何名人嗎？

形容词的词尾永遠都是不變的：

a different place different places (不是differents)

# be（例如am/is/was等等）+形容词

be（例如am/is/was等等）+形容詞- The weather is nice today. 今天天氣很好。- These flowers are very beautiful. 這些花朵十分美麗。- Are you cold? Shall I close the window? 你覺得冷嗎？我把窗子關上好嗎？- I'm hungry. Can I have something to eat? 我肚子餓，可否吃一點東西？- The film wasn't very good. It was boring. 那齣電影沈悶乏味，不太好看。- Please be quiet. I'm reading. 請保持肅靜，我正在閱讀。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/140ed5c69402fab240a42f95f876eae8efb3130568ebf1378eaaf30eea070fde.jpg)

look / feel / smell / taste / sound + 形容詞

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4569c5b5b4eb311d9e43c9b013a306d9c13bf8e0f54c7f50e9a4e8d612486292.jpg)

You look tired.'Yes,I feel tired.'「你看來很疲倦。」「是的，我感到疲倦。」

Gary told me about his new job. It sounds very interesting.

加里告我與他的新工作有關的事，起來十分有趣。

I'm not going to eat this fish. It doesn't smell good. 我打算不吃那條魚，它有異味。

# quickly / badly / suddenly 等等副词(adverbs)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/01757d78a191d242d8df6d4704a3b711ca88c1884be438fffe463feb1926767e.jpg)

He ate his dinner very quickly. 他很快地吃了晚餐。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/16ad13a4e455ec9f61e91f66ca1e4091a42540a12a7a859229b23daf6e1d3453.jpg)

Suddenly the shelf fell down. 那個架子突然倒下。

quickly 和 suddenly 是副词。

形容词  $^+$  - ly  $\rightarrow$  副词：

形容词 quick bad sudden careful heavy 副词 quickly badly suddenly carefully heavily 等等

拼寫方法： 檢索 → 附錄 5 easy → easily heavy → heavily

副词表達出某事情如何發生或某人物怎樣做某事情：

The train stopped suddenly. 火車突然停下來了。  I opened the door slowly. 我慢慢地打開門。  Please listen carefully. 請用心地聆聽。  I understand you perfectly. 我完全明白你。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d22d4522c69738105e6a9e0c9eee16fe45df745fea8a47b9b921a8563cbae506.jpg)

# 試比較

形容词：

Sue is very quiet. 蘇是十分文靜的。

Be careful! 小心一點！

It was a bad game. 這是一場很糟糕的比赛。

I felt nervous. (= I was nervous) 我覺得緊張。

副词

Sue speaks very quietly. (不是 speaks very quiet) 蘇輕聲地說話。  Listen carefully! (不是 listen careful) 小心地聆聽吧！  Our team played badly. (不是 played bad) 我們的隊伍打得很差勁。  I waited nervously. 我緊張地等待着。

hard fast late early這些語既是形容词 也是副词：

Sue's job is very hard. 蘇的工作很艱辛。

Ben is a fast runner. 本是一個跑得很快的人。

The bus was late / early. 那巴士遲 / 早了。

Sue works very hard. (不是 hardly) 蘇工作得十分辛劳。

Ben can run fast. 本跑得很快。

I went to bed late / early. 我遲 / 早睡覺。

# good（形容词）→well（副词）

Your English is very good. 你的英语十分好。  It was a good game. 這是一場很精彩的比赛。

You speak English very well. (不是 very good) 你説英语説得很好。

Our team played well. 我們的隊伍打得很出色。

well也是形容词（=没有病，身體很好）：

How are you? 'I'm very well, thank you. And you?' 「你好嗎？」「我很好，謝謝。你呢？」

# 单元 87

# old / older expensive / more expensive

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/be04d0ecf43310632f1e4425d49402e25a968e84070bc37ab97e7c8c08ec3bed.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ade2ae643bb12cb3f99d951e0d6299506eeb4f428a1d5ec496476a056d7502e5.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a9e9581f8889eb46d5221af40d1004aa49f23f812d7c7ee3ea9a86717516ae00.jpg)

old older

heavy heavier

expensive more expensive

older/heavier/more expensive都是比较级。比较级是- er（例如older）或more...（例如more expensive）。

- er（例如older/heavier等等）：

簡短的語（一個音節）  $\rightarrow$  - er:

old  $\rightarrow$  older slow  $\rightarrow$  slower cheap  $\rightarrow$  cheaper nice  $\rightarrow$  nicer late  $\rightarrow$  later big  $\rightarrow$  bigger

拼寫方法：检索  $\Rightarrow$  附錄5 big  $\rightarrow$  bigger hot  $\rightarrow$  hotter thin  $\rightarrow$  thinner

以- y结尾的語  $\Rightarrow$  - ier:

easy  $\rightarrow$  easier heavy  $\rightarrow$  heavier early  $\rightarrow$  earlier

B Rome is old, but Athens is older.（不是more old）馬馬然古者，但雅典更古者。

Is it cheaper to go by car or by train?（不是more cheap）乘汽車和乘火車，哪樣較便宜？

Helenwantsabiggercar.海偷想要一辆较大的汽車。

This coat is OK, but I think the other one is nicer. 這件大衣算是不錯，但我認為另一件較好。

Don't take the bus. It's easier to take a taxi.（不是more easy）不要乘巴士。乘的士較容易一些。

far  $\rightarrow$  further:

How far is it to the station? A mile? 'No, it's further. About two miles. 「這裏離車站有多遠？有一哩嗎？」「不止，是更遠，約有兩哩。」

more...

長的词語  $(2 / 3 / 4$  個音節）  $\rightarrow$  more...：

careful  $\rightarrow$  more careful polite  $\rightarrow$  more polite

expensive  $\rightarrow$  more expensive interesting  $\rightarrow$  more interesting

You must be more careful. 你必須更加小心。

I don't like my job. I want to do something more interesting.

我不喜歡自己的工作，我想做一些更有趣的事情。

Is it more expensive to go by car or by train?

乘汽車和乘火車，哪樣較昂貴？

good/well  $\rightarrow$  better bad  $\rightarrow$  worse

The weather wasn't very good yesterday, but it's better today. 昨天天氣不是很好，但今天較好。

Do you feel better today? 'No, I feel worse.' 「你今天覺得好一點嗎？」「不，我覺得更差。」

Which is worse — a headache or a toothache? 哪一樣較糟——頭痛還是牙痛？

# older than ... more expensive than ...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/54a95b50e9fd2650c67b2e9533872c9b589cf879ba617c5cf7e1330969c10892.jpg)

She's taller than him. 她长得比他高。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3f8659d748616c60bf5e978b629e9490f56047d8134df7a2fbcd578168f14967.jpg)

The Europa Hotel is more expensive than the Grand. 依洛柏酒店的收费胶索单酒店费。

than 用於比較級之後（例如 older than.../more expensive than...等等）：

- Athens is older than Rome. 雅典比羅馬歷史更悠久。- Are oranges more expensive than bananas? 橙是不是較香蕉昂貴？- It's easier to take a taxi than to take the bus. 乘的士比乘巴士更加容易。- 'How are you today?' 'Not bad. Better than yesterday.' 「你今天怎樣？」「不太差，已較昨天好了。」- The restaurant is more crowded than usual. 那間餐廳較平時擁擠。

我們通常用than me／than him／than her／than us／than them：

- I can run faster than him. 或 I can run faster than he can. 我跑得比他快。- You are a better singer than me. 或 You are a better singer than I am. 你唱歌比我好。- I got up earlier than her. 或 I got up earlier than she did. 我比她更加早起。

more / less than...

- A: How much did your shoes cost? £50? 你的鞋子要多少钱？五十镑？- B: No, more than that. (= more than £50) 不，比還要多。

- The film was very short—less than an hour. 那齣電影很短，少於一小時。

- They've got more money than they need.

他們擁有的金錢較他們所需的多。

- You go out more than me. 你外出的次數較我多。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a6d29fd92fb7003e726c17de0e75660e18aec974b4e36b26f3be6038e581f249.jpg)

a bit older / much older 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/449ed0966c9b63411eeeab73ff142cf6b43a371c405ddf3bf97e4e279af54749.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/52fdbf77ca78002a64da5aebb73306fa5df01a757798aaec1f9e73ff3425ca07.jpg)

Box A is a bit bigger than Box B. 箱 A 比箱 B 大少許。

Box C is much bigger than Box D. 箱 C 比箱 D 大很多。

bigger加拿大比法國大很多。a bit older□ Sue is a bit older than Gary — she's 25 and he's 24. better than ...蘇比加里年長一點——她25歲，而他則24歲。much more difficult□ The hotel was much more expensive than I expected. more expensive那酒店比我預期的昂貴得多。□ You go out much more than me.你外出的次數遠較我的多。

- Canada is much bigger than France. 加拿大比法國大很多。- Sue is a bit older than Gary — she's 25 and he's 24. 蘇比加里年長一點——她25歲，而他則24歲。- The hotel was much more expensive than I expected. 那酒店比我預期的昂貴得多。- You go out much more than me. 你外出的次數遠較我的多。

# not as ... as

not as ... as

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/79713bca769e5cd83318d84c556a311f9b5cf79a5454589cd54407fdfb8b547b.jpg)

She's old, but she's not as old as he is. 她跳然年老,但不及他年老。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/015bcc56a433b8870feb4b9316526347c4e14ff380cd21db765a5f20fd6e17d2.jpg)

Box A isn't as big as Box B. 箱A不及箱B大。

Rome is not as old as Athens. (= Athens is older) 羅馬不及雅典那磨歷史悠久。

The Grand Hotel isn't as expensive as the Europa. (= the Europa is more expensive) 豪華酒店不及依洛柏酒店那磨昂贵。

I don't play tennis as often as you. (= you play more often) 我不像你那樣經常打網球。

The weather is better than it was yesterday. It isn't as cold. (= as cold as it was yesterday)

今天天氣較昨天好,並不如昨天那麼寒冷。

not as much as ... / not as many as ...

I haven't got as much money as you. (= you've got more money) 我沒有你那麼多錢。

I don't know as many people as you. (= you know more people) 我不及你認識那麼多人。

I don't go out as much as you. (= you go out more) 我不如你那麼經常外出。

試比較 not as ... as 和 than 的用法及其分別:

Rome is not as old as Athens. 羅馬不及雅典那磨歷史悠久。

Athens is older than Rome. (不是 older as Rome) 雅典較羅馬歷史悠久。

Tennis isn't as popular as football. 網球不及足球那樣受歡迎。 Football is more popular than tennis. 足球較網球受歡迎。

I don't go out as much as you. 我不如你那麼經常外出。

You go out more than me. 你外出的次數較我的多。

as me / as him / as her 等等

She's not as old as him. 或 She's not as old as he is. 她不及他年紀大。

You don't work as hard as me. 或 You don't work as hard as I do. 你不及我那磨努力工作。

the same as ...

The weather today is the same as yesterday. 今天天氣跟昨天一樣。

My hair is the same colour as yours. 我頭髮的顏色跟你的一樣。

I arrived at the same time as Tim. 我和蒂姆同一時間到達。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/732ec4591f11b3e14e339dcc86a9e068da87aac2a01b525db3fd965a12c4813c.jpg)

# HOTEL PRICES IN KINTON (Per room per night)

Europa Hotel 140 Grosvenor 100 Grand Hotel 125 Bennets 90 Royal 120 Carlton 85 Astoria 115 Stai 75 Palace 110 Station 75

The Europa Hotel is more expensive than the Grand. 依洛柏酒店较豪華酒店昂贵。The Europa Hotel is more expensive than all the other hotels in the city. 依洛柏酒店较市中其他的酒店昂贵。The Europa Hotel is the most expensive hotel in the city. 依洛柏酒店是市中最昂贵的酒店。

bigger/older/more expensive 等等是比较级： 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元87 biggest/oldest/most expensive等等是最高級。

B 最高级的形式是- est(oldest)或most...（most expensive）：

簡短的词語（例如old／cheap／nice等等）  $\longrightarrow$  the- est:old  $\longrightarrow$  the oldest cheap  $\longrightarrow$  the cheapest nice  $\longrightarrow$  the nicest但 good  $\longrightarrow$  the best bad  $\longrightarrow$  the worst

拼寫方法：  $\Rightarrow$  附錄5 big  $\longrightarrow$  the biggest hot  $\longrightarrow$  the hottest

以- y结尾的词語（例如easy/heavy等等）  $\longrightarrow$  the- iest:easy  $\longrightarrow$  the easiest heavy  $\longrightarrow$  the heaviest pretty  $\longrightarrow$  the prettiest

長的词語（例如careful／expensive／interesting等等）  $\longrightarrow$  the most...：careful  $\longrightarrow$  the most careful interesting  $\longrightarrow$  the most interesting

the oldest.../the most expensive...等等（與the速用）：

The church is very old. It's the oldest building in the town. (=it is older than all the other buildings）那教堂十分古舊，是市中最古老的建筑物。 What is the longest river in the world? 世界上最長的河流是哪一條？ Money is important, but it isn't the most important thing in life. 金錢然重要，但不是生命中最重要的東西。 Excuse me,where is the nearest bank？對不起，請問最近的銀行在哪裹？

the oldest／the best／the most expensive等等（之後可不接用名）：

Ken is a good player, but he isn't the best in the team. (the best  $=$  the best player）健是一個優秀的運動員，但他不是隧中最好的。

最高級  $^+$  I've ever.../you've ever...等等

The film was very bad. I think it's the worst film I've ever seen. 那電影一分差勁，我為它是我所看過的電影中最差的。 What is the most unusual thing you've ever done? 你做過最不常的事情是甚？

2 Everest

3

4

6

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e1949771b215bfd1312b0f2165da392dc19b8eb73cee6246223652628b4eea26.jpg)

She isn't going to take a taxi. 她不打算乘的士。 She hasn't got enough money. 她没有足夠的金錢。

He can't reach the shell. 他不能觸及書架。 He isn't tall enough. 他不夠高。

enough + 名詞（例如 enough money / enough people 等等）：

Is there enough milk in your coffee? 'Yes, thank you.'

「你咖啡裹的牛奶足钩吗？」「了，謝謝。」

We wanted to play football, but we didn't have enough players.

我們想踢足球，但隊員人數不足。

Why don't you buy a car? You've got enough money. (not money enough) 你為何不買一輛汽車？你有足夠的金錢。

enough 之後可以不接用名詞：

I've got some money, but not enough to buy a car. (= I need more money to buy a car) 我有一些金錢，但不足夠買一輛汽車。

'Would you like some more to eat?' 'No, thanks. I've had enough.'

「你想要多一點東西吃嗎？」「不用了，謝謝。我已足夠了。」

You're always at home. You don't go out enough. 你經常留在家裏，外出的時間不夠。

形容詞 + enough（例如 good enough / tall enough 等等）：

'Shall we sit outside?' 'No, it isn't warm enough.' （不是 enough warm）「我們到外面坐好嗎？」「不，外面不夠暖。」

Can you hear the radio? Is it loud enough for you? 你聽到收音機嗎？音量夠大嗎？

Don't buy that coat. It's nice, but it isn't long enough. (= it's too short) 別買那件大衣，它雖然好，但不夠長。

# 注意

enough 的用法及其詞序

enough + 名詞 但 形容詞 + enough

enough money tall enough enough time good enough enough people old enough

enough for 和 enough to 的用法有別：

enough for somebody / something

enough to do something

enough for somebody / something to do something.

This pullover isn't big enough for me. 這件套頭毛衣對我來說不夠大。 I haven't got enough money for a new car. 我沒有足夠的金錢買一輛新車。 I haven't got enough money to buy a new car. （不是 for buy）我沒有足夠的金錢買一輛新車。 Is your English good enough to have a conversation? （不是 for have）你的英语能力是否足夠跟他人談話？ There aren't enough chairs for everybody to sit down. 没有足夠的椅子供每一個人坐。

His shoes are too big for him. 他的鞋對他來說太大了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b8dd9c01e6575167b41767b9629490e40316b47401fa3eeffe476bdf2cc46e31.jpg)

# B

too + 形容詞 / 副詞（例如 too big / too hard 等等）：

Can you turn the radio down? It's too loud. (= louder than I want) 你可否把收音機的音量調低？實在太大聲了。 I can't work. I'm too tired. 我不能工作，因我太疲倦了。 I think you work too hard. 我認為你工作得太辛勞。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d0baf9d5a7d0b34daa86bbf5b6fe3a66ce63d00ab1e89ea80777054c07fc0eca.jpg)

# C

too much / too many = 較你想要的多，較足夠的多：

I don't like the weather here. There is too much rain. (= more rain than is good) 我不喜歡這麼的天氣，雨水太多了。

Let's go to another restaurant. There are too many people here. 不如到另一間餐廳去，這裏太多人了。 Emily studies all the time. I think she studies too much. 艾米莉總是在讀書，我想她讀得太多了。 Traffic is a problem in this town. There are too many cars. 交通是這市鎮的問題，汽車太多了。

# D

試比較 too 和 not enough 的用法及其分別：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f49c880731bf7b7826f5473bd0445d3af5bd7955f95f2e2c10501854dbc9a693.jpg)

The hat is too big for him. 這頂帽子對他來說太大了。 The radio is too loud. Can you turn it down, please? 收音機的音量太大了，請問你可否把它調低一點？ There's too much sugar in my coffee. (= more sugar than I want) 我的咖啡裏有太多糖了。 I don't feel very well. I ate too much. 我不太舒服，我吃得太多了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b2abeee3b6681e6fd9210c22db09dac910d4b2f071e249ed9b98dd4b708ce93c.jpg)

not big enough

The hat isn't big enough for him. (= it's too small) 這頂帽子對他來說不夠大。 The radio isn't loud enough. Can you turn it up, please? 收音機的音量不夠大，請問你可否把它調高一點？ There's not enough sugar in my coffee. (= I need more sugar) 我的咖啡裏不夠糖。 You're very thin. You don't eat enough. 你十分瘦，你吃得不夠。

too ... for 和 too ... to 的用法有別：

too ... for somebody / something

too ... to do something

too ... for somebody to do something

These shoes are too big for me. 這雙鞋子對我來說太大了。 It's a small house — too small for a large family. 這是一間細小的房屋——對一個大家庭來說太細小了。 I'm too tired to go out. (不是 for go out) 我疲倦得不能外出了。 It's too cold to sit outside. 天氣冷得令人無法在外面坐。 She speaks too fast for me to understand. 她說得太快，我聽不明白。

# He speaks English very well.  阔序1（word order 1）

動語+賓語

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2f0167fccbe5803ffaf5086976c9f5f402b0b4633e7764756f0736944b73a028.jpg)

動詞(reads)和賓語(a newspaper)通常一起使用：

Sue reads a newspaper every day. (不是 Sue reads every day a newspaper) 蘇每天看報紙。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f4ece6a94e0a45359ee9a3c5dbbdd0af8bda039ea724bc6d32e053dcfaa20e6b.jpg)

Sue（主語） a newspaper（賓語）

# 动词+宾語

He speaks English very well. (不是 He speaks very well English) 他説英語説得很好。 I like Italian food very much. (不是 I like very much...) 我非常喜歡意大利菜。 Did you watch television all evening? (不是 Did you watch all evening...) 整個晚上你都在看電視嗎？ Paul often wears a black hat. (不是 Paul wears often...) 保羅常常戴着一頂黑帽子。 We invited a lot of people to the party. 我們邀請了很多人參加派對。 I opened the door slowly. 我慢慢地開門。 Why do you always make the same mistake? 你為何經常犯同樣的錯誤？ I'm going to borrow some money from the bank. 我打算向銀行借一些錢。

# 地點和時間

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/580765065491a61bbe4b0f2e96142e584b46c7c65b54701afe8fce842f2a58b2.jpg)

地點(where?)通常放在時間（when?)之前：

We went to a party last night. (不是 We went last night to a party) 昨晚我們去了一個派對。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6aedad8ba04cbd88016333fa025e7959a1ee31ad1b55e1c504f9d17a6ca6fd1b.jpg)

# 瞭元

# always / usually / often 等等 94 調序2（word order 2）

A 這些語（例如always/never等等）經常配合動词出现於句子的中間：

always often ever rarely also already all usually sometimes never seldom just still both

Mybrotherneverspeaksto me.我的哥哥不跟我話。

She'salwayslate.她總是遇到。

Do you often go to restaurants?你經常到餐廳嗎？

I sometimes eat too much.（或Sometimes I eat too much.）我有時吃得太多。

Don't forget to phone Laura. I've already phoned her.

「别忘記打電話給努拉。」「我已打了電話給她了。」

I've got three sisters. They're all married.我有三個姊姊，她們全都已婚。

B always/never等等用於動词之前：

# 助词

always go often play never have 等等 等等

Ialways drink coffee in the morning.（不是I drink always coffee)我總是在早上喝咖啡。

Helen often goes to London.（不是 Helen goes often）海倫經常去倫敦。

You sometimes look unhappy.你有時看來不開心。

They usually have dinner at 7 o'clock.他們通常於七時正吃晚飯。

We rarely（或seldom）watch television.我們很少看電視。

Richard is a good footballer. He also plays tennis and volleyball.（不是He plays also tennis）理查德是一個優秀的足球員，他還打網球和排球。

I've got three sisters. They all live in London.我有三個姊姊，她們全住在倫敦。

但always/never等等用於am/is/are/was/were之後：

am is are was were

I am always tired.（不是I always am tired）我總是疲倦。

They are never at home during the day.他們在日間從不在家。

It is usually very cold here in winter.冬天時這裏通常很寒冷。

When I was a child, I was often late for school.我小時候上學經常遇到。

'Where's Laura?' 'She's still in bed.'「努拉在哪裹？」「她仍在睡覺。」

I've got two brothers. They're both doctors.我有兩個哥哥，他們都是醫生。

C always/never等等用於兩個動词之間（例如have...been/can...find等等）：

動词1

動词2

# 单元 95 still yet already

still

an hour ago

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e7a3b5f2725859e4056b8d46d2b07a0d2dc7da12e0e70137d6459b0c538d564f.jpg)

An hour ago it was raining. 一小時前正在下雨。

The rain hasn't stopped 雨還沒有停

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3a9d318ce45cd54872df56fe5b972e562606dd25e13b6f9dffb26562b1759f45.jpg)

It is still raining now. 現在仍在下雨。

# still = 某事如之前一様：

still = 某事如之前一様：  I had a lot to eat, but I'm still hungry. (= I was hungry before, and I'm hungry now)  我吃了很多東西，但現在仍然覺得餓。  'Did you sell your car?' 'No, I've still got it.' 「你賣掉了你的汽車嗎？」「沒有，我仍然擁有它。」  'Do you still live in Barcelona?' 'No, I live in Madrid now.'  「你仍住在巴塞隆納嗎？」「不，我現住在馬德里。」

yet

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ca4accaa5e64f7eaf8aa208aac60874bc9956ab352795c75e180904b54e6fcee.jpg)

Twenty minutes ago they were waiting for Bill. 二十分鐘前他們在等比爾。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/171446cba889a8ecaf44e12733343110eea1034dd675d457043c4276371d7e2e.jpg)

They are still waiting for Bill. Bill hasn't come yet. 他們仍然在等比爾。比爾還沒到來。

yet = 直至現在：

yet用於否定句（He hasn't come yet.）和疑問句（Has he come yet?）中。yet通常放在句末。

A: Where's Emma? 埃瑪在哪裹？

B: She isn't here yet. (= she will be here, but until now she hasn't come) 她還沒到來。

A: What are you doing this evening? 今晚你打算做甚麼？

B: I don't know yet. (= I will know later, but I don't know at the moment) 我仍未知道。

A: Are you ready to go yet? 你準備好離去嗎？

B: Not yet. In a minute. (= I will be ready, but I'm not ready at the moment) 尚未，等一會兒。

A: Have you finished with the newspaper yet? 你看完那份報紙了嗎？

B: No, I'm still reading it. 尚未，我還在看它。

试比較 yet 和 still 的用法及其分別：

She hasn't gone yet. = She's still here. (不是 she is yet here) 她仍未離開。 = 她仍在這裏。

I haven't finished eating yet. = I'm still eating.

我還沒有吃完東西。 = 我仍在吃東西。

# already = 比預期的早：

'What time is Joe coming?' 'He's already here.'

「喬甚麼時候來？」「他已到了。」(=喬比預期中早到達)

'I'm going to tell you what happened.' 'That's not necessary. I already know.'

「我會告訴你發生了甚麼事。」「不必了，我已經知道。」

Sarah isn't coming to the cinema with us. She has already seen the film.

薩拉不打算來我們一起去看電影，她已經看過的電影了。

# 檢索

already / yet + 現在完成式  $\Rightarrow$  單元 16 詞序 (still / already)  $\Rightarrow$  單元 94

# Give me that book! Give it to me!

give lend pass send show

這些動词（例如give/lend等等）之後可以有兩種結構：

give something to somebody

I gave the keys to Sarah. 我把匙給了薩拉。

give somebody something

I gave Sarah the keys.

我給薩拉匙。

Did you get it?</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>Nicole</td><td>showed</td><td>us</td><td>我寄了一封電郵給你,你收到了嗎?</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>Can you</td><td>pass</td><td>me</td><td>her holiday photos. 尼科爾給我們看她的度假照片。</td></tr></table>

也可以用 buy / get somebody something':

I bought my mother some flowers. (= I bought some flowers for my mother.) 我買了一些花給媽媽。 Can you get me a newspaper when you go out? (= get a newspaper for me) 你外出時可否給我買一份報紙？

# 试比較

give的兩種用法及其詞序的分別：

I gave the keys to Sarah. 我把匙給了薩拉。

I gave Sarah the keys. (不是I gave to Sarah the keys.) 我給薩拉匙。

That's my book. Can you give it to me? 那是我的書,你可否把它給我?

Can you give me that book? (不是Can you give to me that book?) 你可否給我那本書？

如用it或them代表某事物時，應該用第一種結構（give something to somebody）：

I gave it to her. (不是I gave her it) 我把這個給她。

Here are the keys. Give them to your father. (不是Give your father them) 鑰匙在這裏,把它們給你的爸爸。

# 联元 97

# and but or so because

and but or so because

這些词語（連接词）用於連接兩句句子，即是把兩句短句結合成一句較長的句子。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8b5ad52f7b6e6ef25d0eb23d9b7b8ddf6b3cc57e2f4230b48242dbcb1fbe062d.jpg)

and / but / or

<table><tr><td>句子A</td><td>句子B</td><td>*無需重複‘we’和‘she’。</td></tr><tr><td>We stayed at home</td><td>and</td><td>(we)* watched television. 我們留在家裏看電視。</td></tr><tr><td>My sister is married</td><td>and</td><td>(she)* lives in London. 我的妹妹已婚，居住在倫敦。</td></tr><tr><td>He doesn&#x27;t like her,</td><td>and</td><td>she doesn&#x27;t like him. 他不喜歡她，她也不喜歡他。</td></tr><tr><td>I bought a newspaper,</td><td>but</td><td>I didn&#x27;t read it. 我買了一份報紙，但沒有看。</td></tr><tr><td>It&#x27;s a nice house,</td><td>but</td><td>it hasn&#x27;t got a garden. 這是一間很好的房子，但沒有花園。</td></tr><tr><td>Do you want to go out,</td><td>or</td><td>are you too tired? 你想外出還是覺得太疲倦？</td></tr></table>

在列事物時，我們用逗號()。在最後一個列的事物之前用and。

I got home, had something to eat, sat down in an armchair and fell asleep. 我回家後，吃了點東西，坐在扶手椅上，然後睡着了。

Karen is at work, Sue has gone shopping and Chris is playing football.

十倫在工作，蘇去了購物，而克里斯则在踢足球。

# so（某事情的結果）

句子A 句子B

It was very hot, so I opened the window. 天氣很熱，所以我開窗。Joe does a lot of sport, so he's very fit. 希做很多運動，所以他很健壯。They don't like travelling, so they haven't been to many places. 他們不喜歡旅遊，所以沒有去過很多地方。

# because（某事情的原因）

<table><tr><td>句子A</td><td>句子B</td></tr><tr><td>I opened the window</td><td>because it was very hot. 我開窗是因為天氣很熱。</td></tr><tr><td>Joe can&#x27;t come to the party</td><td>because he&#x27;s going away. 不能來參加派對，因為他要離開。</td></tr><tr><td>Lisa is hungry</td><td>because she didn&#x27;t have breakfast. 莉莎覺得餓是因為她沒有吃早餐。</td></tr></table>

because也可用於句子的開頭：

Because it was very hot, I opened the window. 因為天氣很熱，我打開窗。

# 以下例子中有多於一個連接词出現：

It was late and I was tired, so I went to bed. 已很晚了，加上我覺得疲倦，所以我去睡覺了。I always enjoy visiting London, but I wouldn't like to live there because it's too big. 我經常樂於到倫敦遊玩，但卻不想住在那裏，因為它太大了。

# 睿

97.1 從以下的框內選擇適當的句子,然後用and / but / or 把兩句句子連接起來。

I stayed at home. I bought a newspaper. I went to the window. I wanted to phone you. I jumped into the river. I usually drive to work. Do you want me to come with you?

I didn't have your number. Shall I wait here? I didn't read it. I went by bus this morning. I watched television. I swam to the other side. I looked out.

1 I stayed at home and watched television. 2 I bought a newspaper, but I didn't read it. 3 I 4 5 6 7

97.2 依照下圖所示,用and / but / so / because完成句子。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0ba300cc5b8b6ab2a0ef60b82402c8b7a15b06e98d01b0bda60b70ec7f419aab.jpg)

1 It was very hot, so he opened the window. 2 They couldn't play tennis. 3 They went to the museum, 4 Bill wasn't hungry, 5 Helen was late 6 Sue said

# 97.3 用and / but等等寫一些你在昨天所做的事情。

1 (and) In the evening I stayed at home and studied. 2 (because) I went to bed very early because I was tired. 3 (but) 4 (and) 5 (so) 6 (because)

when可用於句子的開頭或中間： When I went out, it was raining.

這句子有兩部分：

部分A 部分B whenIwent out + it was raining

可以用部分A或部分B作為句子的開頭：

When I went out, it was raining. It was raining when I went out. 我外出時正在下雨。

如部分A（When...）在部分B之前，應該加上逗號（，

When you're tired, don't drive. Don't drive when you're tired. 當你感到疲倦時，切勿駕駛。

Helen was 25 when she got married. When Helen got married, she was 25. 海倫25時結婚。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1331b178fb50e1798c64469887320bfa12338fdb55c802701408b57bb99cacea.jpg)

如句子中有before／while／after，也是用相同的方式處理：

Always look both ways before you cross the road. 過馬路前應先向雨邊看。 Before you cross the road, always look both ways.

While I was waiting for the bus, it began to rain. 我在等候巴士時開始下雨。 It began to rain while I was waiting for the bus.

He never played football again after he broke his leg. 他自跌斷腳後便沒有再踢足球了。 After he broke his leg, he never played football again.

WhenIam.../WhenIgo...等等

薩拉（Sarah）打算下星期會去紐約。她有一個叫莉莎（Lisa）的朋友住在紐約，但莉莎也會離開到墨西哥去，所以她們不會在紐約會面。

Lisa will be in Mexico when Sarah is in New York.

當薩拉在紐約時，莉莎將會在墨西哥。

雖然時間是將來的（下星期），但我們用：

...when Sarah is in New York.

（不是when Sarah will be）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/efe807ae5841559cfba9e08d73bc5243a247f5e3ce7ecd9b3f02c544234e9226.jpg)

在when之後用現在式（例如Iam／Igo等等），以表達將來的意思：

When I get home this evening, I'm going to have a shower. （不是WhenIwillgethome）今晚回家後，我會洗澡。 I can't talk to you now. I'll talk to you later when I have more time. 我現在不能跟你。選一點我有時間時，將會跟你。

在before／while／after／until之後也是用相同的方式處理：

Please close the window before you go out.（不是before you will go）你外出前請把窗子關上。

Rachel is going to stay in our flat while we are away.（不是while we will be）當我們不在時，雷切爾將會住在我們的住宅單位裏。

I'll wait here until you come back.（不是until you will come back）我會留在這裏直至你回來。

# 98.1 從以下的框內選擇適當的詞句,並以 when 開頭造句。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/78168ef96c5b502ce7e79f34b57dba9b874d03e03617635aa8792ce1b7c57e5d.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/957a1db96481cf20b3ad41978af6bb1248c38d8348daae52a9e94413ddd504d2.jpg)

# 98.2 從以下的框內選擇適當的詞句完成句子。

somebody broke into the house before they came here when they heard the news before they crossed the road while they were away they didn't believe me they went to live in New Zealand

1 They looked both ways before they crossed the road  2 They were very surprised  3 After they got married,  4 Their house was damaged in a storm  5 Where did they live  6 While we were asleep,  7 When I told them what happened,

# 98.3 選擇正確的答案。

1 I stay / I'll stay here until you come / you'll come back. (I'll stay 和 you come 是正確答案)  2 I'm going to bed when I finish / I'll finish my work.  3 We must do something before it's / it will be too late.  4 Helen is going away soon. I'm / I'll be very sad when she leaves / she'll leave.  5 Don't go out yet. Wait until the rain stops / will stop.  6 We come / We'll come and visit you when we're / we'll be in England again.  7 When I come / I'll come to see you tomorrow, I bring / I'll bring our holiday photos.  8 I'm going to Paris next week. I hope to see some friends of mine while I'm / I'll be there.  9 'I need your address.' 'OK, I give / I'll give it to you before I go / I'll go.'  10 I'm not ready yet. I tell / I'll tell you when I'm / I'll be ready.

# 98.4 試用你自己的構思完成下列句子。

1 Can you close the window before when go out  2 What are you going to do when  3 When I have enough money,  4 I'll wait for you while  5 When I start my new job,  6 Will you be here when

# If we go ... If you see ... 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/b7073c2f9eb328516bc8fd2704167b82019fca59e83f6536053f4974623cc2e4.jpg)

# if可用於句子的關頭或中间：

# if用於關頭

If we go by bus, it will be cheaper. 如果我們乘巴士，會便宜一點。If you don't hurry, you'll miss the train. 如果你不趕快的話，你會錯過那班火車。If you're hungry, have something to eat. 如果你肚子餓，吃一點東西。If the phone rings, can you answer it, please? 如果電話響起，麻煩你可否接聽？

# if用於中間

It will be cheaper if we go by bus. 如果我們乘巴士，會便宜一點。You'll miss the train if you don't hurry. 如果你不趕快的話，你會趕不上那班火車。I'm going to the concert if I can get a ticket. 如果我有一張門票的話，我打算去聽那音樂會。Is it OK if I use your phone? 如果我用你的電話，可以嗎？

在會話中，句子中的if...那部分通常會單獨使用：「Are you going to the concert?' 'Yes, if I can get a ticket.' 「你打算去聽音樂會嗎？」「是的，如果我有門票的話。」

# If you see Ann tomorrow...等等：

if之後用現在式（不是will）（例如if you see...等等，不是if you will see）：

If you see Ann tomorrow, can you ask her to call me? 如果你明天見到安，你可否請她致電給我？If I'm late this evening, don't wait for me.（不是if I will be）如果我今晚遲到，便不用等我了。What shall we do if it rains?（不是if it will rain）如果下雨我們怎麼辦？If I don't feel well tomorrow, I'll stay at home. 如果我明天不適，我會留在家裏。

# if和when

IfIgoout  $=$  我可能會外出，但我不肯定：

A:Areyougoingoutlater？你打算待會兒外出？ B:Maybe.IfIgoout,I'llclosethewindows.也許吧。如果我外出，我會把窗子關上。

WhenIgoout  $=$  我打算外出（肯定的）：

A:Areyougoingoutlater？你打算待會兒外出？ B:Yes,Iam.WhenIgoout,I'llclosethewindows.是的，我外出時會把窗子關上。

# 試比較

when和if的用法及其分别：

When I get home this evening, I'm going to have a shower. 今晚回家後，我會洗澡。If I'm late this evening, don't wait for me.（不是When I'm late）如果今晚我遲到，便不用等我了。We're going to play tennis if it doesn't rain.（不是when it doesn't rain）如果沒有下雨，我們會打網球。

you don't hurry you pass the exam you fail the exam you don't want this magazine If + you want those pictures you're busy now you're hungry you need money

we can have lunch now you can have them I can lend you some you'll get a certificate you'll be late I'll throw it away we can talk later you can do it again

If you don't hurry, you'll be late.

2 If you pass

3 If

5

6

7

8

# If I had ... If we went ... 等等

丹（Dan）喜歡跑車，可是他沒有足夠的金錢買。If he had the money, he would buy a fast car.如果他有錢的話，他會買一輛跑得很快的車。

had通常是過去式，但在這句子中had並不是過去式。If he had the money = if he had the money now（但事實上他現在沒有錢）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/581eb34b5ed3bf1c26bdfc3edd525c535da2a349fe82e4fbb133c88740d593f9.jpg)

<table><tr><td>If</td><td>I
you
it
they
等等</td><td>had / knew / lived / went (等等) ... ,
didn&#x27;t have / didn&#x27;t know (等等) ... ,
were ... ,
could ... ,</td><td>I
you
it
they
等等</td><td>would(n&#x27;t)</td><td>buy ... be ... have ... go ... 等等</td></tr></table>

可以說：

If he had the money, he would buy a car.

He would buy a car if he had the money.如果他有錢的話，他會買一輛汽車。

I'd / she'd / they'd 等等 = I would / she would / they would 等等：

I don't know the answer. If I knew the answer, I'd tell you.

我不知道答案。如果我知道答案的話，我會告訴你的。

It's raining, so we're not going out. We'd get wet if we went out.

下雨了，所以我們不打算外出。如果我們外出的話，我們會弄濕的。

Jane lives in a city. She likes cities. She wouldn't be happy if she lived in the country.

珍住在城市裏。她喜歡城市。如果她住在郊外的話，她不會感到快樂的。

If you didn't have a job, what would you do? 如果你失業，你會怎麼辦？（但事實上你是有工作的）

I'm sorry I can't help you. I'd help you if I could.對不起，我幫不了你。如果我能夠的話，我會幫你的。（但事實上我不能幫忙）

If we had a car, we could travel more. 如果我們有一輛汽車，我們可更常外遊。（但事實上我們沒有汽車，所以不能經常外遊）

# B

If (I) was / were ...

可以用‘if I / he / she / it was’或‘if I/he / she / it were’：

It's not a very nice place. I wouldn't go there if I were you.（或...if I was you）那不是一個很好的地方，如果我是你，我不會到那裏去。

It would be nice if the weather was better.（或...if the weather were better）如果天氣更佳便好了。

What would Tom do if he were here?（或...if he was here）如果湯姆在這裏，他會做甚麼呢？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3c870f0e3ce8397c7d8f31c4cc2b89891ab5fcddbd4d85d23582f7a365dd56cd.jpg)

# 試比較

If I have / if it is 等等

I must go and see Helen.

If I have time, I will go today.

我一定要去看海倫。如果我有時間，我會今天去。（=我可能會有時間，所以可能會去）

I like that jacket.

I'll buy it if it isn't too expensive.

我喜歡那件夾克。如果它不是太昂貴的話，我會買下的。（=可能它不是太昂貴）

I'll help you if I can. 如果可以的話，我會幫你的。（=可能我可以幫忙）

if I had / if it was 等等

I must go and see Helen.

If I had time, I would go today.

我一定要去看海倫。如果我有時間，我會今天去。（=我今天沒有時間，所以我不會去）

I like that jacket, but it's very expensive.

I'd buy it if it wasn't so expensive.

我喜歡那件夾克，但它十分昂貴。如果它不是這麼昂貴的話，我會買下的。

（=因為它十分昂貴，所以我不會買）

I'd help you if I could, but I can't.

如果可以的話，我會幫你的，可是我不能。

If I to go out, I'd go.  4 We haven't got a key. If we a key, we could get into the house.  5 I'm not hungry. I would have something to eat if I hungry.  6 Sue enjoys her work. She wouldn't do it if she it.  7 He can't speak any foreign languages. If he speak a foreign language, perhaps he would get a better job.  8 You don't try hard enough. If you harder, you would have more success.  9 I have a lot to do today. If I so much to do, we could go out.

(she / not / be)  3 If I wanted to learn Italian, to Italy. (I / go)  4 I haven't told Helen what happened. She'd be angry if (she / know)  5 If a map, I could show you where I live. (we / have)  6 What would you do if a lot of money? (you / win)  7 It's not a very good hotel. there if I were you. (I / not / stay)  8 If nearer London, we would go there more often. (we / live)  9 It's a pity you have to go now. nice if you had more time. (it / be)  10 I'm not going to take the job. I'd take it if better. (the salary / be)  11 I don't know anything about cars. If the car broke down, what to do. (I / not / know)  12 If you could change one thing in the world, what ? (you / change)

# a person who ... a thing that / which ... 關係從句1（relative clauses 1）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/61682ef3c8c06e65b49321b470fcb88c5cb79f0027701a0e70f3d50e54525df9.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5a252d6884bd732ac931451e66d6a78b09fcdc176c9e6aef796b3814e66d01ce.jpg)

# who 用於指人物（不是事物）：

<table><tr><td>A thief is a person</td><td>who steals things.</td><td>賊人是指偷東西的人。</td></tr><tr><td>Do you know anybody</td><td>who can play the piano?</td><td>你認識懂得彈鋼琴的人嗎？</td></tr><tr><td>The man</td><td>who phoned</td><td>didn&#x27;t give his name. 那個致電來的男子沒有留下姓名。</td></tr><tr><td>The people</td><td>who work in the office</td><td>are very friendly. 那些在辦公室工作的人十分友善。</td></tr></table>

# that 用於指事物或人物：

<table><tr><td>An aeroplane is a machine</td><td>that flies.</td><td>飛機是一架會飛行的機器。</td></tr><tr><td>Emma lives in a house</td><td>that is 400 years old.</td><td>埃瑪住在一間有 400 年歷史的房子裏。</td></tr><tr><td>The people</td><td>that work in the office</td><td>are very friendly. 那些在辦公室工作的人十分友善。</td></tr></table>

that 可用於指人，但 who 比較常用。

# which 用於指事物（不是人物）：

<table><tr><td>An aeroplane is a machine</td><td>which flies. (不是 a machine who ...)</td><td>飛機是一架會飛行的機器。</td></tr><tr><td>Emma lives in a house</td><td>which is 500 years old. 埃瑪住在一間有 500 年歷史的房子裏。</td><td></td></tr></table>

# 注意

別將 which 用於指人物：

Do you remember the woman who was playing the piano at the party? （不是 the woman which ...）你記得那個在派對上彈鋼琴的女士嗎？

101.1 從以下的框內選擇適當的詞句,以 A ... is a person who ... 的句式造句。有需要時可查閱詞典。

a thief a dentist a butcher a fool a musician a genius a patient a liar

doesn't tell the truth is ill in hospital takes care of your teeth steals- things is very intelligent does stupid things plays a musical instrument sells meat

1 A thief is a person who steals things. 2 A butcher is a person 3 A musician 4 5 6 7 8

1 (A man phoned. He didn't give his name.) The man who phoned didn't give his name. 2 (A woman opened the door. She was wearing a yellow dress.) The woman a yellow dress. 3 (Some students took the exam. Most of them passed.) Most of the students 4 (A policeman stopped our car. He wasn't very friendly.) The

# 101.3 在接線上填上 who 或 which。

1 I met a woman who can speak six languages. 2 What's the name of the man has just started work in your office? 3 What's the name of the river flows through the town? 4 Where is the picture was hanging on the wall? 5 Do you know anybody wants to buy a car? 6 You always ask questions are difficult to answer. 7 I have a friend is very good at repairing cars. 8 I think everybody went to the party enjoyed it very much. 9 Why does he always wear clothes are too small for him?

OK 3 A coffee maker is a machine who makes coffee. 4 Have you seen the money that was on the table? 5 I don't like people which never stop talking. 6 I know somebody that can help you. 7 I know somebody who works in that shop. 8 Correct the sentences who are wrong. 9 My neighbour bought a car who cost £40,000.

# the people we met the hotel you stayed at 102 關係句2（relative clauses 2）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d600c8ce95454f74d4b8c70cb175d35ed29f44545a272e0e0ac412b5cd1efd8e.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6a50cc6e97cb461ce44cd04cc9fdaa16a9fb2a938b6309d2ca4f8034748c3024.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/812ac424275fbd72cb2241172b8a2d04d086d5f9182e56bbb6391b6ce75256ac.jpg)

可以説：

The bag that he is carrying ... 或 The bag he is carrying ... (是否加上 that 也可以)  ... the money that Kate won? 或 ... the money Kate won?

當所指的是賓語時，不用that／who／which也可以：

主語 動語

賓語  

<table><tr><td>The man</td><td>was carrying</td><td>a bag</td><td>→ the bag (that) the man was carrying</td></tr><tr><td>Kate</td><td>won</td><td>some money</td><td>→ the money (that) Kate won</td></tr><tr><td>You</td><td>wanted</td><td>some books</td><td>→ the books (that) you wanted</td></tr><tr><td>We</td><td>met</td><td>some people</td><td>→ the people (who) we met</td></tr></table>

Did you find the books you wanted?（或...the books that you wanted?）你找到你想要的書？ The people we met were very friendly.（或The people who we met...）我們遇到的人很友善。 Everything I said was true.（或Everything that I said...）我説的每件事都是真的。

# 注意

The film we saw was very good.（不是The film we saw it was...）我們看的那電影十分好看。

# 有時動词後须加上介词（例如to／in／at等等）：

Eve is talking to a man. Do you know the man Eve is talking to? 伊娃正在跟一名男子話。 → 你識那個正在跟伊娃話的男子？ We stayed at a hotel. The hotel we stayed at was near the station. 我們住過一間酒店。 → 我們住過的那間酒店在車站附近。 I told you about some books. These are the books I told you about. 我向你及一些書籍。 → 這些就是我向你及的書籍。

# 注意

...the books I told you about.（不是the books I told you about them）可以説（a place）where...： The hotel where we stayed was near the station.（=The hotel we stayed at...）我們住過的那間酒店在車站附近。

當所指的是主語時，必须用who／that／which： 檢索 →單元101

I met a woman who can speak six languages.（who是主語）我遇見一位懂得六種語言的女士。 Jack was wearing a hat that was too big for him.（that是主語）傑克戴了一頂大大的帽子。

a person who...和a thing that／which...（關係徙句1）=單元101

2 (You cooked a meal. It was excellent.) The

# at 8 o'clock on Monday in April

at

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/80e0c3b15996bf3a1936c50d3ca654efaa69ff2e0588d0f037931b31ac4bf8da.jpg)

8 o'clock at 10:30 midnight 等等

I start work at 8 o'clock. 我在八時開始工作。 The shops close at 5:30. 商店於五時半關門。

on

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6dcaa5fe03b8e3e341b5111afb67faa095dd8fc9124de144383dbda1b0494e58.jpg)

Sunday(s) / Monday(s) 等等 25 April / 6 June 等等 New Year's Day 等等

Bye! I'll see you on Friday. 再見！星期五再見你。 What do you usually do on Sundays? 星期日你通常做甚麼？ The concert is on 22 November. 那場音樂會於十一月二十二日舉行。

in

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9e3853f028b597d9ef245cc6ea5f04907cd80c6761b310ba4f045983681c8a5c.jpg)

April / June 等等 2003 / 1968 等等 summer / spring 等等

I'm going on holiday in October. 我打算十月去度假。 Emma was born in 1983. 埃瑪出生於1983年。 The park is beautiful in spring. 那個公園在春天時是很美麗的。

# B at用於以下情况：

at the weekend at night at Christmas / at Easter at the end of ... at the moment

Are you going away at the weekend? 週末時你會離開嗎？ I can't sleep at night. 我晚上不能入睡。 Where will you be at Christmas? (或 on Christmas Day) 里誕節的時候你會在哪裏？ I'm going on holiday at the end of October. 我打算十月底去度假。 Are you busy at the moment? 這會兒你忙嗎？

in the morning / in the afternoon / in the evening

I always feel good in the morning. 我總是在早上時感覺很好。 Do you often go out in the evening? 你經常在傍晚時外出嗎？

但

on Monday morning / on Tuesday afternoon / on Friday evening / on Saturday night 等等：

I'm meeting Joanne on Monday morning. 我星期一早上將會見到喬安妮。

Are you doing anything on Saturday night? 星期六傍晚你有甚麼事情做嗎？

# D 在下列詞語之前不用 at / on / in：

this ... (this morning / this week 等等)  last ... (last August / last week 等等)  next ... (next Monday / next week 等等)  every ... (every day / every week 等等)

Are you going out this evening? 你今晚會外出嗎？

We go on holiday every summer. Last summer we went to Canada. 我們每年夏天都去度假。上個夏天我們去了加拿大。

I'm leaving next Monday.

(不是 on next Monday) 下星期一我將會離開。

in five minutes / in a few days / in six weeks / in two years 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/352961add65c71578fbac13ff706d18e7c08124dee5eae714ad64936b01a43d4.jpg)  
now

Hurry! The train leaves in five minutes.

(= it leaves five minutes from now)

趕快一點！火車會於五分鐘後開出。

Bye! I'll see you in a few days.

(= a few days from now)

再見！我數天後見你。

# from ... to until since for

A from ... to ...

We lived in Japan from 1992 to 2001.

由1992年至2001年我們住在日本。

I work from Monday to Friday. 我由星期一至星期五工作。

也可用from...until...

We lived in Japan from 1992 until 2001.

由1992年至2001年我們住在日本。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e76ab386ac22f3b2f02f8399312f0dd2f7b3b355aacb96e03167c72714162934.jpg)

# until  $^+$  某一段期間的结尾

<table><tr><td>until</td><td>Friday
December
3 o&#x27;clock
I come back</td></tr></table>

They're going away tomorrow.

They'll be away until Friday.

他們將會在明天離開,直至星期五才回來。

I went to bed early, but I wasn't tired. I read a book until 3 o'clock.

我很早便上琳睡覺,但我不覺得疲倦,於是看書至三時。

Wait here until I come back. 在這裹等候直至我回來。

也可用till  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  until):

Wait here till I come back. 在這裹等候直至我回來。

試比較

until和on的用法及分别:

How long will you be away?'Until Monday'「你將會離開多久？」「直至星期一。」 When are you coming back?'On Monday'「你將會何時回來？」「星期一。」

# since  $^+$  過去的某一時間(直至現在)

since用於現在完成式(例如havebeen/havedone等等)之後:

<table><tr><td>since</td><td>Monday
1998
2.30
I arrived</td></tr></table>

Joe is in hospital. He has been in hospital since Monday.  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  from Monday to now)

喬在醫院。他自星期一起已經在醫院了。Monday

Sue and Dave have been married since 1968.

$\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  from 1968 to now)

蘇和戴夫自1968年起已經結婚。

It has been raining since I arrived. 自我到達後一直下着雨。

試比較

from...to...和until的用法及分别:

We lived in Japan from 1992 to 2001. 由1992年至2001年我們住在日本。

We lived in Japan until 2001. 至2001為止年我們都住在日本。

Now we live in Canada. We came to Canada in 2001.

現在我們住在加拿大,我們於2001年來到加拿大。

We have lived in Canada since 2001.  $\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  from 2001 until now)我們自2001年起一直都住在加拿大。

for(不是since)  $^+$  一段時間(例如three days／ten years等等)：

Joe has been in hospital for three days. (不是since three days) 喬已經在醫院三天了。

# for  $^+$  一段時間

<table><tr><td>for</td><td>three days
ten years
five minutes
a long time</td></tr></table>

Gary stayed with us for three days. 加里跟我們一起三天。

I'm going away for a few weeks.

我將會離開數星期。

I'm going away for the weekend.

週末期間我將會離開。

They've been married for ten years.

他們結婚已十年了。

# 单元 105 before after during while

before = 之前  $\Rightarrow$  during = 期間和 after = 之後

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/369f28814be0df68503a73a7bfc42fe8eb16d348c2994a418c22f64b985a3670.jpg)

- Everybody feels nervous before exams. 每個人在考試前都感到緊張。- I fell asleep during the film. 我在影片放映時睡着了。- We were tired after our visit to the museum. 我們參觀過博物館後感到疲倦。

before = 之前  $\Rightarrow$  while = 寄…的時候和 after = 之後

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3d1f4243c4b3619d10cca72b804c988537e60f49c80c1d11e2025e5ed646fd8b.jpg)  
before we played

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9e14897f1fe7f4362a5fd0e0d766e0742a844fdd8eae6aa79af49bffca3faffe.jpg)  
while we were playing

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ea85c0ee1582093a8195f6148626dd70e49c9fd8176a7b203e9327e7bf137722.jpg)  
after we played

- Don't forget to close the window before you go out. 你外出前別忘記關窗。- I often fall asleep while I'm reading. 我經常在閱讀時睡着。- They went home after they did the shopping. 他們購物後回家去了。

during, while 和 for

during + 名詞 (during the film); while + 動詞 (while I'm reading):

- We didn't speak during the meal. 我們在吃飯時不說話。但 We didn't speak while we were eating. (不是 during we were eating) 我們在吃飯時不說話。

for (不是 during) + 一段時間 (例如 three days / two hours / a year 等等):

- We played tennis for two hours. (不是 during two hours) 我們打了兩小時錦球。- I lived in London for a year. (不是 during a year) 我在倫敦居住了一年。

before / after + - ing (例如 before going / after eating 等等):

- I always have breakfast before going to work. (= before I go to work) 我總是在上班前吃早餐。- After doing the shopping, they went home. (= after they did) 購物完畢後，他們回家去了。

# 注意

可以用 before going (不是 before to go)、after doing (不是 after to do) 等等:

- Before eating the apple, I washed it carefully. (不是 before to eat) 吃蘋果之前，我小心地把它洗淨。- I started work after reading the newspaper. (不是 after to read) 閥報後我開始工作。

# in at on 107 地點2（places2）

# in

in bed in hospital

in the sky

in the world in a newspaper / in a book

in a photograph / in a picture in a car / in a taxi in the middle (of ...)

'Where's Kate?' 'She's in bed.' 「凯特在哪裹？」「她在睡覺。」

David's father is ill. He's in hospital.

大衛的爸爸病倒了，他在醫院裹。

I like to look at the stars in the sky at night.

我喜歡在晚上看天上的星星。

What's the largest city in the world? 世界上最大的城市是哪一個？

I read about the accident in the newspaper.

我在報紙上閱讀過有關那宗意外的新聞。

You look sad in this photograph. 照片中的你看來很傷感。

Did you come here in your car? 你是否乘自己的汽車來？

There's a big tree in the middle of the garden.

花園的中央有一棵大樹。

# B

# at

at home at work / at school

at university / at college

at the station / at the airport

at Jane's (house) / at my sister's (house) / at the doctor's / at the hairdresser's 等等

at a concert / at a party / at a football match 等等

Will you be at home this evening? 今晚你會在家嗎？

'Where's Kate?' 'She's at work.'

「凱特在哪裹？」「她在工作。」

Helen is studying law at university. 海倫在大學裹修讀法律。

I'll meet you at the station, OK? 我會在車站接你，好嗎？

A: Where were you yesterday? 昨天你在哪裹？

B: At my sister's. 我在妹妹的家裹。

I saw Tom at the doctor's. 我在診所看見湯姆。

I say weren't many people at the party.

There weren't many people at the party.

在底對裹的人不多。

在一般的情况下可用in或at來表示建築物（例如hotels、restaurants等等）：

We stayed at a nice hotel. 或 We stayed in a nice hotel.

我們會住在一間舒適的酒店裹。

# 单元 to in at 108 地點3（places3）

to

go / come / return / walk（等等）to...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1641ec3029a404fb7d2148ed5b4ee574a2d77cc4d1784896628e67d5e7c82e53.jpg)

We're going to London on Sunday. 我們下星期會去倫敦。

I want to go to Italy next year. 我想明年去意大利。

We walked from my house to the centre of town. 我們從自己的家步行到市鎮中心。

What time do you go to bed? 你何時睡覺？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/61bc6cad3b4e03998f00879861d698aa69e5f313e1fda450a211fcc5313dc3be.jpg)

The bus is going to the airport.

那辆巴士現正驶往機場。

Karen didn't go to work yesterday.

卡倫昨天沒有上班。

I went to a party last night.

昨晚我參加了一個派對。

You must come to our house.

你一定要到我們家來。

in / at : 檢索 → 畢元 106- 107be / stay / do something（等等）in...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ab37f44ae62cfdf78915554dc2f382bdb63a03eb861973e3256f07e4a5151e7a.jpg)

Piccadilly Circus is in London.

皮卡迪利廣場位於倫敦。

My brother lives in Italy. 我的哥哥住在意大利。

The main shops are in the centre of town. 主要的商店都在市镇中心里。

I like reading in bed. 我喜歡在林上閱讀。

be / stay / do something（等等）at...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9d3fe846387211ed05cb553f5a92e35fb2674d76c4b34bc36d7c8440341c2a98.jpg)

The bus is at the airport.

那辆巴士在機場裹。

Sarah wasn't at work yesterday.

薩拉昨天没有上班。

I met a lot of people at the party. 我在派對中遇見很多人。

Helen stayed at her brother's house.

海倫暫住在她哥哥的家裏。

# B

go / come / walk（等等）home（不用 to）:

I'm tired. I'm going home.（不是 to home）我感到疲倦，我要回家了。

Did you walk home?

你是否步行回家？

be / stay / do something（等等）at home:

I'm staying at home tonight.

今晚我會留在家裏。

Dan doesn't go to an office. He works at

home. 丹不是到辦公室上班。他在家裏工作。

C arrive 和 get

arrive in + 某國家或某城鎮（例如 arrive in Italy / arrive in Paris 等等）：

They arrived in England last week.（不是 arrived to England）他們上星期到達英格蘭。

arrive at + 其他地方（例如 arrive at the station / arrive at work 等等）：

What time did you arrive at the hotel?（不是 arrive to the hotel）你何時到達酒店？

get to（某一地方）：

What time did you get to the hotel? 你何時到達酒店？

What time did you get to Paris? 你何時到達巴黎？

get home / arrive home（不用介詞）：

I was tired when I got home. 或 I was tired when I arrived home. 我抵家後感到疲倦。

检索

been to → 舉元 17 get (to ...) → 舉元 56 in / at → 舉元 106- 107

# 练習

# 108.1在横線上填上to/in°

1 I like reading in bed. 2 We're going Italy next month. 3 Sue is on holiday Italy at the moment. 4 I have to go the bank today.

5 I was tired, so I stayed bed late. 6 What time do you usually go bed? 7 Does this bus go the centre? 8 Would you like to live another country?

# 108.2適當時填上to/at°留意有些句子亚不需要介词。

1 Paula didn't go to work yesterday.

2 I'm tired. I'm going home. (不需要介词)

3 Tina is not very well. She has gone the doctor.

4 Would you like to come a party on Saturday?

5 Is Liz home?' No, she's gone work.

6 There were 20,000 people the football match.

7 Why did you go home early last night?

8 A boy jumped into the river and swam the other side.

9 There were a lot of people waiting the bus stop.

10 We had a good meal a restaurant, and then we went back the hotel.

# 108.3適當時填上to/in/at°留意有些句子亚不需要介词

1 I'm not going out this afternoon. I'm staying at home.

2 We're going a concert tomorrow evening.

3 I went New York last year.

4 How long did you stay New York?

5 Next year we hope to go Canada to visit some friends.

6 Do you want to go the cinema this evening?

7 Did you park your car the station?

8 After the accident three people were taken hospital.

9 How often do you go the dentist?

10 Is Sarah here?' No, she's Helen's.

11 My house is the end of the street on the left.

12 I went Maria's house, but she wasn't home.

13 There were no taxis, so we had to walk home.

14 Who did you meet the party?' I didn't go the party.

# 108.4適當時填上to/in/at°留意有些句子亚不需要介词。

1 What time do you usually get work? 4 When did you arrive London?

2 What time do you usually get home? 5 What time does the train get Paris?

3 What time did you arrive the party? 6 We arrived home very late.

2 Yesterday I went

3 At 11 o'clock yesterday morning I was

4 One day I'd like to go

5 I don't like going

6 At 9 o'clock yesterday evening I was

# under, behind, opposite 等等

next to / beside / between / in front of / behind

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/31279d54411030c70e1c0f93b91595f6e2fbf475f340376434e9fe7ac692c869.jpg)

A is next to B. 或 A is beside B. B is between A and C. D is in front of B. E is behind B.

此外,

A is on the left. C is on the right. B is in the middle (of the group).

A在左。C在右。B在（人）中央。

opposite / in front of

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e08759683bf03564b968ef6dfb7aeb48175c6f812abbfa8475331ae5acf9dfba.jpg)

A is sitting in front of B. A坐在B前面。 A is sitting opposite C. A坐在C封面。 C is sitting opposite A. C坐在A封面。

by  $\mathbf{\Psi} =$  next to / beside)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/bd5733146236222f343e016695716caaaeeafdf2c2e551bd24e0d13f41a55d96.jpg)

by the window

Our house is by the sea. (= beside the sea) 我們的房子在海邊。 Who is that man standing by the window? 那個站在窗旁的男子是誰？ If you feel cold, why don't you sit by the fire? 如果你感到寒冷，為甚麼不坐在爐火旁邊？

under

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6072aee225abf5f7e98ecf94980f23ae85416d3d322b0c413e58ae454cb32049.jpg)

The cat is under the table. 那隻貓在桌下。 The girl is standing under a tree. 那個女孩站在樹下。 I'm wearing a jacket under my coat. 我在大衣下穿着一件夾克。

above 和 below

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2f15db72812c1fc638c6a0cad54ea2b01aad0db8c9baa0629fa7fbefa992a65c.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2b0cfba9be5d2c3c66949b1d9bce882e63f4d4b510c0740e8f91949b6bd4090d.jpg)

The pictures are above the shelves. 那些畫在書架之上。 The shelves are below the pictures. 那書架在那些畫之下。

# up, over, through 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d8a8117be7e2cd3992b7dd03e471020d55e1971576aa70f5bdc21e4e02883d69.jpg)

Jane is going to France next week. 珍下星期會去法國。  - We walked from the hotel to the station. 我們由酒店步行至車站。  - A lot of English words come from Latin. 很多英文語都是來自拉丁文。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2b7dfb60a384328e34fc71284d2de39e82235ffaf676a3260d5e14a59c0f2c59.jpg)

We jumped into the water. 我們跳進水裹。  - A man came out of the house and got into a car. 一名男子從屋內走出來，然後上了車。  - Why are you looking out of the window? 你為何望出窗外？  - I took the old batteries out of the radio. 我把收音機內的舊電池拿出來。

put something in... (通常不用 into):  - I put new batteries in the radio. 我把新電池放進收音機內。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8bbfab5823d528e6b709d19c262103c60c9623e4e6ed2d721d90ca785b1c1aa3.jpg)

Don't put your feet on the table. 別把腳放在桌上。  - Please take your feet off the table. 請把你的腳從桌上放下來。  - I'm going to hang some pictures on the wall. 我會把一些畫掛在牆上。  - Be careful! Don't fall off your bicycle. 小心！別從自行車上跌下來。  - We got on the bus in Princes Street. 我們在王子街上巴士。

We walked up the hill to the house. 我們步行上山，往那間房子去。  - Be careful! Don't fall down the stairs. 小心！別從梯級跌下來。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/43de901673701148de4c75cc448b580d8973cd3bdbbcf0acc36fe721f65b93e2.jpg)

The plane flew over the mountains. 那架飛機飛過山脈。  - I jumped over the wall into the garden. 我跳過那牆壁進入花園。  - Some people say it is unlucky to walk under a ladder. 有人說在梯子下走過會帶來惡運。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8ccf09d85b05e95b37cb208b1846c4ccb2049f2579b7d25220efebf7cfe2ad68.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/42a5a1061c864a4907b58adc7080f9957ff195c037dc36092a8038ab9881632f.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4454abedad56af0cf2a05c3c3789e0bcc68db2575bc01e8542ae1f30186f034d.jpg)

through

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4e42aa63e6cfc3d5f62c79062349e2de6101d8a78bee6755775a307d1af36a97.jpg)

A bird flew into the room through a window. 一隻小鸟通過窗子飛進房間。  - The old road goes through the village. 那條破舊的道路穿越那鄉村。  - The new road goes round the village. 那條新道路繞過那鄉村。  - The bus stop is just round the corner. 巴士站在附近。  - I walked round the town and took some photographs. 我在市鎮內四處逛，拍了一些照片。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c4dbe5f11dce5c98ae9b8669b13a8f048756ad9bd6166c39f971ce612e5da487.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5c6d3e5ead048540c71dc6e4e49bd6c74b8d5353f8d9018b2154dee6792e3e07.jpg)

也可用around  $\mathbf{\Psi} =$  round)

We walked around the town. 我們在市鎮內四處逛。

round the town

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d4aa9de53b110e74fa07e0fa692ca7d6f7593de04fadf976823f13bdaa777e19.jpg)

I was walking along the road with my dog. 我與狗兒沿路步行。

Let's go for a walk along the river. 我們沿着河流散步吧。  - The dog swam across the river. 那隻狗游了過河。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/1eca68c9f15ddbba3b70da8d3c313273b0f5070d00d0d771bf5bac09e5c0b544.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8f17fb7cb4ce9dc7ef87237a071f78e83662581d7feba2aee5d17f897bb1cc14.jpg)

They walked past me without speaking. 他們不發一言走過我身旁。

A: Excuse me, how do I get to the hospital?

對不起，請問怎樣到醫院去？

B: Go along this road, past the cinema, under the

bridge and the hospital is on the left. 沿這條路步行，經過電影院，在橋下走過一醫院就在你的左面。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/5df294b4f5b3e0a43d3953d478874dd80b24efe21a93742e832644ea01d72058.jpg)

# 110.3 在下列句子中填上介詞（例如over / from / into等等）。

'You put it your bag.' 4 How far is it here the airport? 5 We walked the museum for an hour and saw a lot of interesting things. 6 You can put your coat the back of the chair. 7 In tennis, you have to hit the ball the net. 8 Silvia took a key her bag and opened the door.

# 111 on at by with about

on holiday on television on the radio on the phone on fire on time (= not late)

Jane isn't at work this week. She's on holiday. 這個星期珍没有上班, 她正在放假。 We watched the news on television. 我們收看電視的新聞報道。 We listened to the news on the radio. 我們收聽電台的新聞報道。 I spoke to Rachel on the phone last night. 昨晚我跟雷切爾通電話。 The house is on fire! Call the fire brigade. 那房子起火了! 召喚消防隊。 'Was the train late?' 'No, it was on time.' 「那班火車是否遲到了？」！不, 它準時到。」

# B

at (the age of) 21 / at 50 kilometres an hour / at 100 degrees 等等:

Lisa got married at 21. (或... at the age of 21. ) 莉莎在21歲時結婚。

A car uses more petrol at 120 kilometres an hour than at 90.

汽車開行時速120公里比時速90公里消耗更多汽油。

Water boils at 100 degrees Celsius. 水在攝氏100度沸騰。

# C

by car / by bus / by plane / by bike 等等:

Do you like travelling by train? 你喜歡乘火車旅行嗎?

Jane usually goes to work by bike. 珍通常騎自行車上班。

# 注意

on foot:

You can't get there by car. You have to go on foot.

(= you have to walk) 你不能乘車去那裏。你必須步行去。

a book by ... / a painting by ... / a piece of music by ... 等等:

Have you read any books by Charles Dickens?

你有看過狄更斯的著作嗎?

Who is that painting by? Picasso?

那幅畫是誰畫的?畢卡索?

by 用於被動語態之後: 檢索 → 單元21

I was bitten by a dog. 我被一隻狗咬。

# with / without

Did you stay at a hotel or with friends? 你住在酒店還是跟朋友一起?

Wait for me. Please don't go without me.

等我一會,沒有我同行請不要走。

Do you like your coffee with or without milk? 你喜歡咖啡加牛奶嗎?

I cut the paper with a pair of scissors. 我用剪刀把紙張剪開。

a man with a beard / a woman with glasses 等等:

Do you know that man with the beard? 你認識那個蓄鬍的男子嗎?

I'd like to have a house with a big garden.

我希望擁有一間有大花園的房屋。

by bus

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/61b8769f5c2c325a7669226cd08e816c286365d123fb0a02d40fbc344d360b51.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/82498b595373ba5e869201d6fd2a6b80ffdb603467ed7a6e89bcc009c011288f.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/e86d283add98ccb0011073dce9a547c3acf9dac791e25b189ab4072bcc660f44.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/eb225de120ca78a031e9265c12ad298b9e762d338e0be87e35476270a2977a12.jpg)

a man a woman with a beard with glasses

# about

talk / speak / think / hear / know about ...

Some people talk about their work all the time. 有些人總是談論自己的工作。

I don't know much about cars. 我對汽車認識不多。

a book / a question / a programme / information (等等) about ...

There was a programme about volcanoes on TV last night. Did you see it?

昨晚有一個關於火山的電視節目。你有收看嗎?

搜索 by = 瞭元21, 63, 109 at / on = 瞭元103, 106- 107 介詞 + - ing = 瞭元112

# afraid of ..., good at ... 等等 of / at / for 等等 介词(prepositions) + -ing

afraid of ... / good at ... 等等（形容词 + 介词）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2244134c641665ccf9d832ad666c24988ea2eba0bcdc5523060dbdd37bb01009.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/2a17637b160206eb6c0bb9e47ef29dc87bc2fa307efa0977d750f951d60883d3.jpg)

afraid of ... angry with somebody

angry about something

different from ... 或 different to ...

fed up with ...

full of ...

good at ...

interested in ...

married to ...

nice / kind of somebody to ...

be nice / kind to somebody

sorry about a situation

sorry for / about doing

something

be / feel sorry for somebody

Are you afraid of dogs? 你怕狗嗎？

Why are you angry with me? What have I done?

你為何生我的氣？我做了甚麼？

Are you angry about last night? (= something that happened last night) 你為了昨晚的事發怒嗎？

Lisa is very different from (或 to) her sister.

莉莎跟她的妹妹很不同。

I'm fed up with my job. I want to do something different.

(= I've had enough of my job)

我對自己的工作感到厭倦，我想做一些不同的事。

The room was full of people. 房間裏擠滿了人。

Are you good at maths? 你精於數學嗎？

I'm not interested in sport. 我對運動不感興趣。

Sue is married to a dentist. (= her husband is a dentist)

蘇跟一名牙醫結了婚。

It was kind of you to help us. Thank you very much.

你真仁慈，十分感謝你對我們的幫助。

David is very friendly. He's always very nice to me.

大衛十分友善，他總是對我很好。

I'm afraid I can't help you. I'm sorry about that.

我想我幫不了你，對不起。

I'm sorry for / about not phoning you yesterday.

(或 I'm sorry I didn't phone you) 真抱歉，昨天沒致電給你。

I feel sorry for them. They are in a very difficult situation.

我同情他們。他們的處境很困難。

of / at / for（等等）+ - ing

介词（例如 of / at / for 等等）之後的動詞以 - ing 形式出現：

<table><tr><td>I&#x27;m not very good at Are you fed up with</td><td>telling doing</td><td>stories. 我不太善於講故事。
the same thing every day?
你對每天都做相同的事情，是否感到厭倦？
you yesterday. 真抱歉，昨天沒致電給你。
me. 謝謝你的幫忙。
a new car. 馬克正考慮買一輛新車。
goodbye. (= he didn&#x27;t say goodbye) 湯姆不辭而別。
the shopping, they went home. 購物完畢後，他們便回家去。</td></tr><tr><td>I&#x27;m sorry for Thank you for Mark is thinking of Tom left without After</td><td>not phoning helping buying saying doing</td><td>goodbye. (= he didn&#x27;t say goodbye) 湯姆不辭而別。
the shopping, they went home. 購物完畢後，他們便回家去。</td></tr></table>

He isn't afraid anything. 6 It was very nice Jane to let us stay in her apartment. 7 Life today is very different life 50 years ago. 8 Are you interested politics? 9 I feel sorry her, but I can't help her. 10 Chris was angry what happened. 11 These boxes are very heavy. They are full books. 12 I'm sorry getting angry you yesterday.

# listen to ..., look at ... 等等動词（verb）+介词（preposition）

ask (somebody) for ...

belong to ...

happen to ...

listen to ... speak / talk to somebody about something

thank somebody for ... think about ... 或 think of ...

wait for ... write to somebody

但 phone / call somebody (不用 to)

A man stopped me and asked me for money. 一名男子叫停我，並向我索取金錢。

Does this book belong to you? (= Is this your book?) 這本書屬於你的嗎？

I can't find my pen. What's happened to it? 我找不到我的筆，它放到哪裏去了？

Listen to this music. It's great. 聽聽這音樂，真優美。

Did you talk to Paul about the problem? 你曾跟保羅談論那個問題嗎？

(on the phone) Can I speak to Chris, please? (在電話中)請問我可否跟克里斯談話？

Thank you very much for your help. 十分感謝你的幫忙。

He never thinks about (或 of) other people. 他從來不為他人着想。

Mark is thinking of (或 about) buying a new computer. 馬克正考慮買一部新電腦。

Wait for me. I'm nearly ready. 等等我。我差不多準備好了。

I couldn't contact the company by phone. I had to write to them. 我不能用電話來聯絡那公司。我必須寫信給他們。

I'm going to phone my parents this evening.

(不是phone to my parents)我打算今晚致電給父母。

look at / look for / look after look at ...

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8e8d11489bd92ef92c9f67a440aefaf70fd9ae9aad284bb83aeb7728976ca71c.jpg)

look for (= try to find)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/6d517385efe8e3d457a526a58b3d40f7322e172db0bf53844bb6f9dd12b4f698.jpg)

look after (= take care of, keep safe)

He's looking at his watch. 他在看他的手錶。

Look at these flowers! They're beautiful. 看看這些花兒，多漂亮。

Why are you looking at me like that? 你為何這樣看着我？

你為何這樣看着我？

She's lost her key. She's looking for it.

她遺失了鑰匙，現正在找尋。

I'm looking for Sarah. Have you seen her? 我在找薩拉，你有見過她嗎？

我在找薩拉，你有見過她嗎？

When Emily is at work, a friend of hers looks after her children. 當埃米莉上班時，她的一位朋友照顧她的孩子。

當埃米莉上班時，她的一位朋友照顧她的孩子。

Don't lose this book. Look after it. (= Keep it safe.)

別遺失這本書，好好保存它。

# depend

depend on ...

A: Do you like eating in restaurants? 你喜歡在餐廳吃飯嗎？

B: Sometimes. It depends on the restaurant. (不是 it depends of) 有時喜歡，視乎是哪一間餐廳。

在it depends what / where / how 等等的句子中，depends 之後用 on 與否也可以：

A: Do you want to come out with us? 你想跟我們一來嗎？

B: It depends where you're going. 或 It depends on where you're going. 現在你們會到哪裏去。

# go in, fall off, run away 等等片語動词（phrasal verbs 1）

片語動词是動词（例如 go / look / be 等等）+ in / out / up / down 等等

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ea0b8be3f6c43f66e244c01315f03a6c4eab4b5590557666f5520333e2a64179.jpg)

I waited outside the shop. I didn't go in. 我在商店外等候，没有入内。 Sarah opened the door of the car and got in. (= into the car) 薩拉打開車門，然後上車。

on

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9c6facda26a8625bf8e022648ce8e7c8be185cac20266048b3a16465a0a804e8.jpg)

The bus came, and I got on. 巴士來到後，我上了車。

up

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d78fdb3c90b963f131fec20d6ace8248813de3ed5e2d945d7d8a58dbfc640684.jpg)

He stood up and left the room.

He stood up and left the room. 他站起來，然後離開房間。 I usually get up early. (= get out of bed) 我經常早起。 We looked up at the stars in the sky. 我們抬頭望天上的星星。

away或off

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/29aa2eaf9aea77e78d08118bdc83ce1c5d1bce6df56ede022817a99ef2178efb.jpg)

The thief ran away.（或...ran off） 贼人逃跑了。

Emma got into the car and drove away.（或...drove off）埃瑪上了車，然後駕車離去。

be / go away (= in / to another place)

Tim has gone away for a few days. 透姆已離開數天。

over

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/ff4f87b3709c21ed43f26381b2abddc2747e57f31cb071606e867c8aebcc158a.jpg)

The wall wasn't very high, so we climbed over. 那壁不算太高，所以我們便爬過去。

Turn over and look at the next page. 翻過這一頁，看下一頁。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/fd0d92c0b0c7c2f7f88642bffe4399d5b5cd937291dabe0a371cc5a5ad40cd11.jpg)

I went to the window and looked out. 我走近窗子，並望出窗外。

The car stopped and a woman got out. (= out of the car) 那辆汽車停下來，一名女子下車。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f501ef55844b48844591e8a5e533cbb842e63c0eec0216cd59f0b54884f874ba.jpg)

Be careful! Don't fall off. 小心！別跌下來。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/57a52b876ce165268d26adf05b56e5f2581dabb148f1075f72cff23f3266f29e.jpg)

The picture fell down.

那幅畫跌下來了。

Would you like to sit down?

你想坐下來嗎？

Lie down on the floor.

躺卧在地板上。

back

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0fccc8b0fbb062ef3fc16140cae2d4f6da3f375eaa0fd04524b6fcd3bf9cc593.jpg)

Go away and don't come back! 走吧，別回來！

We went out for dinner and then went back to our hotel. 我們外出吃晚餐，然後返回酒店。

be back

Tim is away. He'll be back on Monday. 透姆不在，他將於星期一回來。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/272b5ea6c493b00d1e4b448fa5418a1d30297605e9ad07be8b94e73019a75c5c.jpg)

Somebody shouted my name, so I looked round（或around). 有人叫我的名字，於是 我回頭看。

We went for a long walk. After an hour we turned round（或around）and went back. 我們走了很長一段路，一小時後我們轉身回去。

# put on your shoes put your shoes on 片語動词2（phrasal verbs 2）

A 有時片語動词（例如put on/take off等等）须配合資語使用。例如：

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/4e617a1ceb91c41d83372d09727f957b65b354c1f9519349f5226fbe2ad64d06.jpg)

動词 資語 take off your shoes

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/3826a1152718a532ed810027068948b8c69ef023383b29d5a3ed9395230950d8.jpg)

可以用：

put on your coat或 put your coat on

可以用：take off your shoes或 take your shoes off

但it／them（代词）通常用於on／off等等之前：

put it on（不是put on it)

It was cold, so I put on my coat. (或I put my coat on) 天氣很冷，所以我穿上大衣。 Here's your coat. Put it on. 這是你的大衣，穿上它吧。

take them off（不是take off them）

I'm going to take off my shoes. (或take my shoes off) 我要把我的鞋子脱下。 Your shoes are dirty. Take them off. 你的鞋子很，把它們脱下吧。

# 以下有更多的片語動词  $^+$  資語：

turn on / turn off (lights, machines, taps 等等)：

It was dark, so I turned on the light. （或I turned the light on）天黑了，所以我開燈。 I don't want to watch this programme.

You can turn it off. 我不想看這個節目，你可以關掉電視。 ON

還有switch on / switch off (lights, machines 等等)：

I switched on the light and switched off the television. 我開燈並關掉電視。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a763e4bdb5ba91cba7eff4ccde35745fb884256030118b9a6dd164ccc5f28de2.jpg)

pick up / put down:

Those are my keys on the floor. Can you pick them up for me? 地上的鑰匙是我的，你可否替我拾起來？

I stopped reading and put my book down. (或put down my book) 我停止閱讀，並把書放下。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/9e93fed3372140c9124cfbd573c887c0c290fb5699dcb1624a4e0a9b8b09e049.jpg)

bring back / take back / give back / put back:

You can take my umbrella, but please bring it back. 你可拿我的雨傘去，但請你把它帶回來。

I took my new sweater back to the shop. It was too small for me. 我把新買的毛衣拿回商店去。 那毛衣太小了，不合身。

I've got Rachel's keys. I have to give them back to her.

我拿了雷切爾的鑰匙，我必須歸還給她。

I read the letter and then put it back in the envelope. 我看過那封信後把它放回信封内。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/0d93b73f7d1224ae604891641665cd28e3dae87b5b2c5dccbc2faa5705911bb3.jpg)
