# 时錄1主動語態（active）和（passive）

# 1.1 現在式和過去式

#

<table><tr><td rowspan="2">簡單
現在式</td><td>主動語態</td><td>被動語態</td></tr><tr><td>□ We make butter from milk.
我們把牛奶提煉成牛油。
□ Somebody cleans these rooms every day.
每天都有人打掃這些房間。
□ People never invite me to parties.
人們從不邀請我參加派對。
□ How do they make butter?
他們是怎樣製造牛油的?</td><td>□ Butter is made from milk.
牛油從牛奶提煉而成。
□ These rooms are cleaned every day.
這些房間每天都有人打掃。
□ I am never invited to parties.
我從不獲邀請參加派對。
□ How is butter made?
牛油是怎樣製成的?</td></tr><tr><td>簡單
過去式</td><td>□ Somebody stole my car last week.
上星期有人偷了我的車。
□ Somebody stole my keys yesterday.
昨天有人偷了我的鑰匙。
□ They didn&#x27;t invite me to the party.
他們沒有邀請我參加派對。
□ When did they build these houses?
他們何時建成這些房屋?</td><td>□ My car was stolen last week.
上星期我的車被偷了。
□ My keys were stolen yesterday.
昨天我的鑰匙被偷了。
□ I wasn&#x27;t invited to the party.
我沒有獲邀請參加派對。
□ When were these houses built?
這些房屋是何時被建成的?</td></tr><tr><td>現在
進行式</td><td>□ They are building a new airport at the moment.
(= 現在工程尚未完成)他們正在興建一個新機場。
□ They are building some new houses near the river.
他們正在河流附近建造一些新房屋。</td><td>□ A new airport is being built at the moment.
一個新機場正在興建中。
□ Some new houses are being built near the river.
河流附近有一些新房屋正在興建中。</td></tr><tr><td>過去
進行式</td><td>□ When I was here a few years ago, they were building a new airport. (= 當時工程還未完成)當我數年前在這裏時,他們正在興建一個新機場。</td><td>□ When I was here a few years ago, a new airport was being built.
當我數年前在這裏時,一個新機場正在興建中。</td></tr><tr><td>現在
完成式</td><td>□ Look! They have painted the door.
看!他們已把門塗上油漆。
□ These shirts are clean. Somebody has washed them. 這些襯衣是乾淨的,有人已把它們洗乾淨。
□ Somebody has stolen my car. 有人偷了我的車。</td><td>□ Look! The door has been painted.
看!那扇門已被塗上油漆。
□ These shirts are clean. They have been washed.
這些襯衣是乾淨的,它們已被洗乾淨了。
□ My car has been stolen. 我的車給人偷了。</td></tr><tr><td>過去
完成式</td><td>□ Tina said that somebody had stolen her car. 蒂娜說有人偷了她的車。</td><td>□ Tina said that her car had been stolen.
蒂娜說她的車給人偷了。</td></tr></table>

# 1.2 will/can/must/have to 等等

# 主動語態

#

<table><tr><td>□ Somebody will clean the office tomorrow.
明天有人會打掃辦公室。
□ Somebody must clean the office. 必須有人打掃辦公室。
□ I think they&#x27;ll invite you to the party.
我想他們將邀請你參加派對。
□ They can&#x27;t repair my watch. 他們不能修理我的手錶。
□ You should wash this sweater by hand.
你應該用手洗這件毛衣。
□ They are going to build a new airport.
他們將要興建一個新機場。
□ Somebody has to wash these clothes.
有人必須洗乾淨這些衣服。
□ They had to take the injured man to hospital.
他們必須送那受傷的男子到醫院去。</td><td>□ The office will be cleaned tomorrow.
明天辦公室會有人打掃。
□ The office must be cleaned. 辦公室必須有人打掃。
□ I think you&#x27;ll be invited to the party.
我想你將獲邀請參加派對。
□ My watch can&#x27;t be repaired. 我的手錶不能修理了。
□ This sweater should be washed by hand.
這件毛衣應該用手洗。
□ A new airport is going to be built.
一個新機場將要被興建。
□ These clothes have to be washed.
這些衣服須給洗乾淨。
□ The injured man had to be taken to hospital.
那受傷的男子必須被送往醫院去。</td></tr></table>

# 附錄4

# 縮約式(short forms)(例如he's / I'd / don't 等等)

4.1在英语會話中，我們通常將Iam’發音成一個單字，在書寫時可以用縮約式  $(\mathbf{I}^{\mathbf{m}})$  表達：

Iam → I'm it is → it's they have → they've 等等

I'm feeling tired this morning. 今早我感到疲倦。  'Do you like this jacket?' 'Yes, it's nice.' 「你喜歡這件夾克嗎？」「我喜歡，它十分好看。」  'Where are your friends?' 'They've gone home.' 「你的朋友在哪裏？」「他們已回家去了。」

我們用,(省略號)來表達縮約式：

I  $\mathrm{Xm}\rightarrow \mathrm{I}^{\mathrm{?}}\mathrm{m}$  he  $\mathrm{Xs}\rightarrow \mathrm{he}^{\mathrm{s}}$  you have → you've she will → she'll

4.2 以下的形式跟I/he/she等等一起運用：

<table><tr><td>am → &#x27;m</td><td>I&#x27;m</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>is → &#x27;s</td><td></td><td>he&#x27;s</td><td>she&#x27;s</td><td>it&#x27;s</td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>are → &#x27;re</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td>we&#x27;re</td><td>you&#x27;re</td><td>they&#x27;re</td></tr><tr><td>have → &#x27;ve</td><td>I&#x27;ve</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td>we&#x27;ve</td><td>you&#x27;ve</td><td>they&#x27;ve</td></tr><tr><td>has → &#x27;s</td><td></td><td>he&#x27;s</td><td>she&#x27;s</td><td>it&#x27;s</td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><td>had → &#x27;d</td><td>I&#x27;d</td><td>he&#x27;d</td><td>she&#x27;d</td><td></td><td>we&#x27;d</td><td>you&#x27;d</td><td>they&#x27;d</td></tr><tr><td>will → &#x27;ll</td><td>I&#x27;ll</td><td>he&#x27;ll</td><td>she&#x27;ll</td><td></td><td>we&#x27;ll</td><td>you&#x27;ll</td><td>they&#x27;ll</td></tr><tr><td>would → &#x27;d</td><td>I&#x27;d</td><td>he&#x27;d</td><td>she&#x27;d</td><td></td><td>we&#x27;d</td><td>you&#x27;d</td><td>they&#x27;d</td></tr></table>

I've got some new shoes. 我買了一些新鞋子。

We'll probably go out this evening. 今晚我們可能會外出。

It's 10 o'clock. You're late again. 現在是十時正，你又遲到了。

$\mathbf{\dot{s}} = \mathbf{i}\mathbf{s}$  或has:

She's going out this evening. (she's going = she is going) 她今晚會外出。

She's gone out. (she's gone = she has gone) 她已經外出了。

$\mathbf{\dot{d}} =$  would或had:

A: What would you like to eat? 你想吃甚麼？

B: I'd like a salad, please. (I'd like = I would like) 我想吃沙律，謝謝。

I told the police that I'd lost my passport. (I'd lost = I had lost) 我告訴警察我遺失了護照。

注意 別在句末用'm/s/d等等：換索  $\Rightarrow$  單元40

'Are you tired?' 'Yes, I am'（不是Yes, I'm.）「你感到疲倦嗎？」「是的，我感到疲倦。」

She isn't tired, but he is.（不是he's）她不感到疲倦，但他感到疲倦。

4.3縮約式跟I/you/he/she等等一起運用，但其實縮約式（尤其是s）也可跟其他词語一起運用：

Who's your favourite singer? (= who is) 你最喜愛的歌手是誰？

What's the time? (= what is) 現在是甚麼時間？

There's a big tree in the garden. (= there is) 花園裏有一棵大樹。

My sister's working in London. (= my sister is working) 我的姊姊在倫敦工作。

Paul's gone out. (= Paul has gone out) 保羅已經外出了。

What colour's your car? (= What colour is your car?) 你的車是甚麼顏色？

4.4 否定式的縮約式: 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元 43

isn't (= is not) don't (= do not) can't (= cannot) aren't (= are not) doesn't (= does not) couldn't (= could not) wasn't (= was not) didn't (= did not) won't (= will not) weren't (= were not) wouldn't (= would not) hasn't (= has not) shouldn't (= should not) haven't (= have not) mustn't (= must not) hadn't (= had not)

We went to her house, but she wasn't at home. 我們到她的房子去, 但她不在家。

'Where's David?' 'I don't know. I haven't seen him.'

「大衛在哪事？」「我不知道,我沒有見過他。」

You work all the time. You shouldn't work so hard.

你不停地工作,你不愿這麼辛勞地工作的。

I won't be here tomorrow. (= I will not) 明天我將不在這裏。

4.5 's(省略號+s)

s可以表達不同的意思:

(1)  $\mathbf{\dot{s}} = \mathbf{i}\mathbf{s}$  或has( 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  本附錄的4.2部分)

(2) let's = let us ( 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元 35、53)

It's a lovely day. Let's go out. (= Let us go out.) 天氣很好, 我們外出吧。

(3) Kate's camera = her camera

my brother's car = his car

the manager's office = his / her office 等等

( 檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元 64)

試比較 下列三個 's 的意思有何分別:

Kate's camera was very expensive. (Kate's camera = her camera) 凱特的照相機十分昂貴。

Kate's a very good photographer. (Kate's = Kate is) 凱特是一個很優秀的攝影師。

Kate's got a new camera. (Kate's got = Kate has got) 凱特買了一部新的照相機。

# 附錄5 拼寫 (spelling)

5.1 詞語  $^+$  - s和- es（例如birds／watches等等）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/17b696bb4f98b923b4885aea354dd1c79fdb34898490a90c5e1ec1e04f4fea20.jpg)

但

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/dc0395fdb7dcb56b7c58e4fb0f0bdcf8b14c257c5438b04dfc73b4541a4f2d83.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/36aa30147c783e40eae40dde5ed736f1f0d6da84be887bb8b948ff0f86a39743.jpg)

5.2 以- y結尾的詞語（例如baby  $\rightarrow$  babies／study  $\rightarrow$  studied等等）

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/d5454ef4ad0ffc1eba7877132e8ce6e1d99c22497f280ab59155417e8cf4b411.jpg)

- y→-ied（检索  $\Rightarrow$  單元11）study  $\rightarrow$  studied（不是studied）try  $\rightarrow$  tried marry  $\rightarrow$  married copy  $\rightarrow$  copied

- y→-ier/-iest（检索  $\Rightarrow$  單元87和90）easy  $\rightarrow$  easier/easiest（不是easyer/easiest）happy  $\rightarrow$  happier/happiest lucky  $\rightarrow$  luckier/luckiestheavy  $\rightarrow$  heavier/heaviest funny  $\rightarrow$  funnier/funniest

- y→-ily（检索  $\Rightarrow$  單元86）easy  $\rightarrow$  easily（不是easly）happy  $\rightarrow$  happily heavy  $\rightarrow$  heavily lucky  $\rightarrow$  luckily

注意 如果語是以- ay/- ey/- oy/- uy作為結尾的話，y便不用改為i：holiday  $\rightarrow$  holidays（不是holidaies）enjoy  $\rightarrow$  enjoys/enjoyed stay  $\rightarrow$  stays/stayed buy  $\rightarrow$  buys key  $\rightarrow$  keys

但 say  $\rightarrow$  said pay  $\rightarrow$  paid （不规则動词）

5.3 - ing

以- e(例如make／write／drive等等)為结尾的動词  $\rightarrow$  - eing:

make  $\rightarrow$  making write  $\rightarrow$  writing come  $\rightarrow$  coming dance  $\rightarrow$  dancing

以- ie為结尾的動词  $\rightarrow$  - ying:lie  $\rightarrow$  lying die  $\rightarrow$  dying tie  $\rightarrow$  tying

5.4 stop  $\rightarrow$  stopped, big  $\rightarrow$  bigger等等

元音和辅音:

元音字母:a e i o u

辅音字母:b c d f g k l m n p r s t w y

有時一個語會以一個元音 + 一個辅音结尾,例如stop、big、get。

在加上- ing/- ed/- er/- est之前,語本身结尾的辅音(例如- p/- g/- t等等)便要重複(例如- pp- /- gg- /- tt- 等等)。例如:

V+C V= vowel(元音) stop ST O P p → pp stopping stopped run R U N n → nn running get G E T t → tt getting swim SW I M m → mm swimming big B I G g → gg bigger biggest hot H O T t → tt hotter hottest thin TH I N n → nn thinner thinnest

$\mathrm{V} =$  vowel(元音)  $\mathrm{C} =$  consonant(辅音)

# 注意

以下的情况除外:

(1)如語以兩個辅音字母结尾  $(\mathrm{C} + \mathrm{C})$  :

C+C help HE L P helping helped work WO R K working worked fast FA S T faster fastest

(2)如語以兩個元音字母 + 一個辅音字母结尾  $(\mathrm{V} + \mathrm{V} + \mathrm{C})$  :

$\mathrm{V} + \mathrm{V} + \mathrm{C}$  need N E E D needing needed wait W A I T waiting waited cheap CH E A P cheaper cheapest

(3)如在長的語(兩個音節或以上)中,最後部分不是重音:

重音happen HAP- pen → happening / happened (不是happened)visit VIS- it → visiting / visitedremember re- MEM- ber → remembering / remembered但prefer pre- FER (重音在最後) → preferring / preferredbegin be- GIN (重音在最後) → beginning

(4)如語以- y 或 - w 结尾(在語的結尾部分,y 和 w 都不是辅音)。

enjoy  $\rightarrow$  enjoying / enjoyed snow  $\rightarrow$  snowing / snowed few  $\rightarrow$  fewer / fewest

# 附錄6 片語動詞（phrasal verbs）（例如take off / give up 等等）

以下是一些重要的片語動詞：檢索  $\Rightarrow$  單元114

# on

carry on  $=$  總續

Don't stop working. Carry on. (= continue working) 別停下工作，繼續。

A: Excuse me, where is the station? 對不起，請問車站在哪裹？

B: Carry on along this road and turn right at the lights. (= Continue along ...) 沿這條路續行，然後在交通燈位轉右。

還有go on /walk on /drive on 等等  $=$  總續前進：

Don't stop here. Drive on. 別在這裹停下來，續駕。

come on  $=$  提快一點：

Come on! Everybody is waiting for you. 快一點！大家都在等你。 get on  $=$  進展、應付（例如in a job, at school, in an exam等等）：

How was your exam? How did you get on?

$\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  how did you do?) 你的考試怎樣？考得好？

hold on  $=$  等一等

Can you hold on a minute? (= can you wait?) 你可否稍等？

# off

take off  $=$  （指飛機）起飛：

The plane took off 20 minutes late, but arrived on time. 那架飛機選了20分鐘起飛，但準時降落。

go off  $=$  （指炸彈）爆炸或（指警報器、鬧鐘等）響起：

A bomb went off and caused a lot of damage.

炸彈爆炸並造成大量破壞。

A car alarm goes off if somebody tries to break into the car. 如果有人嘗試撬開汽車，汽車警報器就會響起。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7b76d9281abce7a564a3cbbcaf7a09c68a5a064842326ac3e3f253fc1fe7eb52.jpg)

# TAKE OFF

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7352617df2c818dbafd0c53d64e1256194ae206f957090bf93d774b1218a0915.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/534af17f730e294b62c945c48ecea117c747a3ff0b3b0d7b910f1dd138e5b2ea.jpg)

GO OFF

# up

give up  $=$  放棄：

I know it's difficult, but don't give up.

$\mathbf{\Psi} = \mathbf{\Psi}$  don't stop trying) 我知道這是很困難的，但別放棄。

grow up  $=$  長大成人：

What does your son want to do when he grows up?

你的兒子長大後想做甚麼？

hurry up  $=$  提快：

Hurry up! We haven't got much time. 趙快一點！我們時間不多了。

speak up  $=$  說話大聲一點：

I can't hear you. Can you speak up, please? 我聽不到你說話，你可否說大聲一點？

wake up  $=$  醒來：

I often wake up in the middle of the night. 我經常在半夜醒來。

wash up  $=$  （在餐後）清洗碗碟等等：

Do you want me to wash up? (或...to do the washing- up?) 你想我洗碗嗎？

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/21522270ec7713d9719ec29533662ea86352e55bbe5cfac87776097580604202.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/209d75f8680b042eec38800bd171f66dbbab40c89f0d642f61e02ee5bcec9232.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/cbac4f9ae72179407677746ffd99c1f2c28094a4963b70712656e29b5ddbdb2a.jpg)

down slow down  $=$  减速：

You're driving too fast. Slow down! 你駕駛得太快了，減速吧。

break down  $=$  （指汽車/機器等等）壞掉、發生故障

Sue was very late because her car broke down. 蘇因為車子故障，所以遲了很多。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f593a7819b6de1a1159d0c1c7e9444816cc16353e830f12018246b0fb8c30a1b.jpg)

# over

fall over  $=$  跌倒：

I fell over because my shoes were too big for me. 我因為鞋子太大而跌倒。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c097d8f77596ed2457dcce7318d8476f70eba7e23cbbc294a4fe707b708c19bd.jpg)

# 附錄7 片語動詞(phrasal verbs) + 實語(object) (例如put out a fire / give up your job等等)

以下是一些重要的片語動詞+資語： 檢索 →單元115

in/out fill in/full out a form = 填寫（一份表格）：

Can you fill in this form, please? 或Can you fill out this form, please?請問你可否填寫這份表格？

out put out a fire, a cigarette 等等 = 熄滅（一場火／一根煙）：

The fire brigade arrived and put the fire out.

消防隊到達並把火撲滅。

cross out a mistake, a word 等等 = 剛去（錯誤／字等等）：

If you make a mistake, cross it out. 如果你做錯了，把它刪掉。

on try on clothes = 試穿衣服，看看是否合身：

(in a shop) This is a nice jacket. Shall I try it on? （在商店裏）這件夾克很好看，我試穿上好嗎？

up

give up something = 停止做某事／放棄：

Sue gave up her job when her baby was born. (= she stopped working)

蘇生了孩子之後就放棄工作。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/7d809797af074f0b030419e33f7b48c8f8b5e5dadd2e87515bb7164e5095742c.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/db564337944eb383a1077dff8d5a814dff224cb55505115c45626551796d7438.jpg)

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/f7bbb6828534fb223e14dcb4a864927a6fa7074733d893235c97e27f4c2e6f01.jpg)

CROSS OUT

'Are you still learning Italian?' 'No, I gave it up.' 「你仍在學意大利語嗎？」「不，我放棄了。」look up a word in a dictionary 等等 = 翻查（詞典等等）

I didn't know the meaning of the word, so I looked it up in a dictionary. 我不懂這個詞的意思，所以便查詞典。

turn up the TV, radio, music, heating 等等 = 調高音量（例如電視、收音機、音樂等等）

Can you turn the radio up? I can't hear it. 你可否把收音機的音量調高？我聽不到。

wake up somebody who is sleeping = 噪醒（睡着的人）

I have to get up early tomorrow. Can you wake me up at 6:30?

我明天要很早起麻。你可以在6時30分叫醒我嗎？

down knock down a building = 拆毀（一座建築物）：

They are going to knock down the school and build a new one. 他們將拆毀那間學校，然後興建一間新的。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/c90471facd26ded38a8f1caab9da0447dd32bf856f9fa9e8743f5796ccd80f36.jpg)

turn down the TV, radio, music, heating etc. = 調低音量

（例如電視、收音機、音樂等等）：

The music is too loud. Can you turn it down?

那音樂太大聲了，你可否把音量調低一點？

over

knock over a cup, a glass, a person 等等 = 打翻或撞倒（例如杯子／玻璃杯／人等等）：

Be careful. Don't knock your cup over. 小心！別打翻你的杯子。

There was an accident at the end of the road.

A man was knocked over by a car.

（或A man was knocked down by a car.）

那道路的盡頭發生了意外，一名男子給一輛汽車撞倒了。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/a6281b78368696ca1bafa24daa64ff67b19b57d0913dd0fc7b5eb8a058151700.jpg)

KNOCK OVER OR KNOCK DOWN

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/46db421650e2122adec0fbeea787eaa4d0c4a7030c45f2a78e15e30943ba2546.jpg)

away

throw away rubbish = 扔掉（垃圾，你不想要的东西）：

These apples are bad. Shall I throw them away?

這些蘋果變壞了，我把它們扔掉好嗎？

Don't throw away that picture. I want it. 別扔掉那幅畫，我想要它。

put something away = 把物件收拾、儲存：

After they finished playing, the children put their toys away. 孩子們玩耍過後，把玩具收拾好。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8f0440210c4b31ebaee8036bd00812b7b0a5c2de6b1f490d444519446977bb12.jpg)

back

pay somebody back = 價還、歸還：

Thank you for lending me the money. I'll pay you back next week.

多謝你借錢給我，我下星期會還給你的。

round/

around

show somebody round / around = 帶某人參觀某地方：

We visited a factory last week. The manager showed us round. 我們在星期參觀了一間工廠。那裏的經理帶我們四處參觀。

# 學習锦囊

若你不確定需要研習哪個單元,請使用此學習錦囊。

你需要決定哪個選擇(A、B、C等等)是正確的,有些問題會多於一個正確答案。

若你不知道(或不確定)哪個答案是正確的,請研習右欄建議的單元,你會在該單元找到正確的句子。這學習錦囊的答案在第312頁。

![](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/extract/5e1427f6-5130-4a16-ab8e-bb25c5b282ea/8c6c676a7fd9c75827bfebbf744196100a5eab9bfad4877635ca1097b838a738.jpg)

# Essential Grammar in Use

With answers and CD- ROM THIRDEDITION

劍橋英語語法精解 (第三版)

附答案及自學光碟

本書專為自學基礎英語語法的讀者而編寫。第三版以彩色印刷,附全新插圖,生動活潑,趣味盎然。運用清晰例子及簡易練習,令學習更易掌握。除涵蓋所有基礎英語語法外,本書有以下特色:

- 每單元兩頁,左頁為精闢解釋,右頁為練習,即時鞏固所學。

- 書末提供全新的「學習錦囊」,有助學生自我評估學習進度。

- 書末的「附加練習」額外提供全新練習,進一步操練所學。

新增「自學光碟」包含數以百計互動題目,為每章提供更多練習,特色包括:

多元化的遊戲及練習增添學習趣味。

- 可按課題,從包含逾600題的語法題庫中選取題目,

製作試卷,測試學習進度。

- 聆聽錄音提供額外聆聽及發音練習。

- 內置詞典即時提供生字的定義。

- 互動說話練習於每部分末提供額外練習。
